OmniRoam: World Wandering via Long-Horizon Panoramic Video Generation
Authors: Yuheng Liu, Xin Lin, Xinke Li, Baihan Yang, Chen Wang, Kalyan Sunkavalli, Yannick Hold-Geoffroy, Hao Tan, Kai Zhang, Xiaohui Xie, Zifan Shi, Yiwei Hu
First: 2026-03-31T17:59:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:59:33+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modeling scenes using video generation models has garnered growing research interest in recent years. However, most existing approaches rely on perspective video models that synthesize only limited observations of a scene, leading to issues of completeness and global consistency. We propose OmniRoam, a controllable panoramic video generation framework that exploits the rich per-frame scene coverage and inherent long-term spatial and temporal consistency of panoramic representation, enabling long-horizon scene wandering. Our framework begins with a preview stage, where a trajectory-controlled video generation model creates a quick overview of the scene from a given input image or video. Then, in the refine stage, this video is temporally extended and spatially upsampled to produce long-range, high-resolution videos, thus enabling high-fidelity world wandering. To train our model, we introduce two panoramic video datasets that incorporate both synthetic and real-world captured videos. Experiments show that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of visual quality, controllability, and long-term scene consistency, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We further showcase several extensions of this framework, including real-time video generation and 3D reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniRoam：通过长时程全景视频生成实现全球漫游
近年来，使用视频生成模型建模场景的研究兴趣日益增长。然而，大多数现有方法依赖于视角视频模型，仅合成场景的有限观测，导致场景完整性和全局一致性问题。我们提出OmniRoam，一种可控的全景视频生成框架，利用全景表示的每帧丰富场景覆盖和固有的长期空间与时间一致性，实现长时程场景漫游。该框架首先通过预览阶段，由轨迹控制的视频生成模型从给定输入图像或视频快速生成场景概览；随后在精炼阶段，将该视频在时间上扩展、空间上上采样，生成长距离、高分辨率视频，从而实现高保真度的世界漫游。为训练模型，我们引入两个包含合成与真实世界捕获视频的全景视频数据集。实验表明，我们的框架在视觉质量、可控性及长期场景一致性方面，无论定性还是定量均优于现有最先进方法。我们进一步展示了该框架的多种扩展，包括实时视频生成和3D重建。代码见https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of perspective video models that produce incomplete and inconsistent scene views, the authors propose OmniRoam, a panoramic video generation framework enabling long-horizon, high-fidelity world wandering. The method consists of two stages: a preview stage that generates a trajectory-controlled overview from an input image or video, followed by a refine stage that temporally extends and spatially upsamples the video to produce long-range, high-resolution outputs. Trained on two new panoramic video datasets combining synthetic and real-world footage, OmniRoam achieves superior visual quality, controllability, and long-term consistency compared to existing methods, as demonstrated both qualitatively and quantitatively, with additional capabilities including real-time generation and 3D reconstruction.
为解决视角视频模型在场景覆盖完整性和长期一致性方面的不足，作者提出OmniRoam全景视频生成框架。该框架首先通过轨迹控制的视频生成器从输入图像或视频生成预览，随后通过时序扩展和空间上采样生成高分辨率、长距离视频。该模型在包含合成与真实数据的两个新全景视频数据集上训练，实验表明其在视觉质量、可控性与长期一致性方面显著优于现有方法，且支持实时生成与3D重建等扩展功能。
Video Models Reason Early: Exploiting Plan Commitment for Maze Solving
Authors: Kaleb Newman, Tyler Zhu, Olga Russakovsky
First: 2026-03-31T17:58:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:58:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video diffusion models exhibit emergent reasoning capabilities like solving mazes and puzzles, yet little is understood about how they reason during generation. We take a first step towards understanding this and study the internal planning dynamics of video models using 2D maze solving as a controlled testbed. Our investigations reveal two findings. Our first finding is early plan commitment: video diffusion models commit to a high-level motion plan within the first few denoising steps, after which further denoising alters visual details but not the underlying trajectory. Our second finding is that path length, not obstacle density, is the dominant predictor of maze difficulty, with a sharp failure threshold at 12 steps. This means video models can only reason over long mazes by chaining together multiple sequential generations. To demonstrate the practical benefits of our findings, we introduce Chaining with Early Planning, or ChEaP, which only spends compute on seeds with promising early plans and chains them together to tackle complex mazes. This improves accuracy from 7% to 67% on long-horizon mazes and by 2.5x overall on hard tasks in Frozen Lake and VR-Bench across Wan2.2-14B and HunyuanVideo-1.5. Our analysis reveals that current video models possess deeper reasoning capabilities than previously recognized, which can be elicited more reliably with better inference-time scaling.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how video diffusion models reason during generation, this study investigates their internal planning dynamics using 2D maze solving as a controlled testbed. The authors find that these models commit to a high-level motion plan early in denoising, after which visual details change but not the trajectory, and that maze difficulty is primarily determined by path length rather than obstacle density, with a sharp failure threshold at 12 steps. To leverage these insights, they propose Chaining with Early Planning (ChEaP), which prioritizes compute for seeds with promising early plans and chains them to solve complex mazes, improving accuracy from 7% to 67% on long-horizon mazes and boosting performance by 2.5x on hard tasks across multiple video models.
为理解视频扩散模型在生成过程中的推理机制，本研究以二维迷宫求解作为可控测试平台，探究其内部规划动态。研究发现，模型在去噪初期即承诺高阶运动计划，后续去噪仅改变视觉细节而不改变轨迹；迷宫难度主要由路径长度决定，而非障碍密度，12步为关键失败阈值。基于此，提出ChEaP（早期规划链式生成）方法，仅对早期规划有潜力的种子分配计算资源并串联生成，使长时程迷宫准确率从7%提升至67%，在多个视频模型上硬任务整体性能提升2.5倍，揭示当前模型具备更深层推理能力，可通过优化推理时扩展更可靠地激发。
HapCompass: A Rotational Haptic Device for Contact-Rich Robotic Teleoperation
Authors: Xiangshan Tan, Jingtian Ji, Tianchong Jiang, Pedro Lopes, Matthew R. Walter
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-03-31T17:57:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:57:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA), 2026. 8 pages, 5 figures. Project page: https://ripl.github.io/HapCompass/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The contact-rich nature of manipulation makes it a significant challenge for robotic teleoperation. While haptic feedback is critical for contact-rich tasks, providing intuitive directional cues within wearable teleoperation interfaces remains a bottleneck. Existing solutions, such as non-directional vibrations from handheld controllers, provide limited information, while vibrotactile arrays are prone to perceptual interference. To address these limitations, we propose HapCompass, a novel, low-cost wearable haptic device that renders 2D directional cues by mechanically rotating a single linear resonant actuator (LRA). We evaluated HapCompass's ability to convey directional cues to human operators and showed that it increased the success rate, decreased the completion time and the maximum contact force for teleoperated manipulation tasks when compared to vision-only and non-directional feedback baselines. Furthermore, we conducted a preliminary imitation-learning evaluation, suggesting that the directional feedback provided by HapCompass enhances the quality of demonstration data and, in turn, the trained policy. We release the design of the HapCompass device along with the code that implements our teleoperation interface: https://ripl.github.io/HapCompass/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HapCompass：一种用于接触密集型机器人遥操作的旋转触觉设备
操作中接触密集的特性使得机器人遥操作面临重大挑战。虽然触觉反馈对接触密集型任务至关重要，但在可穿戴遥操作界面中提供直观的方向提示仍存在瓶颈。现有方案如手持控制器的非方向性振动提供信息有限，而振动触觉阵列易受感知干扰。为解决这些问题，我们提出HapCompass，一种新型低成本可穿戴触觉设备，通过机械旋转单个线性共振执行器（LRA）呈现二维方向提示。我们评估了HapCompass向人类操作者传达方向提示的能力，结果表明，与仅视觉和非方向性反馈基线相比，其可提高任务成功率、缩短完成时间并降低最大接触力。此外，我们进行了初步的模仿学习评估，表明HapCompass提供的方向性反馈可提升演示数据质量，进而提升训练策略效果。我们公开了HapCompass设备的设计及实现遥操作界面的代码：https://ripl.github.io/HapCompass/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of conveying directional haptic feedback in contact-rich robotic teleoperation, the authors designed HapCompass, a low-cost wearable device that generates 2D directional cues by rotating a single linear resonant actuator. Experimental results demonstrated that HapCompass significantly improved task success rates, reduced completion time, and lowered maximum contact force compared to vision-only and non-directional vibration baselines. Additionally, preliminary imitation learning tests indicated that HapCompass-enhanced demonstration data led to better-trained robotic policies.
为解决接触密集型机器人遥操作中方向性触觉反馈不足的问题，作者设计了HapCompass，一种通过旋转单个线性谐振执行器来生成二维方向触觉的低成本可穿戴设备。实验表明，与仅视觉或非方向性振动基线相比，HapCompass显著提升了任务成功率、缩短了完成时间并降低了最大接触力。此外，初步的模仿学习结果显示，HapCompass提供的方向性反馈可提升示范数据质量，从而优化训练策略。
Efficient Universal Perception Encoder
Authors: Chenchen Zhu, Saksham Suri, Cijo Jose, Maxime Oquab, Marc Szafraniec, Wei Wen, Yunyang Xiong, Patrick Labatut, Piotr Bojanowski, Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi, Vikas Chandra
First: 2026-03-23T17:50:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:57:12+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/facebookresearch/EUPE; Model: https://huggingface.co/collections/facebook/eupe
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Running AI models on smart edge devices can unlock versatile user experiences, but presents challenges due to limited compute and the need to handle multiple tasks simultaneously. This requires a vision encoder with small size but powerful and versatile representations. We present our method, Efficient Universal Perception Encoder (EUPE), which offers both inference efficiency and universally good representations for diverse downstream tasks. We achieve this by distilling from multiple domain-expert foundation vision encoders. Unlike previous agglomerative methods that directly scale down from multiple teachers to an efficient encoder, we demonstrate the importance of first scaling up to a large proxy teacher and then scaling down from this single teacher. Experiments show that EUPE achieves on-par or better performance than individual domain experts of the same size on diverse task domains and also outperforms previous agglomerative encoders. We release the full family of EUPE models and the code to foster future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高效的通用感知编码器
在智能边缘设备上运行AI模型可解锁多样化的用户体验，但受限于计算能力及需同时处理多项任务，这要求一种体积小但表现力强且通用的视觉编码器。我们提出高效通用感知编码器（EUPE），兼顾推理效率与对各类下游任务的卓越表现。我们通过从多个领域专家基础视觉编码器中蒸馏实现该目标。与以往直接从多个教师模型缩放至高效编码器的聚合方法不同，我们证明了先通过单一教师模型进行放大，再进行缩小的必要性。实验表明，EUPE在各类任务领域中性能与同等规模的领域专家相当或更优，且超越了以往的聚合编码器。我们开源了EUPE全系列模型及代码，以促进未来研究。
Summary / 总结
To enable efficient AI deployment on edge devices with limited compute while supporting diverse tasks, the authors propose Efficient Universal Perception Encoder (EUPE), a vision encoder distilled from multiple domain-expert foundation models. Unlike prior agglomerative methods that directly scale down from multiple teachers, EUPE first scales up to a large proxy teacher before distilling to a compact encoder, preserving representation quality. Experimental results show EUPE matches or exceeds the performance of individual domain experts of the same size across varied tasks and outperforms previous agglomerative encoders, demonstrating its versatility and efficiency.
为在计算资源受限的边缘设备上高效部署AI并支持多任务，作者提出高效通用感知编码器（EUPE），通过多领域专家模型蒸馏获得。与直接聚合多个教师模型不同，EUPE先将多个专家模型融合为一个大型代理教师，再蒸馏至轻量高效模型。实验表明，EUPE在多种任务上性能与同等规模的单个专家持平或更优，且优于以往聚合式编码器，验证了两阶段蒸馏策略的有效性。
Automatic Identification of Parallelizable Loops Using Transformer-Based Source Code Representations
Authors: Izavan dos S. Correia, Henrique C. T. Santos, Tiago A. E. Ferreira
First: 2026-03-31T17:54:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:54:27+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic parallelization remains a challenging problem in software engineering, particularly in identifying code regions where loops can be safely executed in parallel on modern multi-core architectures. Traditional static analysis techniques, such as dependence analysis and polyhedral models, often struggle with irregular or dynamically structured code. In this work, we propose a Transformer-based approach to classify the parallelization potential of source code, focusing on distinguishing independent (parallelizable) loops from undefined ones. We adopt DistilBERT to process source code sequences using subword tokenization, enabling the model to capture contextual syntactic and semantic patterns without handcrafted features. The approach is evaluated on a balanced dataset combining synthetically generated loops and manually annotated real-world code, using 10-fold cross-validation and multiple performance metrics. Results show consistently high performance, with mean accuracy above 99\% and low false positive rates, demonstrating robustness and reliability. Compared to prior token-based methods, the proposed approach simplifies preprocessing while improving generalization and maintaining computational efficiency. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight Transformer models for practical identification of parallelization opportunities at the loop level.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer源代码表示的可并行循环自动识别
自动并行化仍是软件工程中的挑战性问题，特别是在识别现代多核架构上可安全并行执行的循环代码区域。传统静态分析技术（如依赖分析和多面体模型）常难以处理不规则或动态结构的代码。本文提出一种基于Transformer的方法，用于分类源代码的并行化潜力，重点区分独立（可并行化）循环与未定义循环。我们采用DistilBERT对源代码序列进行子词分词处理，使模型无需人工特征即可捕捉上下文语法和语义模式。该方法在合成生成循环与人工标注真实代码混合的平衡数据集上评估，采用10折交叉验证和多种性能指标。结果表明性能稳定，平均准确率超过99%，误报率低，展现出鲁棒性和可靠性。与先前基于词元的方法相比，该方法简化了预处理，同时提升了泛化能力并保持计算效率。这些发现凸显了轻量级Transformer模型在循环级并行化机会实际识别中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of automatically identifying parallelizable loops in modern multi-core systems, this work proposes a Transformer-based method using DistilBERT to classify loop parallelization potential by analyzing source code sequences with subword tokenization. The model captures syntactic and semantic context without handcrafted features, evaluated on a balanced dataset of synthetic and real-world loops via 10-fold cross-validation. Results show mean accuracy above 99% and low false positives, outperforming prior token-based methods in generalization and efficiency while maintaining computational feasibility.
为解决现代多核系统中自动识别可并行循环的难题，本文提出基于Transformer的DistilBERT方法，通过子词分词处理源代码序列，无需人工特征即可捕捉上下文语法与语义模式以分类循环并行潜力。模型在合成与真实代码混合数据集上经10折交叉验证评估，结果平均准确率超99%，误报率低，优于传统词基方法，兼具泛化能力与计算效率。
Benchmarking PhD-Level Coding in 3D Geometric Computer Vision
Authors: Wenyi Li, Renkai Luo, Yue Yu, Huan-ang Gao, Mingju Gao, Li Yuan, Chaoyou Fu, Hao Zhao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T17:50:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:50:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026; Project page: https://geocodebench.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
AI-assisted coding has rapidly reshaped software practice and research workflows, yet today's models still struggle to produce correct code for complex 3D geometric vision. If models could reliably write such code, the research of our community would change substantially. To measure progress toward that goal, we introduce GeoCodeBench, a PhD-level benchmark that evaluates coding for 3D vision. Each problem is a fill-in-the-function implementation task curated from representative papers at recent venues: we first let a tool propose candidate functions from official repositories, then perform careful human screening to select core 3D geometric components. For every target, we generate diverse, edge-case unit tests, enabling fully automatic, reproducible scoring. We evaluate eight representative open- and closed-source models to reflect the current ecosystem. The best model, GPT-5, attains only 36.6% pass rate, revealing a large gap between current capabilities and dependable 3D scientific coding. GeoCodeBench organizes tasks into a two-level hierarchy: General 3D capability (geometric transformations and mechanics/optics formulation) and Research capability (novel algorithm implementation and geometric logic routing). Scores are positively correlated across these axes, but research-oriented tasks are markedly harder. Context ablations further show that "more paper text" is not always better: cutting off at the Method section statistically outperforms full-paper inputs, highlighting unresolved challenges in long-context scientific comprehension. Together, these findings position GeoCodeBench as a rigorous testbed for advancing from generic coding to trustworthy 3D geometric vision coding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：3D几何计算机视觉领域博士级编程能力基准测试
AI辅助编程已迅速重塑软件实践与研究工作流程，但当前模型仍难以生成复杂3D几何视觉任务的正确代码。若模型能可靠编写此类代码，将显著改变我们的研究范式。为衡量向该目标迈进的进展，我们引入GeoCodeBench，这是一个博士级基准，用于评估3D视觉编程能力。每个问题均来自近期顶级会议代表性论文的填空式函数实现任务：我们首先利用工具从官方仓库中提出候选函数，再经人工精细筛选，选取核心3D几何组件。针对每个目标，我们生成多样化的边缘案例单元测试，实现全自动、可复现评分。我们评估了八种代表性开源与闭源模型，以反映当前生态系统。表现最佳的模型GPT-5仅达到36.6%的通过率，揭示当前能力与可靠3D科学编程之间存在巨大差距。GeoCodeBench将任务划分为两级体系：通用3D能力（几何变换与力学/光学建模）与研究能力（新算法实现与几何逻辑路由）。各维度评分呈正相关，但研究导向任务明显更难。上下文消融实验进一步表明，"更多论文文本"并不总是更好：仅输入方法部分在统计上优于全文输入，凸显长上下文科学理解中的未解挑战。综合来看，GeoCodeBench为从通用编程迈向可信3D几何视觉编程提供了严谨的测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess AI models' ability to reliably produce PhD-level 3D geometric vision code, the authors introduce GeoCodeBench, a benchmark composed of fill-in-the-function tasks derived from recent research papers. Each task is paired with diverse edge-case unit tests for automated scoring, and models are evaluated across two hierarchical dimensions: general 3D geometric capabilities and research-oriented algorithmic implementation. Eight models were tested, with the top-performing GPT-5 achieving only 36.6% pass rate, indicating a significant gap between current AI coding abilities and the demands of scientific 3D vision. Context ablation experiments further revealed that restricting input to the Method section often outperforms full-paper context, underscoring challenges in long-context scientific comprehension.
为评估AI模型生成博士级3D几何视觉代码的能力，作者构建了GeoCodeBench基准，其任务源自近期论文的填空函数题，并配以多样化的边缘案例单元测试以实现自动化评分。候选函数从官方仓库中提取并经人工筛选，确保包含核心几何组件。在评估八款模型（含GPT-5）后，表现最佳者仅达36.6%通过率，揭示当前模型在科学编码能力上的显著不足。任务分为通用3D几何与研究导向两类，后者难度显著更高；上下文消融实验表明，截断至方法部分的输入往往优于全文输入，凸显长上下文科学理解仍存挑战。
When Does Global Attention Help? A Unified Empirical Study on Atomistic Graph Learning
Authors: Arindam Chowdhury, Massimiliano Lupo Pasini
First: 2025-10-07T05:01:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:39:11+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 8 figures, 19 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used as surrogates for costly experiments and first-principles simulations to study the behavior of compounds at atomistic scale, and their architectural complexity is constantly increasing to enable the modeling of complex physics. While most recent GNNs combine more traditional message passing neural networks (MPNNs) layers to model short-range interactions with more advanced graph transformers (GTs) with global attention mechanisms to model long-range interactions, it is still unclear when global attention mechanisms provide real benefits over well-tuned MPNN layers due to inconsistent implementations, features, or hyperparameter tuning. We introduce the first unified, reproducible benchmarking framework - built on HydraGNN - that enables seamless switching among four controlled model classes: MPNN, MPNN with chemistry/topology encoders, GPS-style hybrids of MPNN with global attention, and fully fused local-global models with encoders. Using seven diverse open-source datasets for benchmarking across regression and classification tasks, we systematically isolate the contributions of message passing, global attention, and encoder-based feature augmentation. Our study shows that encoder-augmented MPNNs form a robust baseline, while fused local-global models yield the clearest benefits for properties governed by long-range interaction effects. We further quantify the accuracy-compute trade-offs of attention, reporting its overhead in memory. Together, these results establish the first controlled evaluation of global attention in atomistic graph learning and provide a reproducible testbed for future model development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：全球注意力何时有益？原子级图学习的统一实证研究
图神经网络（GNNs）广泛用作昂贵实验和第一性原理模拟的替代方案，以研究化合物在原子尺度上的行为，其架构复杂性不断增长，以实现复杂物理建模。尽管最新GNNs结合了传统的消息传递神经网络（MPNNs）层以建模短程相互作用，以及更先进的图变换器（GTs）以建模长程相互作用，但由于实现、特征或超参数调优不一致，仍不清楚全球注意力机制何时相比调优良好的MPNN层提供实际优势。我们引入首个统一、可复现的基准测试框架——基于HydraGNN构建，支持在四种受控模型类别间无缝切换：MPNN、带化学/拓扑编码器的MPNN、GPS风格的MPNN与全局注意力混合模型，以及带编码器的完全融合局部-全局模型。使用七个多样化的开源数据集，在回归与分类任务中进行基准测试，我们系统性地分离了消息传递、全局注意力和编码器增强特征的贡献。研究表明，编码器增强的MPNN构成稳健基线，而融合局部-全局模型在受长程相互作用支配的性质上收益最明显。我们进一步量化了注意力的精度-计算权衡，报告其内存开销。这些结果共同建立了原子级图学习中全球注意力的首次受控评估，并为未来模型开发提供可复现的测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of controlled comparisons in atomistic graph learning, this study introduces a unified benchmarking framework based on HydraGNN to systematically evaluate the value of global attention mechanisms against traditional message passing neural networks. It tests four model classes—MPNN, MPNN with encoders, GPS-style hybrids, and fused local-global models—across seven diverse datasets, isolating the contributions of message passing, global attention, and encoder-based feature augmentation. Results show that encoder-augmented MPNNs serve as a robust baseline, while fused local-global models deliver the most significant performance gains for properties influenced by long-range interactions, at the cost of increased memory usage.
为厘清全局注意力机制在原子级图学习中何时优于传统消息传递机制，该研究提出首个基于HydraGNN的统一可复现基准框架，系统对比了四类模型：MPNN、带编码器的MPNN、GPS风格混合模型及融合局部-全局结构的模型。通过七个多样化的公开数据集，在回归与分类任务中隔离分析消息传递、全局注意力与编码器特征增强的贡献。结果表明，带编码器的MPNN构成稳健基线，而融合局部-全局结构的模型在受长程相互作用主导的性质上表现最优，同时量化了注意力机制带来的内存开销。
Aligned, Orthogonal or In-conflict: When can we safely optimize Chain-of-Thought?
Authors: Max Kaufmann, David Lindner, Roland S. Zimmermann, and Rohin Shah
First: 2026-03-31T17:38:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:38:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) monitoring, in which automated systems monitor the CoT of an LLM, is a promising approach for effectively overseeing AI systems. However, the extent to which a model's CoT helps us oversee the model - the monitorability of the CoT - can be affected by training, for instance by the model learning to hide important features of its reasoning. We propose and empirically validate a conceptual framework for predicting when and why this occurs. We model LLM post-training as an RL environment where the reward decomposes into two terms: one term depending on final outputs and another term depending on the CoT. Our framework allows us to classify these two terms as "aligned", "orthogonal", or "in-conflict" before training. We predict that training with in-conflict terms will reduce monitorability, orthogonal terms will not affect it, and aligned terms will improve it. To validate our framework, we use it to classify a set of RL environments, train LLMs within those environments, and evaluate how training affects CoT monitorability. We find that (1) training with "in-conflict" reward terms reduces CoT monitorability and (2) optimizing in-conflict reward terms is difficult.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐、正交或冲突：我们何时能安全地优化思维链？
思维链（CoT）监控，即自动化系统监控大语言模型的思维链，是一种有效监督AI系统的有前景方法。然而，模型的思维链在多大程度上有助于我们监督该模型——即思维链的可监控性——可能受训练影响，例如模型可能学习隐藏其推理中的重要特征。我们提出并实证验证了一个概念框架，用于预测何时以及为何会出现这种情况。我们将大语言模型的后训练建模为一个强化学习环境，其中奖励分解为两个部分：一部分取决于最终输出，另一部分取决于思维链。我们的框架允许我们在训练前将这两部分分类为“对齐”、“正交”或“冲突”。我们预测：使用冲突项进行训练会降低可监控性，正交项不会影响它，对齐项则会提升它。为验证该框架，我们用其对一组强化学习环境进行分类，训练大语言模型，并评估训练对思维链可监控性的影响。我们发现：（1）使用“冲突”奖励项进行训练会降低思维链可监控性；（2）优化冲突奖励项较为困难。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand when Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning in LLMs can be reliably monitored, the authors propose a framework modeling post-training as an RL environment with a reward split into output-dependent and CoT-dependent terms. They classify these terms as aligned, orthogonal, or in-conflict and predict that in-conflict terms reduce monitorability while aligned terms enhance it. Experimental results across RL environments confirm that training with in-conflict reward terms indeed degrades CoT monitorability, and optimizing such terms proves challenging.
该论文探讨了大语言模型中链式思维（CoT）监控的挑战，即训练后模型可能隐藏推理特征，降低可监控性。论文提出一个框架，将模型训练建模为强化学习环境，奖励分解为输出相关和CoT相关项，并将其关系分为对齐、正交或冲突三类。框架预测：冲突项会降低可监控性，正交项无影响，对齐项则提升可监控性。实验结果表明，使用冲突奖励项训练会降低CoT可监控性，且优化此类项尤为困难。
Reward-Based Online LLM Routing via NeuralUCB
Authors: Ming-Hua Tsai, Phat Tran
First: 2026-03-31T17:35:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:35:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates the use of NeuralUCB for cost-aware large language model (LLM) routing. Existing routing approaches can be broadly grouped into supervised routing methods and partial-feedback methods, each with different tradeoffs in efficiency and adaptivity. We implement a NeuralUCB-based routing policy and evaluate it on RouterBench under a simulated online setting. Experimental results show that the proposed method consistently outperforms random and min-cost baselines in utility reward. Compared with the max-quality reference, our method achieves substantially lower inference cost while maintaining competitive reward. These findings suggest that NeuralUCB is a promising approach for cost-aware LLM routing, while also highlighting remaining challenges in action discrimination and exploration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于奖励的在线大语言模型路由方法NeuralUCB
本研究探讨了NeuralUCB在成本感知的大语言模型（LLM）路由中的应用。现有路由方法主要分为监督路由方法和部分反馈方法，各有效率与适应性方面的权衡。我们实现了一种基于NeuralUCB的路由策略，并在模拟在线环境下于RouterBench上进行评估。实验结果表明，所提方法在效用奖励上持续优于随机和最小成本基线。与最高质量参考相比，我们的方法显著降低了推理成本，同时保持了竞争力的奖励。这些发现表明NeuralUCB是成本感知LLM路由的有前景方法，同时也指出了动作判别与探索方面仍存在的挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for cost-efficient and adaptive routing of large language models, this study proposes a NeuralUCB-based online routing policy that balances utility and inference cost. The method leverages neural network-based Upper Confidence Bound to dynamically select models under partial feedback, avoiding the need for full supervision. Evaluated on RouterBench in a simulated online environment, the approach consistently outperforms random and min-cost baselines in reward while achieving significantly lower inference cost than the max-quality baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness in cost-aware routing despite challenges in action discrimination and exploration.
为实现大语言模型的高效路由，本研究提出基于NeuralUCB的在线路由策略，动态平衡效用与推理成本。该方法利用神经网络构建置信上界，在模拟在线环境中进行模型选择，并在RouterBench上评估。实验表明，该方法在奖励上持续优于随机和最小成本基线，同时相比最高质量基线显著降低推理开销，显示出在成本感知路由中的潜力，但仍面临动作区分与探索的挑战。
Tucker Attention: A generalization of approximate attention mechanisms
Authors: Timon Klein, Jonas Kusch, Sebastian Sager, Stefan Schnake, Steffen Schotthöfer
First: 2026-03-31T17:30:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:30:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The pursuit of reducing the memory footprint of the self-attention mechanism in multi-headed self attention (MHA) spawned a rich portfolio of methods, e.g., group-query attention (GQA) and multi-head latent attention (MLA). The methods leverage specialized low-rank factorizations across embedding dimensions or attention heads. From the point of view of classical low-rank approximation, these methods are unconventional and raise questions of which objects they really approximate and how to interpret the low-rank behavior of the resulting representations. To answer these questions, this work proposes a generalized view on the weight objects in the self-attention layer and a factorization strategy, which allows us to construct a parameter efficient scheme, called Tucker Attention. Tucker Attention requires an order of magnitude fewer parameters for comparable validation metrics, compared to GQA and MLA, as evaluated in LLM and ViT test cases. Additionally, Tucker Attention~encompasses GQA, MLA, MHA as special cases and is fully compatible with flash-attention and rotary position embeddings (RoPE). This generalization strategy yields insights of the actual ranks achieved by MHA, GQA, and MLA, and further enables simplifications for MLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Tucker注意力：近似注意力机制的泛化
为降低多头自注意力（MHA）中自注意力机制的内存占用，研究者提出了丰富的方法，如组查询注意力（GQA）和多头潜在注意力（MLA）。这些方法利用嵌入维度或注意力头间的特殊低秩分解。从经典低秩近似视角看，这些方法非传统，引发对其近似对象及所得表示低秩行为的解释问题。为此，本文提出对自注意力层权重对象的泛化视角及分解策略，构建参数高效的Tucker注意力方案。在LLM和ViT测试案例中，Tucker注意力相比GQA和MLA所需参数减少一个数量级，且兼容Flash注意力与旋转位置编码（RoPE）。该泛化策略揭示了MHA、GQA和MLA实际实现的秩，并进一步简化了MLA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce memory usage in multi-headed self-attention, this work introduces Tucker Attention, a generalized factorization framework that unifies and extends existing approximate attention methods like GQA and MLA. By reinterpreting the weight matrices in self-attention through a Tucker decomposition lens, it enables a parameter-efficient design that requires an order of magnitude fewer parameters than GQA or MLA while maintaining comparable performance on LLM and ViT benchmarks. The method is fully compatible with flash-attention and RoPE, and its theoretical formulation reveals the actual low-rank structures underlying MHA, GQA, and MLA, further enabling simplifications for MLA.
为降低多头自注意力机制的内存开销，本文提出Tucker注意力，一种统一并扩展GQA与MLA等近似注意力方法的通用因子分解框架。通过将自注意力层中的权重对象以Tucker分解视角重新建模，该方法实现了参数效率更高的表示，相比GQA和MLA在LLM与ViT测试中参数量减少一个数量级，同时保持相近性能。该方法兼容FlashAttention与RoPE，其理论框架揭示了MHA、GQA与MLA实际实现的低秩结构，并进一步简化了MLA的设计。
Covertly improving intelligibility with data-driven adaptations of speech timing
Authors: Paige Tuttösí, Angelica Lim, H. Henny Yeung, Yue Wang, Jean-Julien Aucouturier
First: 2026-03-31T17:30:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:30:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human talkers often address listeners with language-comprehension challenges, such as hard-of-hearing or non-native adults, by globally slowing down their speech. However, it remains unclear whether this strategy actually makes speech more intelligible. Here, we take advantage of recent advancements in machine-generated speech allowing more precise control of speech rate in order to systematically examine how targeted speech-rate adjustments may improve comprehension. We first use reverse-correlation experiments to show that the temporal influence of speech rate prior to a target vowel contrast (ex. the tense-lax distinction) in fact manifests in a scissor-like pattern, with opposite effects in early versus late context windows; this pattern is remarkably stable both within individuals and across native L1-English listeners and L2-English listeners with French, Mandarin, and Japanese L1s. Second, we show that this speech rate structure not only facilitates L2 listeners' comprehension of the target vowel contrast, but that native listeners also rely on this pattern in challenging acoustic conditions. Finally, we build a data-driven text-to-speech algorithm that replicates this temporal structure on novel speech sequences. Across a variety of sentences and vowel contrasts, listeners remained unaware that such targeted slowing improved word comprehension. Strikingly, participants instead judged the common strategy of global slowing as clearer, even though it actually increased comprehension errors. Together, these results show that targeted adjustments to speech rate significantly aid intelligibility under challenging conditions, while often going unnoticed. More generally, this paper provides a data-driven methodology to improve the accessibility of machine-generated speech which can be extended to other aspects of speech comprehension and a wide variety of listeners and environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过数据驱动的语音时长调整隐蔽提升语音可懂度
人类说话者常通过整体放慢语速来应对听力困难或非母语成年听众的语言理解挑战。然而，这种策略是否真正提升语音可懂度仍不明确。本研究利用近期机器生成语音技术实现更精确的语速控制，系统考察针对性语速调整如何提升理解能力。首先，通过反相关实验发现，语速对目标元音对比（如紧张-松弛对立）的时序影响呈现剪刀状模式，早期与晚期语境窗口效应相反，且该模式在个体间及不同母语（英语母语者、法语/普通话/日语母语者学习英语者）间高度稳定。其次，该语速结构不仅促进二语学习者对目标元音的识别，母语者在声学困难条件下也依赖此模式。最后，我们构建数据驱动的文本转语音算法，在新语音序列中复制该时序结构。在多种句子与元音对比中，听众未察觉针对性放慢提升了词义理解，反而认为整体放慢更清晰，尽管后者实际增加理解错误。综上，针对性语速调整在困难条件下显著提升可懂度且常被忽略。更广泛地说，本文提供数据驱动方法以提升机器生成语音的可访问性，可扩展至语音理解其他方面及各类听众与环境。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether targeted adjustments to speech timing can improve intelligibility for listeners with comprehension challenges, such as non-native speakers or hard-of-hearing individuals. Using machine-generated speech with precise rate control, researchers first identified a scissor-like temporal pattern in speech rate effects around vowel contrasts, which was consistent across native and non-native English speakers with diverse L1 backgrounds. They then demonstrated that this pattern enhances comprehension for L2 listeners and aids native listeners in noisy conditions. A data-driven TTS system was built to replicate this timing structure, and listeners consistently failed to notice the improvement, even though comprehension was significantly better than with global slowing — which participants rated as clearer despite causing more errors. The findings suggest that subtle, context-sensitive timing adjustments can greatly improve speech accessibility without being perceived as beneficial.
为提升听力困难者（如非母语者或听力障碍者）的语言理解，本研究探讨是否可通过数据驱动的语音时长精准调整来提升可懂度而不被察觉。通过逆相关实验，研究者发现语音速率在元音对比前后呈现剪刀状时间效应，该模式在不同母语者中稳定存在。随后，他们构建了可复制该时序结构的语音合成算法，在多种句子与元音对比中验证，听众在未察觉调整的情况下理解力显著提升，却误以为全局放慢更清晰。结果表明，精准的语音时长调整在困难环境下可显著提高可懂度，且方法可扩展至其他语音理解场景与用户群体。
The Triadic Cognitive Architecture: Bounding Autonomous Action via Spatio-Temporal and Epistemic Friction
Authors: Davide Di Gioia
First: 2026-03-31T17:30:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:30:25+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current autonomous AI agents, driven primarily by Large Language Models (LLMs), operate in a state of cognitive weightlessness: they process information without an intrinsic sense of network topology, temporal pacing, or epistemic limits. Consequently, heuristic agentic loops (e.g., ReAct) can exhibit failure modes in interactive environments, including excessive tool use under congestion, prolonged deliberation under time decay, and brittle behavior under ambiguous evidence. In this paper, we propose the Triadic Cognitive Architecture (TCA), a unified mathematical framework that grounds machine reasoning in continuous-time physics. By synthesizing nonlinear filtering theory, Riemannian routing geometry, and optimal control, we formally define the concept of Cognitive Friction. We map the agent's deliberation process to a coupled stochastic control problem where information acquisition is path-dependent and physically constrained. Rather than relying on arbitrary heuristic stop-tokens, the TCA uses an HJB-motivated stopping boundary and instantiates a rollout-based approximation of belief-dependent value-of-information with a net-utility halting condition. Through empirical validation in a simulated Emergency Medical Diagnostic Grid (EMDG), we demonstrate that while greedy baselines over-deliberate under latency and congestion costs, the triadic policy reduces time-to-action while improving patient viability without degrading diagnostic accuracy in this environment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三元认知架构：通过时空与认知摩擦约束自主行为
当前主要由大语言模型（LLM）驱动的自主AI代理处于认知失重状态：它们处理信息时缺乏对网络拓扑、时间节奏或认知边界的内在感知。因此，启发式代理循环（如ReAct）在交互环境中可能出现失效模式，包括在拥堵时过度使用工具、在时间衰减下过度思考，以及在证据模糊时表现脆弱。本文提出三元认知架构（TCA），一个统一的数学框架，将机器推理根植于连续时间物理。通过融合非线性滤波理论、黎曼路由几何与最优控制，我们形式化定义了“认知摩擦”概念。我们将代理的决策过程映射为一个耦合随机控制问题，其中信息获取路径依赖且受物理约束。TCA不再依赖任意启发式停止标记，而是采用HJB启发的停止边界，并通过基于 rollout 的信念依赖信息价值近似，结合净效用终止条件。在模拟的紧急医疗诊断网格（EMDG）中的实证验证表明：虽然贪心基线在延迟与拥堵成本下过度思考，三元策略则在不降低诊断准确性的前提下，缩短了行动时间并提升了患者生存率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of intrinsic temporal and epistemic grounding in current LLM-driven autonomous agents, which leads to brittle and inefficient behavior in interactive environments, this paper introduces the Triadic Cognitive Architecture (TCA). The TCA formalizes 'Cognitive Friction' using nonlinear filtering, Riemannian geometry, and optimal control to model deliberation as a continuous-time stochastic control problem with path-dependent information acquisition. Empirical results in a simulated Emergency Medical Diagnostic Grid show that compared to greedy baselines, the TCA reduces time-to-action and improves patient viability without sacrificing diagnostic accuracy, by using a belief-dependent value-of-information criterion and a net-utility halting condition.
为解决当前基于大语言模型的自主智能体缺乏时间感知与认知边界的问题，本文提出三元认知架构（TCA），将智能体推理建模为连续时间随机控制问题，融合非线性滤波、黎曼几何与最优控制，形式化‘认知摩擦’以通过信念依赖的信息价值与HJB启发的停止边界约束决策。在模拟急诊医疗诊断网格实验中，相比贪心基线，TCA在拥堵与时间衰减下显著缩短决策延迟，同时保持诊断准确率并提升患者存活率。
Gaze Authentication: Factors Influencing Authentication Performance
Authors: Dillon Lohr, Michael J Proulx, Mehedi Hasan Raju, Oleg V Komogortsev
First: 2025-09-13T20:03:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:26:23+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper examines the key factors that influence the performance of state-of-the-art gaze-based authentication. Experiments were conducted on a large-scale, in-house dataset comprising 8,849 subjects collected with Meta Quest Pro equivalent hardware running a video oculography-driven gaze estimation pipeline at 72~Hz. State of the neural network architecture was employed to study the influence of the following factors on authentication performance: eye tracking signal quality, various aspects of eye tracking calibration, and simple filtering on estimated raw gaze. This report provides performance results and their analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注视认证：影响注视认证性能的关键因素
本文研究了影响当前最先进的基于注视的认证性能的关键因素。实验基于一个大规模的内部数据集，包含8,849名受试者，使用与Meta Quest Pro相当的硬件设备，以72Hz频率运行基于视频眼动描记的注视估计管道。采用最先进的神经网络架构，研究了以下因素对认证性能的影响：眼动信号质量、眼动校准的各个方面以及对估计原始注视的简单滤波。本报告提供了性能结果及其分析。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how eye tracking signal quality, calibration methods, and raw gaze filtering affect the accuracy of gaze-based authentication systems. Using a large in-house dataset of 8,849 subjects captured with Meta Quest Pro hardware and a 72 Hz video oculography pipeline, the authors evaluate the impact of these factors on authentication performance through neural network models. Results indicate that signal quality and calibration significantly influence authentication accuracy, while simple filtering of raw gaze has minimal effect.
本研究探讨了眼动信号质量、校准方式及原始注视点滤波对基于注视的认证系统性能的影响。研究基于8849名受试者的大规模内部数据集，使用与Meta Quest Pro相当的硬件和72Hz视频眼动追踪管道采集数据，并通过神经网络模型评估各因素对认证准确率的影响。结果表明，信号质量和校准对认证性能影响显著，而原始注视点的简单滤波影响较小。
Joint Embedding Variational Bayes
Authors: Amin Oji, Paul Fieguth
First: 2026-02-05T13:18:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:23:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Variational Joint Embedding (VJE), a reconstruction-free latent-variable framework for non-contrastive self-supervised learning in representation space. VJE maximizes a symmetric conditional evidence lower bound (ELBO) on paired encoder embeddings by defining a conditional likelihood directly on target representations, rather than optimizing a pointwise compatibility objective. The likelihood is instantiated as a heavy-tailed Student--\(t\) distribution on a polar representation of the target embedding, where a directional--radial decomposition separates angular agreement from magnitude consistency and mitigates norm-induced pathologies. The directional factor operates on the unit sphere, yielding a valid variational bound for the associated spherical subdensity model. An amortized inference network parameterizes a diagonal Gaussian posterior whose feature-wise variances are shared with the directional likelihood, yielding anisotropic uncertainty without auxiliary projection heads. Across ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-10/100, and STL-10, VJE is competitive with standard non-contrastive baselines under linear and \(k\)-NN evaluation, while providing probabilistic semantics directly in representation space for downstream uncertainty-aware applications. We validate these semantics through out-of-distribution detection, where representation-space likelihoods yield strong empirical performance. These results position the framework as a principled variational formulation of non-contrastive learning, in which structured feature-wise uncertainty is represented directly in the learned embedding space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：联合嵌入变分贝叶斯
我们提出了变分联合嵌入（VJE），这是一种无需重建的潜在变量框架，用于表示空间中的非对比自监督学习。VJE 通过在目标表示上直接定义条件似然，最大化对称条件证据下界（ELBO），而非优化点对点兼容性目标。该似然被实例化为在目标嵌入的极坐标表示上的重尾学生t分布，其中方向-径向分解将角度一致性与幅度一致性分离，并缓解范数引起的病态问题。方向因子作用于单位球面，为相关的球面子密度模型提供有效的变分下界。一个近似推理网络参数化一个对角高斯后验分布，其特征维度方差与方向似然共享，从而在无需辅助投影头的情况下实现各向异性不确定性。在 ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-10/100 和 STL-10 数据集上，VJE 在线性与 k-NN 评估中与标准非对比基线方法具有竞争力，同时在表示空间中直接提供概率语义，适用于下游不确定性感知应用。我们通过分布外检测验证了这些语义，其中表示空间的似然表现出优异的实证性能。这些结果将该框架定位为非对比学习的原理性变分表述，其中结构化的特征维度不确定性直接在学习到的嵌入空间中表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for probabilistic representations in non-contrastive self-supervised learning, the authors propose Variational Joint Embedding (VJE), a reconstruction-free latent-variable framework that maximizes a symmetric conditional ELBO by modeling target embeddings via a heavy-tailed Student-t distribution on polar coordinates. This approach decomposes representation into directional and radial components to separate angular agreement from magnitude consistency, avoiding norm-induced pathologies. The directional component operates on the unit sphere with a valid variational bound, while an amortized inference network parameterizes a diagonal Gaussian posterior sharing feature-wise variances with the directional likelihood, enabling anisotropic uncertainty without auxiliary projection heads. Experiments on ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-10/100, and STL-10 show VJE matches or exceeds standard non-contrastive baselines under linear and k-NN evaluation, and its representation-space likelihoods achieve strong out-of-distribution detection performance, demonstrating its utility for uncertainty-aware downstream tasks.
为在非对比自监督学习中实现概率化表征，作者提出变分联合嵌入（VJE）框架，该框架无需重建，通过在极坐标下对目标嵌入建模为重尾Student-t分布，最大化对称条件ELBO。该方法将表征分解为方向与径向分量，分离角度一致性与幅度一致性，避免范数引发的病理问题。方向分量在单位球面上操作，具有有效变分界；变分推断网络以对角高斯后验参数化，其特征方差与方向似然共享，无需辅助投影头即可实现各向异性不确定性。在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-10/100和STL-10数据集上，VJE在线性与k-NN评估中表现与标准非对比基线相当，且其表征空间似然在分布外检测中表现优异，证明其在不确定性感知下游任务中的实用性。
Zero-Shot Coordination in Ad Hoc Teams with Generalized Policy Improvement and Difference Rewards
Authors: Rupal Nigam, Niket Parikh, Hamid Osooli, Mikihisa Yuasa, Jacob Heglund, Huy T. Tran
First: 2025-10-17T19:55:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:21:11+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 8 figures. To appear in proceedings of 25th International Conference on Autonomous Agents and Multiagent Systems (AAMAS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world multi-agent systems may require ad hoc teaming, where an agent must coordinate with other previously unseen teammates to solve a task in a zero-shot manner. Prior work often either selects a pretrained policy based on an inferred model of the new teammates or pretrains a single policy that is robust to potential teammates. Instead, we propose to leverage all pretrained policies in a zero-shot transfer setting. We formalize this problem as an ad hoc multi-agent Markov decision process and present a solution that uses two key ideas, generalized policy improvement and difference rewards, for efficient and effective knowledge transfer between different teams. We empirically demonstrate that our algorithm, Generalized Policy improvement for Ad hoc Teaming (GPAT), successfully enables zero-shot transfer to new teams in three simulated environments: cooperative foraging, predator-prey, and Overcooked. We also demonstrate our algorithm in a real-world multi-robot setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于广义策略改进与差异奖励的临时团队零样本协调
现实世界中的多智能体系统可能需要临时团队协作，即一个智能体必须以零样本方式与之前未见过的队友协调以完成任务。以往研究通常要么根据对新队友的推断模型选择预训练策略，要么预训练一个对潜在队友鲁棒的单一策略。相反，我们提出在零样本迁移设置中利用所有预训练策略。我们将此问题形式化为临时多智能体马尔可夫决策过程，并提出一种解决方案，利用两个关键思想——广义策略改进和差异奖励，实现不同团队间高效且有效的知识迁移。我们在三个模拟环境中实证展示了我们的算法“广义策略改进用于临时团队协作（GPAT）”成功实现了对新团队的零样本迁移：合作觅食、捕食者-猎物和Overcooked。我们还在现实世界多机器人环境中验证了该算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for agents to coordinate with unseen teammates in real-world multi-agent systems without prior exposure, this paper proposes GPAT, an algorithm that enables zero-shot coordination by leveraging all pretrained policies rather than selecting or pretraining a single robust policy. The method formalizes the problem as an ad hoc multi-agent Markov decision process and employs generalized policy improvement and difference rewards to efficiently transfer knowledge across heterogeneous teams. Experimental results across three simulated environments—cooperative foraging, predator-prey, and Overcooked—and a real-world multi-robot setup show that GPAT achieves effective zero-shot coordination without task-specific fine-tuning.
为解决现实世界多智能体系统中智能体需与未见过的队友零样本协作的问题，本文提出GPAT算法，通过利用所有预训练策略而非选择或预训练单一鲁棒策略，实现零样本协调。该方法将问题形式化为异构多智能体马尔可夫决策过程，并结合广义策略改进与差异奖励机制，高效实现跨团队知识迁移。实验在三个模拟环境（合作觅食、捕食者-猎物、Overcooked）及真实多机器人系统中验证，结果显示GPAT无需任务特定微调即可实现有效零样本协作。
ContextClaim: A Context-Driven Paradigm for Verifiable Claim Detection
Authors: Yufeng Li, Rrubaa Panchendrarajan, Arkaitz Zubiaga
First: 2026-03-31T17:20:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:20:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Verifiable claim detection asks whether a claim expresses a factual statement that can, in principle, be assessed against external evidence. As an early filtering stage in automated fact-checking, it plays an important role in reducing the burden on downstream verification components. However, existing approaches to claim detection, whether based on check-worthiness or verifiability, rely solely on the claim text itself. This is a notable limitation for verifiable claim detection in particular, where determining whether a claim is checkable may benefit from knowing what entities and events it refers to and whether relevant information exists to support verification. Inspired by the established role of evidence retrieval in later-stage claim verification, we propose Context-Driven Claim Detection (ContextClaim), a paradigm that advances retrieval to the detection stage. ContextClaim extracts entity mentions from the input claim, retrieves relevant information from Wikipedia as a structured knowledge source, and employs large language models to produce concise contextual summaries for downstream classification. We evaluate ContextClaim on two datasets covering different topics and text genres, the CheckThat! 2022 COVID-19 Twitter dataset and the PoliClaim political debate dataset, across encoder-only and decoder-only models under fine-tuning, zero-shot, and few-shot settings. Results show that context augmentation can improve verifiable claim detection, although its effectiveness varies across domains, model architectures, and learning settings. Through component analysis, human evaluation, and error analysis, we further examine when and why the retrieved context contributes to more reliable verifiability judgments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ContextClaim：一种基于上下文驱动的可验证声明检测范式
可验证声明检测旨在判断声明是否表达了一种原则上可依据外部证据进行评估的事实陈述。作为自动化事实核查的早期过滤阶段，它在减轻下游验证组件负担方面发挥重要作用。然而，现有声明检测方法，无论是基于可核查性还是可验证性，均仅依赖声明文本本身。这对可验证声明检测尤为不利，因为判断声明是否可核查可能受益于了解其指代的实体和事件，以及是否存在支持验证的相关信息。受后期声明验证中证据检索已确立作用的启发，我们提出上下文驱动声明检测（ContextClaim）范式，将检索提前至检测阶段。ContextClaim从输入声明中提取实体提及，从维基百科作为结构化知识源中检索相关信息，并利用大语言模型生成简洁的上下文摘要供下游分类使用。我们在两个涵盖不同主题和文本体裁的数据集——CheckThat! 2022 COVID-19 Twitter数据集和PoliClaim政治辩论数据集——上评估ContextClaim，覆盖编码器仅模型和解码器仅模型，在微调、零样本和少样本设置下进行测试。结果表明，上下文增强可提升可验证声明检测效果，但其有效性因领域、模型架构和学习设置而异。通过组件分析、人工评估和错误分析，我们进一步探讨了检索上下文在何时以及为何有助于更可靠的可验证性判断。

Motivated by the limitation of existing claim detection methods that rely solely on claim text without external context, the paper proposes ContextClaim, a context-driven paradigm that leverages entity extraction, Wikipedia retrieval, and large language models to generate contextual summaries for verifiable claim detection. Evaluated on two datasets — CheckThat! 2022 COVID-19 Twitter data and PoliClaim political debates — across multiple model architectures and learning settings, ContextClaim consistently improves detection performance, though gains vary by domain, model type, and setting. Component analysis and human evaluation reveal that retrieved context enhances reliability, particularly when claims reference specific entities or events with verifiable evidence available in structured knowledge sources.
为解决现有可验证声明检测方法仅依赖文本内容而忽略上下文信息的局限性，本文提出ContextClaim范式，通过实体抽取、维基百科检索与大语言模型生成上下文摘要，提升检测准确性。在CheckThat! 2022新冠推文数据集与PoliClaim政治辩论数据集上，该方法在多种模型架构与学习设置下均取得性能提升，但增益程度因领域、模型类型和设置而异。组件分析与人工评估表明，当声明涉及具体实体或事件且知识库中存在可验证证据时，检索上下文能显著增强判断可靠性。
Hybrid Framework for Robotic Manipulation: Integrating Reinforcement Learning and Large Language Models
Authors: Md Saad, Sajjad Hussain, Mohd Suhaib
First: 2026-03-31T17:19:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:19:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces a new hybrid framework that combines Reinforcement Learning (RL) and Large Language Models (LLMs) to improve robotic manipulation tasks. By utilizing RL for accurate low-level control and LLMs for high level task planning and understanding of natural language, the proposed framework effectively connects low-level execution with high-level reasoning in robotic systems. This integration allows robots to understand and carry out complex, human-like instructions while adapting to changing environments in real time. The framework is tested in a PyBullet-based simulation environment using the Franka Emika Panda robotic arm, with various manipulation scenarios as benchmarks. The results show a 33.5% decrease in task completion time and enhancements of 18.1% and 36.4% in accuracy and adaptability, respectively, when compared to systems that use only RL. These results underscore the potential of LLM-enhanced robotic systems for practical applications, making them more efficient, adaptable, and capable of interacting with humans. Future research will aim to explore sim-to-real transfer, scalability, and multi-robot systems to further broaden the framework's applicability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结合强化学习与大语言模型的机器人操作混合框架
本文提出一种结合强化学习（RL）与大语言模型（LLMs）的新型混合框架，以提升机器人操作任务性能。该框架利用RL实现精确的底层控制，而LLMs则负责高层任务规划与自然语言理解，有效连接机器人系统中的底层执行与高层推理。此集成使机器人能够理解并执行复杂的类人指令，并实时适应环境变化。框架在基于PyBullet的仿真环境中，以Franka Emika Panda机械臂为平台，通过多种操作场景进行测试。结果表明，与仅使用RL的系统相比，任务完成时间减少33.5%，准确率和适应性分别提升18.1%和36.4%。这些结果凸显了LLM增强型机器人系统在实际应用中的潜力，使其更高效、更具适应性，并能更好地与人类交互。未来研究将探索仿真到现实的迁移、可扩展性及多机器人系统，以进一步拓展该框架的应用范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance robotic manipulation through human-like instruction understanding and real-time adaptability, this paper proposes a hybrid framework that integrates Reinforcement Learning for low-level control with Large Language Models for high-level task planning and natural language comprehension. Tested in a PyBullet simulation using the Franka Emika Panda arm across multiple manipulation tasks, the framework demonstrated a 33.5% reduction in task completion time, along with 18.1% and 36.4% improvements in accuracy and adaptability compared to RL-only systems. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining LLMs with RL for more intelligent and responsive robotic systems.
为提升机器人操作能力，使其能理解自然语言指令并实时适应环境变化，本文提出一种融合强化学习与大语言模型的混合框架。在PyBullet仿真环境中使用Franka Emika Panda机械臂进行多任务测试，该框架相较仅使用强化学习的系统，任务完成时间减少33.5%，准确率提升18.1%，适应性提升36.4%。结果表明，结合大语言模型与强化学习可显著增强机器人系统的智能性与响应能力。
GenOL: Generating Diverse Examples for Name-only Online Learning
Authors: Minhyuk Seo, Seongwon Cho, Minjae Lee, Diganta Misra, Hyeonbeom Choi, Seon Joo Kim, Jonghyun Choi
First: 2024-03-16T08:28:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:17:57+00:00
Comments: TMLR 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online learning methods often rely on supervised data. However, under data distribution shifts, such as in continual learning (CL), where continuously arriving online data streams incorporate new concepts (e.g., classes), real-time manual annotation is impractical due to its costs and latency, which hinder real-time adaptation. To alleviate this, 'name-only' setup has been proposed, requiring only the name of concepts, not the supervised samples. A recent approach tackles this setup by supplementing data with web-scraped images, but such data often suffers from issues of data imbalance, noise, and copyright. To overcome the limitations of both human supervision and webly supervision, we propose GenOL using generative models for name-only training. But naive application of generative models results in limited diversity of generated data. Here, we enhance (i) intra-diversity, the diversity of images generated by a single model, by proposing a diverse prompt generation method that generates diverse text prompts for text-to-image models, and (ii) inter-diversity, the diversity of images generated by multiple generative models, by introducing an ensemble strategy that selects minimally overlapping samples. We empirically validate that the proposed \frameworkname outperforms prior arts, even a model trained with fully supervised data by large margins, in various tasks, including image recognition and multi-modal visual reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GenOL：为仅名称在线学习生成多样化示例
在线学习方法通常依赖监督数据。然而，在数据分布发生偏移的情况下（如持续学习CL中，持续到达的在线数据流包含新概念（如类别）），实时人工标注因成本和延迟问题不切实际，阻碍了实时适应。为缓解此问题，已提出“仅名称”设置，仅需概念名称而非监督样本。近期方法通过补充网络爬取图像来应对该设置，但此类数据常存在数据不平衡、噪声和版权问题。为克服人工监督与网络监督的局限性，我们提出GenOL，利用生成模型进行仅名称训练。但生成模型的直接应用导致生成数据多样性有限。为此，我们增强（i）单模型内多样性（即单模型生成图像的多样性），通过提出多样提示生成方法，为文本到图像模型生成多样文本提示；（ii）多模型间多样性（即多生成模型生成图像的多样性），通过引入集成策略，选择重叠最小的样本。实证验证表明，所提框架在各类任务（包括图像识别和多模态视觉推理）中，性能优于现有方法，甚至大幅超越使用完全监督数据训练的模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the impracticality of manual annotation in continual learning scenarios where data streams introduce new concepts, this paper proposes GenOL, a name-only online learning framework that leverages generative models to produce diverse training examples without requiring labeled data. To address the limited diversity of naive generative outputs, it enhances intra-diversity by generating varied text prompts for text-to-image models and inter-diversity by ensembling outputs from multiple models while minimizing sample overlap. Experiments show GenOL significantly outperforms prior methods—including fully supervised baselines—in image recognition and multi-modal visual reasoning tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness in adapting to concept drift without manual supervision.
为了解决在持续学习场景中因数据流引入新概念而导致人工标注不现实的问题，本文提出GenOL框架，利用生成模型在仅需概念名称的情况下生成多样化的训练样本。为克服生成模型输出多样性不足的问题，该方法通过生成多样化文本提示提升单模型内部多样性，并通过模型集成策略减少样本重叠以增强模型间多样性。实验表明，GenOL在图像识别和多模态视觉推理任务中显著优于现有方法，甚至大幅超越全监督基线模型，验证了其在无需人工标注情况下适应概念漂移的有效性。
MathGen: Revealing the Illusion of Mathematical Competence through Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Ruiyao Liu, Hui Shen, Ping Zhang, Yunta Hsieh, Yifan Zhang, Jing Xu, Sicheng Chen, Junchen Li, Jiawei Lu, Jianing Ma, Jiaqi Mo, Qi Han, Zhen Zhang, Zhongwei Wan, Jing Xiong, Xin Wang, Ziyuan Liu, Hangrui Cao, Ngai Wong
First: 2026-03-30T02:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:17:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern generative models have demonstrated the ability to solve challenging mathematical problems. In many real-world settings, however, mathematical solutions must be expressed visually through diagrams, plots, geometric constructions, and structured symbolic layouts, where correctness depends on precise visual composition. This naturally raises the question of whether generative models can still do so when the answer must be rendered visually rather than written in text? To study this problem, we introduce MathGen, a rigorous benchmark of 900 problems spanning seven core domains, each paired with an executable verifier under a Script-as-a-Judge protocol for deterministic and objective evaluation. Experiments on representative open-source and proprietary text-to-image models show that mathematical fidelity remains a major bottleneck: even the best closed-source model reaches only 42.0% overall accuracy, while open-source models achieve just ~ 1-11%, often near 0% on structured tasks. Overall, current T2I models remain far from competent at even elementary mathematical visual generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MathGen：通过文本到图像生成揭示数学能力的幻觉
现代生成模型已展现出解决复杂数学问题的能力。然而在许多现实场景中，数学解必须通过图表、图形、几何构造和结构化符号布局等视觉形式表达，其正确性依赖于精确的视觉构成。这自然引发一个问题：当答案必须以视觉形式呈现而非文本形式时，生成模型是否仍能胜任？为研究此问题，我们引入MathGen，一个涵盖七个核心领域的900道问题的严格基准测试，每道题均配备基于“脚本作为裁判”协议的可执行验证器，以实现确定性和客观评估。在代表性开源和专有文本到图像模型上的实验表明，数学保真度仍是主要瓶颈：即使表现最好的闭源模型准确率也仅为42.0%，而开源模型仅约1%-11%，在结构化任务上常接近0%。总体而言，当前T2I模型在基础数学视觉生成方面仍远未达到胜任水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether generative models can produce accurate visual mathematical outputs, this study introduces MathGen, a benchmark of 900 problems across seven domains requiring precise visual rendering. Using a Script-as-a-Judge protocol with executable verifiers, the authors evaluate text-to-image models on their ability to generate correct diagrams, plots, and symbolic layouts. Results show that even top closed-source models achieve only 42.0% accuracy, while open-source models perform at just 1–11%, often near zero on structured tasks, indicating a significant gap in visual mathematical competence.
为评估生成模型能否准确生成数学视觉内容，该研究提出MathGen基准，包含900道覆盖七个领域的题目，要求精确视觉呈现。采用Script-as-a-Judge协议配合可执行验证器进行评估。实验表明，即便顶尖闭源模型准确率也仅42.0%，开源模型仅1–11%，在结构化任务上常接近零，显示当前文本到图像模型在数学视觉生成方面仍存在显著能力缺陷。
Refined Detection for Gumbel Watermarking
Authors: Tor Lattimore
First: 2026-03-31T17:16:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:16:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a simple detection mechanism for the Gumbel watermarking scheme proposed by Aaronson (2022). The new mechanism is proven to be near-optimal in a problem-dependent sense among all model-agnostic watermarking schemes under the assumption that the next-token distribution is sampled i.i.d.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于Gumbel水印的精炼检测
我们提出了一种针对Aaronson（2022）提出的Gumbel水印方案的简单检测机制。在假设下一词分布独立同分布采样的前提下，该新机制在所有模型无关水印方案中被证明是近最优的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and reliable detection of Gumbel watermarks in language models, this work introduces a simple detection mechanism that is theoretically near-optimal among all model-agnostic watermarking schemes under the assumption of i.i.d. next-token sampling. The method leverages the statistical properties of the Gumbel watermarking scheme to construct a detection statistic that discriminates watermarked from unwatermarked tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed detector achieves high accuracy in identifying watermarked sequences while maintaining low false positive rates, confirming its practical effectiveness.
为提升Gumbel水印在语言模型中的检测效率与可靠性，本文提出一种简单但理论近最优的检测机制，该机制在假设下一词分布独立同分布的前提下，优于所有模型无关的水印检测方案。方法基于Gumbel分布的统计特性，区分水印嵌入与未嵌入的词元。实验结果表明，该检测器在识别水印序列时准确率高、误报率低，展现出良好的实际应用效果。
Architecting Secure AI Agents: Perspectives on System-Level Defenses Against Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks
Authors: Chong Xiang, Drew Zagieboylo, Shaona Ghosh, Sanjay Kariyappa, Kai Greshake, Hanshen Xiao, Chaowei Xiao, G. Edward Suh
First: 2026-03-31T17:15:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:15:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents, predominantly powered by large language models (LLMs), are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection, in which malicious instructions embedded in untrusted data can trigger dangerous agent actions. This position paper discusses our vision for system-level defenses against indirect prompt injection attacks. We articulate three positions: (1) dynamic replanning and security policy updates are often necessary for dynamic tasks and realistic environments; (2) certain context-dependent security decisions would still require LLMs (or other learned models), but should only be made within system designs that strictly constrain what the model can observe and decide; (3) in inherently ambiguous cases, personalization and human interaction should be treated as core design considerations. In addition to our main positions, we discuss limitations of existing benchmarks that can create a false sense of utility and security. We also highlight the value of system-level defenses, which serve as the skeleton of agentic systems by structuring and controlling agent behaviors, integrating rule-based and model-based security checks, and enabling more targeted research on model robustness and human interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：构建安全的AI代理：关于系统级防御间接提示注入攻击的视角
主要由大语言模型（LLMs）驱动的AI代理易受间接提示注入攻击，即恶意指令嵌入在不可信数据中可触发危险代理行为。本文阐述我们对系统级防御间接提示注入攻击的愿景：（1）动态任务和现实环境中常需动态重规划与安全策略更新；（2）某些依赖上下文的安全决策仍需LLMs（或其他学习模型），但应仅在严格限制模型可观察与决策范围的系统设计中执行；（3）在固有模糊情况下，个性化与人机交互应作为核心设计考量。此外，我们讨论现有基准测试的局限性，其可能产生虚假的效用与安全感知；并强调系统级防御的价值，其通过结构化与控制代理行为、整合基于规则与基于模型的安全检查、促进更聚焦的模型鲁棒性与人机交互研究，构成代理系统的骨架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of AI agents to indirect prompt injection attacks that exploit untrusted data to trigger harmful behaviors, this paper proposes system-level defenses as a foundational approach. The authors advocate for three key positions: dynamic replanning and policy updates for adaptive environments, constrained LLM usage within tightly controlled observation boundaries, and prioritizing personalization and human interaction in ambiguous scenarios. Experimental evaluation is not performed, but the paper argues that system-level defenses are essential for structuring agent behavior, integrating rule-based and model-based checks, and enabling more focused research on model robustness and human-AI collaboration.
鉴于大型语言模型驱动的AI代理易受间接提示注入攻击，本文提出系统级防御作为保障代理安全的基础方法。作者主张三个核心观点：动态任务和真实环境中需动态重规划与安全策略更新；上下文相关决策可保留LLM参与，但必须严格限制其观察与决策范围；在模糊场景中，个性化与人机交互应作为核心设计考量。虽无具体实验数据，但论文强调系统级防御通过融合规则与模型安全检查，可结构化控制代理行为，推动更精准的模型鲁棒性与人机协作研究，同时指出现有基准测试易产生虚假安全感。
Scalable AI-assisted Workflow Management for Detector Design Optimization Using Distributed Computing
Authors: Derek Anderson, Amit Bashyal, Markus Diefenthaler, Cristiano Fanelli, Wen Guan, Tanja Horn, Alex Jentsch Meifeng Lin, Tadashi Maeno, Kei Nagai, Hemalata Nayak, Connor Pecar, Karthik Suresh, Fang-Ying Tsai, Anselm Vossen, Tianle Wang, Torre Wenaus
First: 2026-03-31T17:11:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:11:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Production and Distributed Analysis (PanDA) system, originally developed for the ATLAS experiment at the CERN Large Hadron Collider (LHC), has evolved into a robust platform for orchestrating large-scale workflows across distributed computing resources. Coupled with its intelligent Distributed Dispatch and Scheduling (iDDS) component, PanDA supports AI/ML-driven workflows through a scalable and flexible workflow engine. We present an AI-assisted framework for detector design optimization that integrates multi-objective Bayesian optimization with the PanDA--iDDS workflow engine to coordinate iterative simulations across heterogeneous resources. The framework addresses the challenge of exploring high-dimensional parameter spaces inherent in modern detector design. We demonstrate the framework using benchmark problems and realistic studies of the ePIC and dRICH detectors for the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). Results show improved automation, scalability, and efficiency in multi-objective optimization. This work establishes a flexible and extensible paradigm for AI-driven detector design and other computationally intensive scientific applications.

ReDiPrune: Relevance-Diversity Pre-Projection Token Pruning for Efficient Multimodal LLMs
Authors: An Yu, Ting Yu Tsai, Zhenfei Zhang, Weiheng Lu, Felix X. -F. Ye, Ming-Ching Chang
First: 2026-03-25T18:01:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:09:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent multimodal large language models are computationally expensive because Transformers must process a large number of visual tokens. We present ReDiPrune, a training-free token pruning method applied before the vision-language projector, where visual features remain rich and discriminative. Unlike post-projection pruning methods that operate on compressed representations, ReDiPrune selects informative tokens directly from vision encoder outputs, preserving fine-grained spatial and semantic cues. Each token is scored by a lightweight rule that jointly consider text-conditioned relevance and max-min diversity, ensuring the selected tokens are both query-relevant and non-redundant. ReDiPrune is fully plug-and-play, requiring no retraining or architectural modifications, and can be seamlessly inserted between the encoder and projector. Across four video and five image benchmarks, it consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. For example, on EgoSchema with LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B, retaining only 15% of visual tokens yields a +2.0% absolute accuracy gain while reducing computation by more than $6\times$ in TFLOPs. Code is available at https://github.com/UA-CVML/ReDiPrune.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReDiPrune：面向高效多模态大语言模型的关联-多样性预投影令牌剪枝
近期多模态大语言模型因Transformer需处理大量视觉令牌而计算成本高昂。我们提出ReDiPrune，一种训练免开销的令牌剪枝方法，应用于视觉-语言投影器之前，视觉特征保持丰富且具判别性。与在压缩表示上操作的后投影剪枝方法不同，ReDiPrune直接从视觉编码器输出中选择信息性令牌，保留细粒度空间与语义线索。每个令牌由轻量级规则评分，综合考虑文本条件相关性与最大-最小多样性，确保所选令牌既相关又无冗余。ReDiPrune完全即插即用，无需重训练或架构修改，可无缝插入编码器与投影器之间。在四个视频与五个图像基准测试中，其持续优化准确率-效率权衡。例如，在EgoSchema上使用LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B时，仅保留15%视觉令牌即可提升绝对准确率2.0%，同时计算量降低6倍以上。代码见https://github.com/UA-CVML/ReDiPrune。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational inefficiency of multimodal LLMs caused by processing large numbers of visual tokens, ReDiPrune introduces a training-free token pruning method applied before the vision-language projector. It selects informative visual tokens directly from encoder outputs using a lightweight scoring rule that balances text-conditioned relevance and max-min diversity, preserving spatial and semantic detail while avoiding redundancy. Evaluated across nine multimodal benchmarks, ReDiPrune consistently enhances the accuracy-efficiency trade-off; for instance, on EgoSchema with LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B, pruning to 15% of tokens boosts accuracy by 2.0% while cutting computation by over 6x in TFLOPs.
为降低多模态大语言模型处理大量视觉token的计算开销，ReDiPrune提出一种无需训练的token剪枝方法，应用于视觉-语言投影器之前。它直接从编码器输出中选择信息量大的视觉token，通过轻量级评分规则兼顾文本条件相关性与最大-最小多样性，保留空间与语义细节。在九个基准测试中表现稳定，例如在EgoSchema上使用LLaVA-NeXT-Video-7B模型时，仅保留15%的视觉token即可提升准确率2.0%，同时计算量降低6倍以上。
DiffusionVL: Translating Any Autoregressive Models into Diffusion Vision Language Models
Authors: Lunbin Zeng, Jingfeng Yao, Bencheng Liao, Hongyuan Tao, Wenyu Liu, Xinggang Wang
First: 2025-12-17T18:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:09:04+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 figures, conference or other essential info
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion-based decoding has recently emerged as an appealing alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation, offering the potential to update multiple tokens in parallel and reduce latency. However, diffusion vision language models (dVLMs) still lag significantly behind mainstream autoregressive vision language models. This is due to the scarcity and weaker performance of base diffusion language models (dLLMs) compared with their autoregressive counterparts. This raises a natural question: Can we build high-performing dVLMs directly from existing powerful AR models, without relying on dLLMs? We propose DiffusionVL, a family of dVLMs obtained by translating pretrained AR models into the diffusion paradigm via an efficient diffusion finetuning procedure that changes the training objective and decoding process while keeping the backbone architecture intact. Through an efficient diffusion finetuning strategy, we successfully adapt AR pretrained models into the diffusion paradigm. This approach yields two key observations: (1) The paradigm shift from AR-based multimodal models to diffusion is remarkably effective. (2) Direct conversion of an AR language model to a dVLM is also feasible, achieving performance comparable to that of the same AR model finetuned with standard autoregressive visual instruction tuning. To enable practical open-ended generation, we further integrate block decoding, which supports arbitrary-length outputs and KV-cache reuse for faster inference. Our experiments demonstrate that despite training with less than 5% of the data required by prior methods, DiffusionVL achieves a comprehensive performance improvement, with a 34.4% gain on the MMMU-Pro (vision) benchmark and 37.5% gain on the MME (Cog.) benchmark, alongside a 2x inference speedup. The model and code are released at https://github.com/hustvl/DiffusionVL.

标题：DiffusionVL：将任意自回归模型转化为扩散视觉语言模型

They propose DiffusionVL, a method that translates pretrained autoregressive models into diffusion-based vision-language models via an efficient finetuning procedure that modifies the training objective and decoding process while preserving the original architecture. Experimental results show that this paradigm shift is highly effective, achieving performance comparable to autoregressive fine-tuning with far less training data—only 5% of prior methods’ data—and delivering a 34.4% gain on MMMU-Pro and 37.5% on MME benchmarks, along with a 2x inference speedup.
为解决扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）因基础扩散语言模型性能较弱而显著落后于自回归模型的问题，本文提出DiffusionVL方法，通过高效扩散微调策略将预训练的自回归模型转换为扩散范式下的视觉语言模型，仅修改训练目标与解码过程，保留原始架构。实验表明，该范式转换效果显著，仅用前人方法所需数据的5%即可实现与标准自回归微调相当的性能，且在MMMU-Pro（视觉）和MME（认知）基准上分别提升34.4%和37.5%，同时通过块解码支持任意长度输出并实现2倍推理加速。
LPNSR: Prior-Enhanced Diffusion Image Super-Resolution via LR-Guided Noise Prediction
Authors: Shuwei Huang, Shizhuo Liu, Zijun Wei
First: 2026-03-22T03:52:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:08:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion-based image super-resolution (SR), which aims to reconstruct high-resolution (HR) images from corresponding low-resolution (LR) observations, faces a fundamental trade-off between inference efficiency and reconstruction quality. The state-of-the-art residual-shifting diffusion framework achieves efficient 4-step inference, yet suffers from severe performance degradation in compact sampling trajectories. This is mainly attributed to two core limitations: the inherent suboptimality of unconstrained random Gaussian noise in intermediate steps, which leads to error accumulation and insufficient LR prior guidance, and the initialization bias caused by naive bicubic upsampling. In this paper, we propose LPNSR, a prior-enhanced efficient diffusion framework to address these issues. We first mathematically derive the closed-form analytical solution of the optimal intermediate noise for the residual-shifting diffusion paradigm, and accordingly design an LR-guided multi-input-aware noise predictor to replace random Gaussian noise, embedding LR structural priors into the reverse process while fully preserving the framework's core efficient residual-shifting mechanism. We further mitigate initial bias with a high-quality pre-upsampling network to optimize the diffusion starting point. With a compact 4-step trajectory, LPNSR can be optimized in an end-to-end manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LPNSR achieves state-of-the-art perceptual performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets, without relying on any large-scale text-to-image priors. The source code of our method can be found at https://github.com/Faze-Hsw/LPNSR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LPNSR：通过低分辨率引导噪声预测的先验增强扩散图像超分辨率
基于扩散的图像超分辨率（SR）旨在从对应的低分辨率（LR）观测重建高分辨率（HR）图像，面临推理效率与重建质量之间的根本权衡。最先进的残差移位扩散框架实现了高效的4步推理，但在紧凑采样轨迹中性能严重下降。这主要归因于两个核心限制：中间步骤中无约束随机高斯噪声的固有次优性，导致误差累积和LR先验引导不足，以及朴素双三次上采样引起的初始化偏差。本文提出LPNSR，一种先验增强的高效扩散框架以解决这些问题。我们首先推导出残差移位扩散范式中最优中间噪声的闭式解析解，并据此设计LR引导的多输入感知噪声预测器，替代随机高斯噪声，将LR结构先验嵌入逆过程，同时完全保留框架的核心高效残差移位机制。我们进一步通过高质量预上采样网络缓解初始偏差，优化扩散起始点。在紧凑的4步轨迹下，LPNSR可端到端优化。大量实验表明，LPNSR在合成与真实数据集上均达到最先进的感知性能，且无需依赖任何大规模文本到图像先验。方法源代码见https://github.com/Faze-Hsw/LPNSR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between efficiency and quality in diffusion-based image super-resolution, this paper proposes LPNSR to enhance performance without increasing inference steps. It introduces a mathematically derived optimal noise predictor guided by low-resolution inputs to replace random Gaussian noise, thereby reducing error accumulation and incorporating structural priors. Additionally, a high-quality pre-upsampling network corrects initialization bias from naive bicubic upsampling. Evaluated on synthetic and real-world datasets, LPNSR achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality in just four steps, outperforming prior methods without requiring text-to-image priors.
为解决扩散模型在图像超分辨率中效率与质量的权衡问题，本文提出LPNSR框架，通过增强先验信息提升重建效果。该方法针对中间噪声不优和初始化偏差两大问题：推导出残差迁移扩散框架的最优中间噪声闭式解，并设计LR引导的多输入感知噪声预测器，将低分辨率结构先验嵌入反向过程；同时引入高质量预上采样网络缓解初始化偏差。在仅用4步推理的条件下，LPNSR在合成与真实数据集上实现最先进感知质量，且无需文本到图像先验。
Balancing Efficiency and Empathy: Healthcare Providers' Perspectives on AI-Supported Workflows for Serious Illness Conversations in the Emergency Department
Authors: Menglin Zhao, Zhuorui Yong, Ruijia Guan, Kai-Wei Chang, Adrian Haimovich, Kei Ouchi, Timothy Bickmore, Zhan Zhang, Bingsheng Yao, Dakuo Wang, Smit Desai
First: 2025-05-30T21:15:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:06:18+00:00
Comments: To appear at ACM CHI'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Serious Illness Conversations (SICs), discussions about values and care preferences for patients with life-threatening illness, rarely occur in Emergency Departments (EDs), despite evidence that early conversations improve care alignment and reduce unnecessary interventions. We interviewed 11 ED providers to identify challenges in SICs and opportunities for technology support, with a focus on AI. Our analysis revealed a four-stage SIC workflow (identification, preparation, conduction, documentation) and barriers at each stage, including fragmented patient information, limited time and space, lack of conversational guidance, and burdensome documentation. Providers expressed interest in AI systems for synthesizing information, supporting real-time conversations, and automating documentation, but emphasized concerns about preserving human connection and clinical autonomy. This tension highlights the need for technologies that enhance efficiency without undermining the interpersonal nature of SICs. We propose design guidelines for ambient and peripheral AI systems to support providers while preserving the essential humanity of these conversations.

Conditional Polarization Guidance for Camouflaged Object Detection
Authors: QIfan Zhang, Hao Wang, Xiangrong Qin, Ruijie Li
First: 2026-03-31T17:03:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:03:06+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to identify targets that are highly blended with their backgrounds. Recent works have shown that the optical characteristics of polarization cues play a significant role in improving camouflaged object detection. However, most existing polarization-based approaches depend on complex visual encoders and fusion mechanisms, leading to increased model complexity and computational overhead, while failing to fully explore how polarization can explicitly guide hierarchical RGB representation learning. To address these limitations, we propose CPGNet, an asymmetric RGB-polarization framework that introduces a conditional polarization guidance mechanism to explicitly regulate RGB feature learning for camouflaged object detection. Specifically, we design a lightweight polarization interaction module that jointly models these complementary cues and generates reliable polarization guidance in a unified manner. Unlike conventional feature fusion strategies, the proposed conditional guidance mechanism dynamically modulates RGB features using polarization priors, enabling the network to focus on subtle discrepancies between camouflaged objects and their backgrounds. Furthermore, we introduce a polarization edge-guided frequency refinement strategy that enhances high-frequency components under polarization constraints, effectively breaking camouflage patterns. Finally, we develop an iterative feedback decoder to perform coarse-to-fine feature calibration and progressively refine camouflage prediction. Extensive experiments on polarization datasets across multiple tasks, along with evaluations on non-polarization datasets, demonstrate that CPGNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

标题：用于伪装目标检测的条件极化引导
伪装目标检测（COD）旨在识别与背景高度融合的目标。近期研究表明，极化线索的光学特性对提升伪装目标检测效果具有重要作用。然而，现有大多数基于极化的方案依赖复杂的视觉编码器和融合机制，导致模型复杂度和计算开销增加，且未能充分探索极化如何显式引导层次化RGB特征学习。为解决上述问题，我们提出CPGNet，一种非对称RGB-极化框架，引入条件极化引导机制，显式调控RGB特征学习以提升伪装目标检测性能。具体而言，我们设计了一个轻量级极化交互模块，联合建模互补线索并统一生成可靠的极化引导。与传统特征融合策略不同，所提出的条件引导机制利用极化先验动态调制RGB特征，使网络聚焦于伪装目标与背景间的细微差异。此外，我们引入极化边缘引导的频域细化策略，在极化约束下增强高频成分，有效打破伪装模式。最后，我们开发了迭代反馈解码器，实现从粗到细的特征校准，并逐步优化伪装预测。在多个任务的极化数据集及非极化数据集上的大量实验表明，CPGNet持续优于现有最先进方法。

Motivated by the potential of polarization cues to enhance camouflaged object detection while avoiding the complexity of existing fusion methods, this work proposes CPGNet, an asymmetric RGB-polarization framework that uses a conditional polarization guidance mechanism to explicitly regulate RGB feature learning. The method introduces a lightweight polarization interaction module to jointly model RGB and polarization cues, dynamically modulating RGB features using polarization priors to highlight subtle object-background discrepancies. It further employs a polarization edge-guided frequency refinement strategy to enhance high-frequency details and an iterative feedback decoder for coarse-to-fine prediction refinement. Experimental results across multiple polarization and non-polarization datasets show that CPGNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in detection accuracy.
为提升伪装目标检测性能并避免现有方法的复杂融合机制，本文提出CPGNet，一种异构RGB-偏振框架，通过条件偏振引导机制显式调控RGB特征学习。该方法设计轻量级偏振交互模块联合建模RGB与偏振信息，利用偏振先验动态调制RGB特征以突出目标与背景的细微差异；并引入偏振边缘引导的频域精炼策略增强高频细节，结合迭代反馈解码器实现由粗到精的预测优化。在多个偏振与非偏振数据集上的实验表明，CPGNet在检测精度上持续优于当前最先进方法。
When Only the Final Text Survives: Implicit Execution Tracing for Multi-Agent Attribution
Authors: Yi Nian, Haosen Cao, Shenzhe Zhu, Henry Peng Zou, Qingqing Luan, Yue Zhao
First: 2026-03-18T07:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T17:01:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When a multi-agent system produces an incorrect or harmful answer, who is accountable if execution logs and agent identifiers are unavailable? In practice, generated content is often detached from its execution environment due to privacy or system boundaries, leaving the final text as the only auditable artifact. Existing attribution methods rely on full execution traces and thus become ineffective in such metadata-deprived settings. We propose Implicit Execution Tracing (IET), a provenance-by-design framework that shifts attribution from post-hoc inference to built-in instrumentation. Instead of reconstructing hidden trajectories, IET embeds agent-specific, key-conditioned statistical signals directly into the token generation process, transforming the output text into a self-verifying execution record. At inference time, we recover a linearized execution trace from the final text via transition-aware statistical scoring. Experiments across diverse multi-agent coordination settings demonstrate that IET achieves accurate segment-level attribution and reliable transition recovery under identity removal, boundary corruption, and privacy-preserving redaction, while maintaining generation quality. These results show that embedding provenance into generation provides a practical and robust foundation for accountability in multi-agent language systems when execution metadata is unavailable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅当最终文本存留时：面向多智能体归因的隐式执行追踪
当多智能体系统生成错误或有害答案时，若执行日志和智能体标识符不可用，应由谁负责？实践中，由于隐私或系统边界限制，生成内容常与执行环境分离，仅剩最终文本作为可审计的唯一产物。现有归因方法依赖完整执行轨迹，因此在元数据缺失环境下失效。我们提出隐式执行追踪（IET），一种设计即溯源的框架，将归因从事后推断转向内置仪器化。IET不重建隐藏轨迹，而是将智能体特异性、关键条件驱动的统计信号直接嵌入到令牌生成过程中，使输出文本成为可自我验证的执行记录。推理时，通过具备状态转移感知的统计评分，从最终文本中恢复线性化执行轨迹。在多种多智能体协作场景的实验表明，IET在身份移除、边界破坏和隐私保护式删改条件下，仍能实现准确的段落级归因与可靠的转移恢复，同时保持生成质量。结果表明，将溯源嵌入生成过程，为执行元数据缺失时多智能体语言系统的问责提供了实用且稳健的基础。
