Motivated by the challenge of attributing responsibility in multi-agent systems when execution logs and agent identities are absent, the paper introduces Implicit Execution Tracing (IET), a framework that embeds agent-specific statistical signals directly into token generation to create self-verifying output text. Instead of reconstructing lost execution traces, IET instruments the generation process to encode provenance implicitly, enabling post-hoc recovery of linearized execution traces via transition-aware scoring from the final text. Experiments across varied coordination tasks show IET achieves accurate segment-level attribution and reliable transition reconstruction even under identity removal, boundary corruption, and redaction, without compromising generation quality.
为了解决多智能体系统在缺乏执行日志时的责任归属难题，本文提出隐式执行追踪（IET）框架，该框架在生成过程中直接嵌入智能体特定的统计信号，使输出文本成为可自我验证的执行记录。无需重建隐藏的执行路径，IET通过关键条件和智能体标识引导生成，从而从最终文本中推断责任归属。实验表明，即使在身份删除、边界破坏和隐私保护式删改等条件下，IET仍能实现精准的段落级归因与可靠的执行轨迹恢复，且不影响生成质量。
Tracking Equivalent Mechanistic Interpretations Across Neural Networks
Authors: Alan Sun, Mariya Toneva
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:57:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:57:52+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 5 figures, ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability (MI) is an emerging framework for interpreting neural networks. Given a task and model, MI aims to discover a succinct algorithmic process, an interpretation, that explains the model's decision process on that task. However, MI is difficult to scale and generalize. This stems in part from two key challenges: there is no precise notion of a valid interpretation; and, generating interpretations is often an ad hoc process. In this paper, we address these challenges by defining and studying the problem of interpretive equivalence: determining whether two different models share a common interpretation, without requiring an explicit description of what that interpretation is. At the core of our approach, we propose and formalize the principle that two interpretations of a model are equivalent if all of their possible implementations are also equivalent. We develop an algorithm to estimate interpretive equivalence and case study its use on Transformer-based models. To analyze our algorithm, we introduce necessary and sufficient conditions for interpretive equivalence based on models' representation similarity. We provide guarantees that simultaneously relate a model's algorithmic interpretations, circuits, and representations. Our framework lays a foundation for the development of more rigorous evaluation methods of MI and automated, generalizable interpretation discovery methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨神经网络的等价机制解释追踪
机制可解释性（MI）是一种新兴的神经网络解释框架。给定一个任务和模型，MI旨在发现一个简洁的算法过程——即解释——以说明模型在该任务上的决策过程。然而，MI难以扩展和泛化，这在一定程度上源于两个关键挑战：尚无对有效解释的精确定义；且生成解释通常是一个随意的过程。本文通过定义并研究解释等价性问题来应对这些挑战：在不需显式描述该解释内容的前提下，判断两个不同模型是否共享同一解释。我们的方法核心是提出并形式化了如下原则：若两个模型的解释在所有可能实现中均等价，则这两个解释等价。我们开发了一种算法以估计解释等价性，并在基于Transformer的模型上进行了案例研究。为分析该算法，我们基于模型表征相似性引入了解释等价性的必要且充分条件。我们提供了同时关联模型算法解释、电路与表征的保证。本框架为MI更严谨的评估方法及自动化、可泛化的解释发现方法的发展奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling and generalizing mechanistic interpretability due to ambiguous interpretation definitions and ad hoc generation methods, this paper introduces interpretive equivalence — a formal framework to determine if two models share a common interpretation without explicitly describing it. The core method defines equivalence based on the equivalence of all possible implementations of interpretations and develops an algorithm to estimate this equivalence using representation similarity. Experimental case studies on Transformer models demonstrate that the framework successfully identifies equivalent interpretations across different architectures and provides theoretical guarantees linking algorithmic interpretations, circuit structures, and representations.
为了解决机制可解释性难以扩展和泛化的问题——这源于解释定义模糊和生成过程随意——本文提出了“解释等价”概念，即在不显式定义解释内容的前提下判断两个模型是否共享同一解释。核心思想是，若两个解释的所有实现方式等价，则它们本身等价，作者据此设计了基于表征相似度的算法来估计等价性。在Transformer模型上的案例研究表明，该方法能有效识别不同架构模型间的等价解释，并提供理论保证，将算法解释、电路结构与表征联系起来，为机制可解释性的严谨评估与自动化发现奠定了基础。
Phyelds: A Pythonic Framework for Aggregate Computing
Authors: Gianluca Aguzzi, Davide Domini, Nicolas Farabegoli, Mirko Viroli
First: 2026-03-31T16:57:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:57:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aggregate programming is a field-based coordination paradigm with over a decade of exploration and successful applications across domains including sensor networks, robotics, and IoT, with implementations in various programming languages, such as Protelis, ScaFi (Scala), and FCPP (C++). A recent research direction integrates machine learning with aggregate computing, aiming to support large-scale distributed learning and provide new abstractions for implementing learning algorithms. However, existing implementations do not target data science practitioners, who predominantly work in Python--the de facto language for data science and machine learning, with a rich and mature ecosystem. Python also offers advantages for other use cases, such as education and robotics (e.g., via ROS). To address this gap, we present Phyelds, a Python library for aggregate programming. Phyelds offers a fully featured yet lightweight implementation of the field calculus model of computation, featuring a Pythonic API and an architecture designed for seamless integration with Python's machine learning ecosystem. We describe the design and implementation of Phyelds and illustrate its versatility across domains, from well-known aggregate computing patterns to federated learning coordination and integration with a widely used multi-agent reinforcement learning simulator.

为满足数据科学和机器学习领域用户对聚合计算的需求，Phyelds 提供了一个轻量级、Python 风格的框架，实现场演算计算模型。它通过场抽象支持分布式协调，并能无缝集成 Python 的机器学习生态，如 TensorFlow 和 PyTorch。实验结果表明，该框架在传统聚合计算模式、联邦学习及多智能体强化学习仿真等场景中均表现有效，验证了其在实际分布式系统中的广泛适用性与实用性。
Enhancing Structural Mapping with LLM-derived Abstractions for Analogical Reasoning in Narratives
Authors: Mohammadhossein Khojasteh, Yifan Jiang, Stefano De Giorgis, Frank van Harmelen, Filip Ilievski
First: 2026-03-31T16:57:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:57:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analogical reasoning is a key driver of human generalization in problem-solving and argumentation. Yet, analogies between narrative structures remain challenging for machines. Cognitive engines for structural mapping are not directly applicable, as they assume pre-extracted entities, whereas LLMs' performance is sensitive to prompt format and the degree of surface similarity between narratives. This gap motivates a key question: What is the impact of enhancing structural mapping with LLM-derived abstractions on their analogical reasoning ability in narratives? To that end, we propose a modular framework named YARN (Yielding Abstractions for Reasoning in Narratives), which uses LLMs to decompose narratives into units, abstract these units, and then passes them to a mapping component that aligns elements across stories to perform analogical reasoning. We define and operationalize four levels of abstraction that capture both the general meaning of units and their roles in the story, grounded in prior work on framing. Our experiments reveal that abstractions consistently improve model performance, resulting in competitive or better performance than end-to-end LLM baselines. Closer error analysis reveals the remaining challenges in abstraction at the right level, in incorporating implicit causality, and an emerging categorization of analogical patterns in narratives. YARN enables systematic variation of experimental settings to analyze component contributions, and to support future work, we make the code for YARN openly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型生成的抽象增强叙事中的类比推理结构映射
类比推理是人类在解决问题和论证中实现泛化的关键驱动力，但机器在叙事结构间的类比仍具挑战性。现有认知引擎因假设实体已预先提取而无法直接应用，而大语言模型的表现则受提示格式和叙事表面相似度影响。这一差距促使我们提出核心问题：利用大语言模型生成的抽象增强结构映射，对叙事类比推理能力有何影响？为此，我们提出模块化框架YARN（Yielding Abstractions for Reasoning in Narratives），通过大语言模型将叙事分解为单元、抽象这些单元，再传递给映射组件以对齐故事元素并执行类比推理。我们基于先前的框架研究，定义并操作化了四个抽象层级，以捕捉单元的通用语义及其在故事中的角色。实验表明，抽象显著提升模型性能，达到或优于端到端大语言模型基线。深入错误分析揭示了抽象层级选择、隐含因果关系整合及叙事类比模式分类等现存挑战。YARN支持实验设置的系统性变化以分析各组件贡献，为未来研究，我们公开了YARN的代码。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of analogical reasoning in narrative structures, this work investigates how LLM-derived abstractions can enhance structural mapping. The authors propose YARN, a modular framework that uses LLMs to decompose narratives into units, abstract them into four levels based on meaning and narrative role, and then maps these abstractions across stories for analogical reasoning. Experiments show that incorporating abstractions consistently improves performance over end-to-end LLM baselines, while error analysis highlights remaining issues such as insufficient abstraction granularity, lack of implicit causal modeling, and the need for better categorization of narrative analogies.
鉴于机器在处理叙事结构类比推理时受限于表面相似性和提示敏感性，本文提出YARN框架，通过LLM将叙事分解为单元并生成四层级抽象（涵盖语义与叙事角色），再传递给映射模块进行跨故事类比推理。实验表明，抽象显著提升性能，常优于端到端LLM基线；错误分析揭示抽象粒度、隐含因果关系及叙事模式分类仍存挑战。
Unsupervised Modular Adaptive Region Growing and RegionMix Classification for Wind Turbine Segmentation
Authors: Raül Pérez-Gonzalo, Riccardo Magro, Andreas Espersen, Antonio Agudo
Venue: WACV 2026
First: 2026-01-07T16:29:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:57:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted to WACV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable operation of wind turbines requires frequent inspections, as even minor surface damages can degrade aerodynamic performance, reduce energy output, and accelerate blade wear. Central to automating these inspections is the accurate segmentation of turbine blades from visual data. This task is traditionally addressed through dense, pixel-wise deep learning models. However, such methods demand extensive annotated datasets, posing scalability challenges. In this work, we introduce an annotation-efficient segmentation approach that reframes the pixel-level task into a binary region classification problem. Image regions are generated using a fully unsupervised, interpretable Modular Adaptive Region Growing technique, guided by image-specific Adaptive Thresholding and enhanced by a Region Merging process that consolidates fragmented areas into coherent segments. To improve generalization and classification robustness, we introduce RegionMix, an augmentation strategy that synthesizes new training samples by combining distinct regions. Our framework demonstrates state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and strong cross-site generalization by consistently segmenting turbine blades across distinct windfarms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无监督模块自适应区域生长与RegionMix分类用于风力涡轮机分割
风力涡轮机的可靠运行需要频繁检查，因为即使表面微小损伤也会降低气动性能、减少发电量并加速叶片磨损。自动化检查的核心是准确地从视觉数据中分割涡轮机叶片。传统方法依赖密集的像素级深度学习模型，但此类方法需要大量标注数据，存在可扩展性挑战。本文提出一种标注效率高的分割方法，将像素级任务重构为二元区域分类问题。图像区域通过完全无监督、可解释的模块自适应区域生长技术生成，结合图像特定自适应阈值，并通过区域合并过程将碎片化区域整合为连贯的分割段。为提升泛化能力和分类鲁棒性，我们引入RegionMix增强策略，通过组合不同区域合成新的训练样本。该框架在分割精度上达到最先进水平，并在不同风电场间展现出强大的跨站点泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the scalability challenge of annotating large-scale wind turbine inspection datasets, this work proposes an unsupervised segmentation method that transforms pixel-level classification into a binary region classification task. It employs a Modular Adaptive Region Growing algorithm to generate image regions using adaptive thresholding and region merging to resolve fragmentation. To enhance generalization, RegionMix synthesizes training samples by combining distinct regions. Experimental results show the method achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and robust cross-site generalization across diverse windfarms.
为降低风力涡轮机叶片分割任务对标注数据的依赖，该研究将像素级分割重构为二分类区域问题。采用无监督的模块化自适应区域生长方法生成图像区域，结合自适应阈值与区域合并策略以整合碎片化区域。为提升泛化能力，引入RegionMix数据增强技术，通过组合不同区域合成新训练样本。实验表明该方法在分割精度上达到当前最优，并在不同风电场间展现出强泛化能力。
Understanding and Improving Shampoo and SOAP via Kullback-Leibler Minimization
Authors: Wu Lin, Scott C. Lowe, Felix Dangel, Runa Eschenhagen, Zikun Xu, Roger B. Grosse
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-03T14:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:56:27+00:00
Comments: an extended version of the ICLR 2026 paper (added a sentence about viewing KL-Shampoo from a gradient orthogonalization viewpoint)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Shampoo and its efficient variant, SOAP, employ structured second-moment estimations and have shown strong performance for training neural networks (NNs). In practice, however, Shampoo typically requires step-size grafting with Adam to be competitive, and SOAP mitigates this by applying Adam in Shampoo's eigenbasis -- at the cost of additional memory overhead from Adam in both methods. Prior analyses have largely relied on the Frobenius norm to motivate these estimation schemes. We instead recast their estimation procedures as covariance estimation under Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence minimization, revealing a previously overlooked theoretical limitation and motivating principled redesigns. Building on this perspective, we develop $\textbf{KL-Shampoo}$ and $\textbf{KL-SOAP}$, practical schemes that match or exceed the performance of Shampoo and SOAP in NN pre-training while achieving SOAP-level per-iteration runtime. Notably, KL-Shampoo does not rely on Adam to attain competitive performance, eliminating the memory overhead introduced by Adam. Across our experiments, KL-Shampoo consistently outperforms SOAP, Shampoo, and even KL-SOAP, establishing the KL-based approach as a promising foundation for designing structured methods in NN optimization. An implementation of KL-Shampoo/KL-SOAP is available at https://github.com/yorkerlin/KL-Methods
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过KL散度最小化理解并改进Shampoo和SOAP
Shampoo及其高效变体SOAP采用结构化的二阶矩估计，在训练神经网络（NN）方面表现强劲。然而，实践中Shampoo通常需与Adam结合步长调整才能具备竞争力，而SOAP通过在Shampoo的特征基上应用Adam来缓解此问题——但两种方法均需额外的Adam内存开销。此前分析多依赖Frobenius范数来论证这些估计方案。我们转而将它们的估计过程重新表述为KL散度最小化下的协方差估计，揭示了此前被忽视的理论局限性，并推动了原理性的重新设计。基于此视角，我们提出了实用方案KL-Shampoo和KL-SOAP，在NN预训练中性能可匹配或超越Shampoo和SOAP，且达到SOAP级别的每迭代运行时。值得注意的是，KL-Shampoo无需依赖Adam即可实现竞争力表现，消除了Adam引入的内存开销。在我们的实验中，KL-Shampoo持续优于SOAP、Shampoo，甚至优于KL-SOAP，确立了KL方法作为设计NN优化结构化方法的有前景基础。KL-Shampoo/KL-SOAP的实现见https://github.com/yorkerlin/KL-Methods
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Shampoo and SOAP, while effective for neural network training, rely on Adam for competitive performance and incur memory overhead, this work reinterprets their second-moment estimation as covariance estimation under Kullback-Leibler divergence minimization, revealing a theoretical gap in prior Frobenius-norm-based analyses. Based on this insight, the authors propose KL-Shampoo and KL-SOAP, which achieve or surpass Shampoo and SOAP performance in neural network pre-training while matching SOAP’s per-iteration runtime; notably, KL-Shampoo eliminates the need for Adam, thereby removing its associated memory cost. Experimental results show KL-Shampoo consistently outperforms both Shampoo and SOAP, including KL-SOAP, demonstrating the KL-divergence framework as a more effective foundation for designing structured optimization methods.
该研究受现有基于Frobenius范数分析的局限性启发，将Shampoo和SOAP的二阶矩估计重新诠释为KL散度最小化下的协方差估计，揭示了其设计中的理论盲区。在此基础上，作者提出KL-Shampoo和KL-SOAP两种新方法，其性能不亚于甚至优于原方法，且达到SOAP的每轮运行效率；特别地，KL-Shampoo无需依赖Adam，消除了其内存开销。实验表明，KL-Shampoo在多个神经网络预训练任务中持续优于Shampoo、SOAP乃至KL-SOAP，证明KL框架是设计结构化优化方法的更优基础。
Extending MONA in Camera Dropbox: Reproduction, Learned Approval, and Design Implications for Reward-Hacking Mitigation
Authors: Nathan Heath
First: 2026-03-31T16:56:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:56:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Myopic Optimization with Non-myopic Approval (MONA) mitigates multi-step reward hacking by restricting the agent's planning horizon while supplying far-sighted approval as a training signal~\cite{farquhar2025mona}. The original paper identifies a critical open question: how the method of constructing approval -- particularly the degree to which approval depends on achieved outcomes -- affects whether MONA's safety guarantees hold. We present a reproduction-first extension of the public MONA Camera Dropbox environment that (i)~repackages the released codebase as a standard Python project with scripted PPO training, (ii)~confirms the published contrast between ordinary RL (91.5\% reward-hacking rate) and oracle MONA (0.0\% hacking rate) using the released reference arrays, and (iii)~introduces a modular learned-approval suite spanning oracle, noisy, misspecified, learned, and calibrated approval mechanisms. In reduced-budget pilot sweeps across approval methods, horizons, dataset sizes, and calibration strategies, the best calibrated learned-overseer run achieves zero observed reward hacking but substantially lower intended-behavior rates than oracle MONA (11.9\% vs.\ 99.9\%), consistent with under-optimization rather than re-emergent hacking. These results operationalize the MONA paper's approval-spectrum conjecture as a runnable experimental object and suggest that the central engineering challenge shifts from proving MONA's concept to building learned approval models that preserve sufficient foresight without reopening reward-hacking channels. Code, configurations, and reproduction commands are publicly available. https://github.com/codernate92/mona-camera-dropbox-repro
中文标题/摘要
标题：在相机存取箱环境中扩展MONA：再现、学习型批准及奖励滥用缓解的设计启示
Myopic Optimization with Non-myopic Approval（MONA）通过限制智能体的规划时间范围并提供远见型批准作为训练信号，缓解多步奖励滥用问题~\cite{farquhar2025mona}。原论文提出一个关键开放问题：批准的构建方式——特别是批准对达成结果的依赖程度——如何影响MONA的安全性保障。我们对公开的MONA相机存取箱环境进行了首次再现性扩展：（i）将发布代码库重新封装为标准Python项目并支持脚本化PPO训练；（ii）使用发布参考数组验证了普通强化学习（91.5%奖励滥用率）与oracle MONA（0.0%滥用率）之间的对比；（iii）引入一套模块化学习型批准机制，涵盖oracle、噪声、误指定、学习型及校准型批准方式。在预算受限的初步实验中，针对不同批准方法、规划时长、数据集规模和校准策略，表现最佳的校准学习型监督者运行实现了零观测到的奖励滥用，但其预期行为率显著低于oracle MONA（11.9% vs. 99.9%），符合优化不足而非重新出现滥用的特征。这些结果将MONA论文中的批准光谱猜想转化为可运行的实验对象，并表明核心工程挑战已从证明MONA概念转向构建保留足够远见且不重新开启奖励滥用通道的学习型批准模型。代码、配置及再现命令均已公开。https://github.com/codernate92/mona-camera-dropbox-repro
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to empirically validate and extend the MONA framework’s safety guarantees, this work reproduces and extends the Camera Dropbox environment, confirming the original claim that oracle MONA eliminates reward hacking while standard RL fails. The study introduces a modular suite of learned approval mechanisms—oracle, noisy, misspecified, learned, and calibrated—to test how approval construction affects safety. Experimental results show that the best calibrated learned overseer achieves zero observed reward hacking but significantly lower intended-behavior rates (11.9% vs. 99.9%) compared to oracle MONA, suggesting under-optimization rather than re-emergent hacking. This confirms the approval-spectrum conjecture as an experimentally tractable concept and shifts the engineering focus to designing learned approval models that maintain foresight without reintroducing reward hacking.
为验证并扩展MONA的安全性保障，本研究复现并扩展了MONA相机存取环境，确认了原始论文中“oracle MONA可消除奖励黑客行为”而标准强化学习失败的结论。研究引入了模块化的学习型审批机制，测试了包括噪声、误标和校准型审批等变体。实验结果显示，最优校准的学习型监督者实现了零奖励黑客行为，但其目标行为率（11.9%）远低于oracle MONA（99.9%），表明是优化不足而非黑客行为重现。这说明MONA落地的核心挑战已从理论证明转向工程实现——如何构建保留前瞻能力又不重开奖励黑客通道的学习型审批模型。
SceneDiff: A Benchmark and Method for Multiview Object Change Detection
Authors: Yuqun Wu, Chih-hao Lin, Henry Che, Aditi Tiwari, Chuhang Zou, Shenlong Wang, Derek Hoiem
First: 2025-12-18T18:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:55:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We investigate the problem of identifying objects that have been added, removed, or moved between a pair of captures (images or videos) of the same scene at different times. Accurately identifying verifiable changes is extremely challenging -- some objects may appear to be missing because they are occluded or out of frame, while others may appear different due to large viewpoint changes. To study this problem, we introduce the SceneDiff Benchmark, the first multiview change detection dataset for scenes captured along different camera trajectories, comprising 350 diverse video pairs with dense object instance-level annotations. We also introduce the SceneDiff algorithm, a training-free approach that solves for image poses, segments images into objects, and compares them using semantic and geometric features. By building on pretrained models, SceneDiff generalizes across domains without retraining and naturally improves as the underlying models advance. Experiments on multiview and two-view benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches by large margins (53.0\% and 30.6\% relative AP improvements). Project page: https://yuqunw.github.io/SceneDiff
中文标题/摘要
标题：SceneDiff：多视角物体变化检测的基准与方法
我们研究了在不同时间拍摄的同一场景的两组捕获（图像或视频）之间，识别被添加、移除或移动的物体的问题。准确识别可验证的变化极具挑战——某些物体可能因被遮挡或超出画面而看似消失，而另一些物体则因视角大幅变化而显得不同。为研究该问题，我们引入了SceneDiff基准数据集，这是首个针对不同相机轨迹拍摄场景的多视角变化检测数据集，包含350对具有密集物体实例级标注的视频。我们还提出了SceneDiff算法，一种无需训练的方法，可求解图像姿态、将图像分割为物体，并利用语义和几何特征进行比较。通过构建在预训练模型之上，SceneDiff无需重新训练即可跨领域泛化，并随着底层模型的进步自然提升性能。在多视角和双视角基准上的实验表明，我们的方法相较现有方法大幅领先（相对AP提升53.0%和30.6%）。项目页面：https://yuqunw.github.io/SceneDiff
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately detecting object changes between multiview captures of the same scene, where occlusion and viewpoint shifts complicate verification, the authors introduce SceneDiff, a training-free method that estimates image poses, segments objects, and compares them using semantic and geometric features. Built on pretrained models, it generalizes across domains without retraining. Evaluated on the newly created SceneDiff Benchmark — the first multiview change detection dataset with 350 video pairs and dense object annotations — the method achieves significant performance gains over existing approaches, improving average precision by 53.0% on multiview and 30.6% on two-view benchmarks.
为解决因遮挡和视角变化导致的多视角场景中物体变化检测难题，作者提出了SceneDiff方法，该方法无需训练，通过估计图像姿态、分割物体并结合语义与几何特征进行比较，利用预训练模型实现跨域泛化。在首个多视角变化检测数据集SceneDiff Benchmark（含350组视频对与密集实例级标注）上评估，该方法在多视角和双视角基准上分别比现有方法提升53.0%和30.6%的平均精度。
SurgNavAR: An Augmented Reality Surgical Navigation Framework for Optical See-Through Head Mounted Displays
Authors: Abdullah Thabit, Mohamed Benmahdjoub, Rafiuddin Jinabade, Hizirwan S. Salim, Marie-Lise C. van Veelen, Mark G. van Vledder, Eppo B. Wolvius, Theo van Walsum
First: 2026-03-31T16:54:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:54:30+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Augmented reality (AR) devices with head mounted displays (HMDs) facilitate the direct superimposition of 3D preoperative imaging data onto the patient during surgery. To use an HMD-AR device as a stand-alone surgical navigation system, the device should be able to locate the patient and surgical instruments, align preoperative imaging data with the patient, and visualize navigation data in real time during surgery. Whereas some of the technologies required for this are known, integration in such devices is cumbersome and requires specific knowledge and expertise, hampering scientific progress in this field. This work therefore aims to present and evaluate an integrated HMD-based AR surgical navigation framework that is adaptable to diverse surgical applications. The framework tracks 2D patterns as reference markers attached to the patient and surgical instruments. It allows for the calibration of surgical tools using pivot and reference-based calibration techniques. It enables image-to-patient registration using point-based matching and manual positioning. The integrated functionalities of the framework are evaluated on two HMD devices, the HoloLens 2 and Magic Leap 2, with two surgical use cases being evaluated in a phantom setup: AR-guided needle insertion and rib fracture localization. The framework was able to achieve a mean tooltip calibration accuracy of 1 mm, a registration accuracy of 3 mm, and a targeting accuracy below 5 mm on the two surgical use cases. The framework presents an easy-to-use configurable tool for HMD-based AR surgical navigation, which can be extended and adapted to many surgical applications. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/abdullahthabit/SurgNavAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SurgNavAR：面向光学透视式头戴显示器的增强现实手术导航框架
配备头戴显示器（HMD）的增强现实（AR）设备可在手术过程中直接将三维术前影像数据叠加在患者体表。要将HMD-AR设备作为独立的手术导航系统，该设备需能定位患者与手术器械、将术前影像数据与患者对齐，并在手术中实时可视化导航数据。尽管部分所需技术已知，但其在设备中的集成复杂且需特定知识与技能，阻碍了该领域科学进展。本研究旨在提出并评估一个可适配多种手术应用的集成式HMD-AR手术导航框架。该框架通过追踪附着于患者与手术器械上的二维标记图案作为参考标记，支持基于枢轴与参考点的手术器械校准，支持基于点匹配与手动定位的影像-患者配准。该框架在两款HMD设备（HoloLens 2与Magic Leap 2）上进行了功能评估，通过仿体实验验证了两个手术应用场景：AR引导针插入与肋骨骨折定位。框架在两个手术场景中分别实现了平均工具尖端校准精度1mm、配准精度3mm、靶向精度低于5mm。该框架提供了一个易用且可配置的HMD-AR手术导航工具，可扩展至多种手术应用。框架开源代码见https://github.com/abdullahthabit/SurgNavAR。

Evaluated on HoloLens 2 and Magic Leap 2 in phantom surgical tasks including needle insertion and rib fracture localization, the framework achieved mean tooltip calibration accuracy of 1 mm, registration accuracy of 3 mm, and targeting accuracy below 5 mm, demonstrating its practical viability and adaptability across diverse surgical contexts.
为解决光学透视式头戴显示器在手术导航中集成复杂、使用门槛高的问题，该研究提出SurgNavAR框架，整合患者与器械追踪、影像配准及实时可视化功能。系统通过2D标记物实现追踪，采用枢轴与参考点校准工具，利用点匹配完成影像与患者定位。在HoloLens 2与Magic Leap 2设备上，针对针插入与肋骨骨折定位两项模拟手术任务，系统平均器械标定精度达1毫米，配准精度3毫米，靶向精度低于5毫米，验证了其在手术导航中的实用精度与可扩展性。
Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb: Improving German-language LLM pre-training with model-based data curation and synthetic data generation
Authors: Thomas F Burns, Letitia Parcalabescu, Stephan Wäldchen, Michael Barlow, Gregor Ziegltrum, Volker Stampa, Bastian Harren, Björn Deiseroth
Venue: EACL 2026, volume 1, pages 1267-1283
First: 2025-04-24T17:23:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:49:26+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 3 figures; published at EACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling data quantity is essential for large language models (LLMs), yet recent findings show that data quality can significantly boost performance and training efficiency. We introduce a German-language dataset curation pipeline that combines heuristic and model-based filtering techniques with synthetic data generation. We use our pipeline to create Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb, a 628B-word German pre-training dataset composed of three subsets drawing from: (1) Common Crawl web data (organic subset; 78B words), (2) FineWeb2 (organic subset; 235B), and (3) synthetically-generated data conditioned on actual, organic web data (synthetic subset; 329B). We evaluate our dataset by pre-training both a 1B Llama-style model and an 8B tokeniser-free hierarchical autoregressive transformer (HAT) from scratch. A comparison on German-language benchmarks, including MMMLU, shows significant performance gains of Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb over FineWeb2 alone. This advantage holds at the 8B scale even when FineWeb2 is enriched by human-curated high-quality data sources such as Wikipedia. Our findings support the growing body of evidence that model-based data curation and synthetic data generation can significantly enhance LLM pre-training datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb：通过基于模型的数据筛选与合成数据生成提升德语大语言模型预训练
扩大数据量对大语言模型（LLM）至关重要，但近期研究表明，数据质量可显著提升性能与训练效率。我们提出一种德语数据筛选流程，结合启发式与基于模型的过滤技术及合成数据生成。我们利用该流程构建了Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb，一个由三部分组成的6280亿词德语预训练数据集：（1）Common Crawl网页数据（有机子集；780亿词），（2）FineWeb2（有机子集；2350亿词），（3）基于真实有机网页数据生成的合成数据（合成子集；3290亿词）。我们通过从零开始预训练10亿参数的Llama风格模型和80亿参数的无编码器分层自回归Transformer（HAT）来评估该数据集。在包括MMMLU在内的德语基准测试中，Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb相比FineWeb2显著提升性能。即使在FineWeb2中加入人类精选的高质量数据源（如维基百科），该优势在80亿参数规模下依然存在。我们的研究支持了越来越多的证据，即基于模型的数据筛选与合成数据生成可显著增强LLM预训练数据集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that data quality can outperform mere quantity in LLM pre-training, the authors develop a German-language dataset pipeline combining model-based filtering and synthetic data generation to create Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb, a 628B-word corpus. This dataset integrates organic web data from Common Crawl and FineWeb2 with synthetically generated content conditioned on real data. Pre-training a 1B Llama-style model and an 8B tokeniser-free HAT model from scratch on this dataset yields significant performance gains on German benchmarks like MMMLU, surpassing FineWeb2 even when the latter is augmented with high-quality human-curated sources such as Wikipedia.
为提升德语大语言模型性能，作者提出Aleph-Alpha-GermanWeb数据集，通过结合启发式与模型驱动的数据筛选及合成数据生成方法构建，总规模达6280亿词。该数据集由三部分组成：来自Common Crawl的780亿词、来自FineWeb2的2350亿词，以及基于真实网页数据生成的3290亿合成词。在该数据集上从零开始预训练10亿参数Llama风格模型和80亿参数无分词器层级自回归Transformer模型，在德语基准测试（如MMMLU）中表现显著优于仅使用FineWeb2的数据，甚至在80亿参数规模下超越了FineWeb2叠加人工精选高质量数据（如维基百科）的效果。
SIMPACT: Simulation-Enabled Action Planning using Vision-Language Models
Authors: Haowen Liu, Shaoxiong Yao, Haonan Chen, Jiawei Gao, Jiayuan Mao, Jia-Bin Huang, Yilun Du
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-05T18:51:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:48:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026; camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit remarkable common-sense and semantic reasoning capabilities. However, they lack a grounded understanding of physical dynamics. This limitation arises from training VLMs on static internet-scale visual-language data that contain no causal interactions or action-conditioned changes. Consequently, it remains challenging to leverage VLMs for fine-grained robotic manipulation tasks that require physical understanding, reasoning, and corresponding action planning. To overcome this, we present SIMPACT, a test-time, SIMulation-enabled ACTion Planning framework that equips VLMs with physical reasoning through simulation-in-the-loop world modeling, without requiring any additional training. From a single RGB-D observation, SIMPACT efficiently constructs physics simulations, enabling the VLM to propose informed actions, observe simulated rollouts, and iteratively refine its reasoning. By integrating language reasoning with physics prediction, our simulation-enabled VLM can understand contact dynamics and action outcomes in a physically grounded way. Our method demonstrates state-of-the-art performance on five challenging, real-world rigid-body and deformable manipulation tasks that require fine-grained physical reasoning, outperforming existing general-purpose robotic manipulation models. Our results demonstrate that embedding physics understanding via efficient simulation into VLM reasoning at test time offers a promising path towards generalizable embodied intelligence. Project webpage can be found at https://simpact-bot.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIMPACT：基于视觉-语言模型的仿真赋能动作规划
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）展现出卓越的常识和语义推理能力，但缺乏对物理动态的具身理解。这一局限源于VLMs在静态的互联网规模视觉-语言数据上训练，这些数据不包含因果交互或动作条件变化。因此，利用VLMs处理需要物理理解、推理和对应动作规划的细粒度机器人操作任务仍具挑战性。为解决此问题，我们提出SIMPACT，一种测试时仿真赋能的动作规划框架，通过循环仿真世界建模赋予VLM物理推理能力，无需额外训练。从单帧RGB-D观测出发，SIMPACT高效构建物理仿真，使VLM提出有依据的动作、观察仿真回放并迭代优化推理。通过融合语言推理与物理预测，我们的仿真赋能VLM可具身化理解接触动态与动作结果。该方法在五个需细粒度物理推理的现实世界刚体与可变形操作任务上达到最先进性能，超越现有通用机器人操作模型。结果表明，测试时将物理理解通过高效仿真嵌入VLM推理，是迈向可泛化的具身智能的有前景路径。项目网页：https://simpact-bot.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of physical grounding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which limits their ability to perform fine-grained robotic manipulation, SIMPACT introduces a test-time framework that integrates simulation into VLM reasoning without additional training. It constructs physics-based simulations from single RGB-D observations, enabling the model to propose actions, simulate their outcomes, and iteratively refine its plans. Experimental results show SIMPACT achieves state-of-the-art performance across five real-world manipulation tasks involving rigid and deformable objects, outperforming existing general-purpose robotic models by leveraging physics-aware reasoning through simulation-in-the-loop.
由于视觉语言模型（VLMs）缺乏物理世界的理解能力，难以胜任需要精细物理推理的机器人操作任务，作者提出了SIMPACT框架，该框架在测试时通过引入物理仿真增强VLM的推理能力，无需额外训练。给定单帧RGB-D图像，SIMPACT构建物理仿真环境，使VLM能提出动作、观察模拟结果并迭代优化决策。实验表明，该方法在五个涉及刚体与柔性物体的现实世界操作任务中达到当前最优性能，显著优于现有通用机器人操作模型，验证了仿真驱动的物理推理在具身智能中的有效性。
Aligning Validation with Deployment: Target-Weighted Cross-Validation for Spatial Prediction
Authors: Alexander Brenning, Thomas Suesse
First: 2026-03-31T16:44:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:44:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-validation (CV) is commonly used to estimate predictive risk when independent test data are unavailable. Its validity depends on the assumption that validation tasks are sampled from the same distribution as prediction tasks encountered during deployment. In spatial prediction and other settings with structured data, this assumption is frequently violated, leading to biased estimates of deployment risk. We propose Target-Weighted CV (TWCV), an estimator of deployment risk that accounts for discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions, thus accounting for (1) covariate shift and (2) task-difficulty shift. We characterize prediction tasks by descriptors such as covariates and spatial configuration. TWCV assigns weights to validation losses such that the weighted empirical distribution of validation tasks matches the corresponding distribution over a target domain. The weights are obtained via calibration weighting, yielding an importance-weighted estimator that targets deployment risk. Since TWCV requires adequate coverage of the deployment distribution's support, we combine it with spatially buffered resampling that diversifies the task difficulty distribution. In a simulation study, conventional as well as spatial estimators exhibit substantial bias depending on sampling, whereas buffered TWCV remains approximately unbiased across scenarios. A case study in environmental pollution mapping further confirms that discrepancies between validation and deployment task distributions can affect performance assessment, and that buffered TWCV better reflects the prediction task over the target domain. These results establish task distribution mismatch as a primary source of CV bias in spatial prediction and show that calibration weighting combined with a suitable validation task generator provides a viable approach to estimating predictive risk under dataset shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将验证与部署对齐：面向空间预测的目标加权交叉验证
当无独立测试数据时，交叉验证（CV）常用于估计预测风险。其有效性依赖于验证任务与部署期间遇到的预测任务来自同一分布的假设。在空间预测及其他结构化数据场景中，该假设常被违反，导致部署风险估计偏倚。我们提出目标加权交叉验证（TWCV），一种考虑验证与部署任务分布差异的部署风险估计器，从而处理（1）协变量偏移和（2）任务难度偏移。我们通过协变量与空间配置等描述符刻画预测任务。TWCV为验证损失分配权重，使加权经验分布与目标域上的对应分布匹配。权重通过校准加权获得，形成目标部署风险的重要性加权估计器。由于TWCV需覆盖部署分布的支持域，我们结合空间缓冲重采样以多样化任务难度分布。模拟研究表明，传统及空间估计器在不同采样下存在显著偏倚，而缓冲TWCV在各场景下近似无偏。环境污染物制图案例研究进一步证实，验证与部署任务分布差异会影响性能评估，缓冲TWCV更准确反映目标域上的预测任务。结果表明，任务分布不匹配是空间预测中CV偏倚的主要来源，校准加权结合合适的验证任务生成器是应对数据集偏移下预测风险估计的可行方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the bias in cross-validation due to mismatches between validation and deployment task distributions in spatial prediction, the paper introduces Target-Weighted Cross-Validation (TWCV), which assigns weights to validation losses to align their empirical distribution with the target deployment domain, correcting for covariate and task-difficulty shifts. TWCV uses calibration weighting to derive importance weights and combines with spatially buffered resampling to ensure adequate coverage of the deployment distribution. Experimental results show that conventional CV methods exhibit substantial bias depending on sampling, while buffered TWCV remains approximately unbiased across simulation scenarios and performs better in a real-world environmental pollution mapping case study.
为解决空间预测中验证与部署任务分布不一致导致的交叉验证偏差问题，本文提出目标加权交叉验证（TWCV），通过为验证损失分配权重，使验证任务的经验分布逼近目标域分布，以校正协变量与任务难度偏移。TWCV采用校准加权计算重要性权重，并结合空间缓冲重采样以确保覆盖部署分布支持。实验表明，传统交叉验证方法在不同采样场景下偏差显著，而缓冲TWCV在模拟与环境污染物制图案例中均保持近似无偏，有效评估了分布偏移下的预测风险。
From Moments to Models: Graphon-Mixture Learning for Mixup and Contrastive Learning
Authors: Ali Azizpour, Reza Ramezanpour, Santiago Segarra
First: 2025-10-04T06:03:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:42:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world graph datasets often arise from mixtures of populations, where graphs are generated by multiple distinct underlying distributions. In this work, we propose a unified framework that explicitly models graph data as a mixture of probabilistic graph generative models represented by graphons. To characterize and estimate these graphons, we leverage graph moments (motif densities) to cluster graphs generated from the same underlying model. We establish a novel theoretical guarantee, deriving a tighter bound showing that graphs sampled from structurally similar graphons exhibit similar motif densities with high probability. This result enables principled estimation of graphon mixture components. We show how incorporating estimated graphon mixture components enhances two widely used downstream paradigms: graph data augmentation via mixup and graph contrastive learning. By conditioning these methods on the underlying generative models, we develop graphon-mixture-aware mixup (GMAM) and model-aware graph contrastive learning (MGCL). Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate strong empirical performance. In supervised learning, GMAM outperforms existing augmentation strategies, achieving new state-of-the-art accuracy on 6 out of 7 datasets. In unsupervised learning, MGCL performs competitively across seven benchmark datasets and achieves the lowest average rank overall.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从瞬间到模型：用于Mixup与对比学习的图翁混合学习
现实世界中的图数据常源于多个群体的混合，其中图由多个不同的底层分布生成。本文提出一个统一框架，显式地将图数据建模为由图翁表示的概率图生成模型的混合。为刻画和估计这些图翁，我们利用图矩（图模式密度）对来自相同底层模型生成的图进行聚类。我们建立了一个新的理论保证，推导出更紧的界限，表明结构相似的图翁所生成的图在高概率下具有相似的图模式密度。这一结果使得图翁混合成分的原理性估计成为可能。我们展示了如何将估计的图翁混合成分融入两种广泛使用的下游范式：图数据增强（Mixup）和图对比学习。通过将这些方法基于底层生成模型进行条件化，我们开发了图翁混合感知的Mixup（GMAM）和模型感知的图对比学习（MGCL）。在模拟和真实数据集上的大量实验表明其强大的实证性能。在监督学习中，GMAM优于现有增强策略，在7个数据集中有6个取得了新的最先进准确率。在无监督学习中，MGCL在七个基准数据集上表现具有竞争力，整体平均排名最低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that real-world graphs often stem from mixtures of distinct generative processes, this work introduces a graphon-mixture framework that models graphs as combinations of probabilistic graph generative models. The method leverages graph moments (motif densities) to cluster graphs sharing similar underlying graphons, and establishes a theoretical guarantee that structurally similar graphons yield similar motif densities. This enables principled estimation of graphon mixture components, which are then used to enhance two downstream tasks: mixup-based data augmentation (via GMAM) and contrastive learning (via MGCL). Experimental results show GMAM achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on six out of seven supervised datasets, while MGCL ranks highest overall across seven unsupervised benchmarks.
鉴于现实世界图数据常由多个不同生成过程混合而成，本文提出一种图翁混合框架，将图建模为多个概率图生成模型的组合。通过图矩（图模式密度）对来自相同底层模型的图进行聚类，并建立理论保证：结构相似的图翁会以高概率产生相似的图模式密度，从而实现混合成分的原理性估计。该估计结果用于增强两个下游任务：图数据增强（GMAM）和图对比学习（MGCL），二者均基于推断出的生成模型进行条件化。实验表明，GMAM在7个监督数据集中有6个达到最先进准确率，MGCL在7个无监督基准中综合排名最高。
Structural Feature Engineering for Generative Engine Optimization: How Content Structure Shapes Citation Behavior
Authors: Junwei Yu, Mufeng Yang, Yepeng Ding, Hiroyuki Sato
First: 2026-03-31T16:41:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:41:43+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures. This paper proposes GEO-SFE, a structural feature engineering framework for generative engine optimization
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The proliferation of AI-powered search engines has shifted information discovery from traditional link-based retrieval to direct answer generation with selective source citation, creating new challenges for content visibility. While existing Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) approaches focus primarily on semantic content modification, the role of structural features in influencing citation behavior remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose GEO-SFE, a systematic framework for structural feature engineering in generative engine optimization. Our approach decomposes content structure into three hierarchical levels: macro-structure (document architecture), meso-structure (information chunking), and micro-structure (visual emphasis), and models their impact on citation probability across different generative engine architectures. We develop architecture-aware optimization strategies and predictive models that preserve semantic integrity while improving structural effectiveness. Experimental evaluation across six mainstream generative engines demonstrates consistent improvements in citation rate (17.3 percent) and subjective quality (18.5 percent), validating the effectiveness and generalizability of the proposed framework. This work establishes structural optimization as a foundational component of GEO, providing a data-driven methodology for enhancing content visibility in LLM-powered information ecosystems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式引擎优化中的结构特征工程：内容结构如何塑造引用行为
AI驱动的搜索引擎的普及已将信息发现从传统的基于链接的检索转变为直接答案生成并选择性引用来源，这为内容可见性带来了新挑战。尽管现有生成式引擎优化（GEO）方法主要关注语义内容修改，但结构特征对引用行为的影响仍被忽视。 本文提出GEO-SFE，一种面向生成式引擎优化的结构特征工程系统框架。我们的方法将内容结构分解为三个层次：宏观结构（文档架构）、中观结构（信息分块）和微观结构（视觉强调），并建模其在不同生成式引擎架构中的引用概率影响。我们开发了架构感知的优化策略和预测模型，在保持语义完整性的同时提升结构有效性。 在六个主流生成式引擎上的实验评估显示，引用率提升17.3%，主观质量提升18.5%，验证了该框架的有效性和普适性。本研究确立了结构优化作为GEO的基础组成部分，提供了一种数据驱动的方法，以增强LLM驱动信息生态系统中的内容可见性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the shift from link-based search to AI-generated answers with selective citations, this paper addresses the underexplored role of content structure in generative engine optimization. It introduces GEO-SFE, a framework that decomposes content into macro-, meso-, and micro-structural levels and models their impact on citation probability across different generative engine architectures. Through architecture-aware optimization and predictive modeling, the framework preserves semantic meaning while enhancing structural effectiveness. 随着AI生成答案和选择性引用取代传统链接检索，本文关注内容结构在生成式引擎优化中的被忽视作用。提出GEO-SFE框架，将内容结构分解为宏观、中观和微观三个层级，并建模其对不同生成式引擎架构下引用概率的影响。该方法采用架构感知优化与预测建模，在保持语义完整性的同时提升结构有效性。在六个主流引擎上的实验显示，引用率平均提升17.3%，主观质量提升18.5%，验证了该框架的有效性和普适性。
Quantifying Cross-Modal Interactions in Multimodal Glioma Survival Prediction via InterSHAP: Evidence for Additive Signal Integration
Authors: Iain Swift, JingHua Ye, Ruairi O'Reilly
First: 2026-03-31T16:39:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:39:56+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 figure, under review at XAI 2026 LBW
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal deep learning for cancer prognosis is commonly assumed to benefit from synergistic cross-modal interactions, yet this assumption has not been directly tested in survival prediction settings. This work adapts InterSHAP, a Shapley interaction index-based metric, from classification to Cox proportional hazards models and applies it to quantify cross-modal interactions in glioma survival prediction. Using TCGA-GBM and TCGA-LGG data (n=575), we evaluate four fusion architectures combining whole-slide image (WSI) and RNA-seq features. Our central finding is an inverse relationship between predictive performance and measured interaction: architectures achieving superior discrimination (C-index 0.64$\to$0.82) exhibit equivalent or lower cross-modal interaction (4.8\%$\to$3.0\%). Variance decomposition reveals stable additive contributions across all architectures (WSI${\approx}$40\%, RNA${\approx}$55\%, Interaction${\approx}$4\%), indicating that performance gains arise from complementary signal aggregation rather than learned synergy. These findings provide a practical model auditing tool for comparing fusion strategies, reframe the role of architectural complexity in multimodal fusion, and have implications for privacy-preserving federated deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过InterSHAP量化多模态胶质瘤生存预测中的跨模态交互：支持加性信号整合的证据
多模态深度学习在癌症预后中通常被认为受益于协同的跨模态交互，但这一假设尚未在生存预测场景中直接验证。本研究将基于Shapley交互指数的InterSHAP指标从分类模型适配至Cox比例风险模型，并应用于量化胶质瘤生存预测中的跨模态交互。利用TCGA-GBM和TCGA-LGG数据（n=575），我们评估了四种结合全切片图像（WSI）与RNA-seq特征的融合架构。核心发现是预测性能与测量交互呈负相关：表现更优的架构（C指数0.64→0.82）展现出等同或更低的跨模态交互（4.8%→3.0%）。方差分解显示所有架构中各模态贡献稳定（WSI≈40%，RNA≈55%，交互≈4%），表明性能提升源于互补信号聚合而非学习到的协同效应。这些发现为比较融合策略提供了实用的模型审计工具，重新定义了多模态融合中架构复杂性的角色，并对隐私保护的联邦部署具有启示意义。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether cross-modal interactions in multimodal glioma survival prediction enhance model performance, challenging the common assumption of synergy. It adapts InterSHAP, a Shapley-based interaction metric, to Cox proportional hazards models and applies it to four fusion architectures using TCGA-GBM and TCGA-LGG data. Results show that models with higher predictive accuracy (C-index 0.64–0.82) exhibit lower cross-modal interaction (3.0%–4.8%), suggesting performance gains stem from additive signal integration rather than interaction. Variance decomposition confirms consistent contributions from WSI (~40%), RNA (~55%), and interaction (~4%), supporting a complementary rather than synergistic model. This provides a practical tool for evaluating fusion architectures and redefines the role of complexity in multimodal learning.
为验证多模态胶质瘤生存预测中跨模态交互的协同效应，该研究将用于分类的InterSHAP（基于Shapley值的交互指标）适配至Cox比例风险模型。在TCGA-GBM和TCGA-LGG数据集（n=575）上，对四种融合WSI与RNA-seq特征的架构进行评估。结果发现，预测性能更高的模型（C指数0.64–0.82）其交互强度更低或相当（4.8%–3.0%），而方差分解显示各架构中贡献稳定加性（WSI约40%，RNA约55%，交互约4%），表明性能提升源于特征互补而非协同学习。
ReCALL: Recalibrating Capability Degradation for MLLM-based Composed Image Retrieval
Authors: Tianyu Yang, Chenwei He, Xiangzhao Hao, Tianyue Wang, Jiarui Guo, Haiyun Guo, Leigang Qu, Jinqiao Wang, Tat-Seng Chua
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-02-02T04:52:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:38:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve target images based on a hybrid query comprising a reference image and a modification text. Early dual-tower Vision-Language Models (VLMs) struggle with cross-modality compositional reasoning required for this task. While adapting generative Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for retrieval offers a promising direction, we identify that this strategy overlooks a fundamental issue: compressing a generative MLLM into a single-embedding discriminative retriever triggers a paradigm conflict, which leads to Capability Degradation - the deterioration of native fine-grained reasoning after retrieval adaptation. To address this challenge, we propose ReCALL, a model-agnostic framework that follows a diagnose-generate-refine pipeline: First, we diagnose cognitive blind spots of the retriever via self-guided informative instance mining. Next, we generate corrective instructions and triplets by prompting the foundation MLLM and conduct quality control with VQA-based consistency filtering. Finally, we refine the retriever through continual training on these triplets with a grouped contrastive scheme, thereby internalizing fine-grained visual-semantic distinctions and realigning the discriminative embedding space of retriever with intrinsic compositional reasoning within the MLLM. Extensive experiments on CIRR and FashionIQ show that ReCALL consistently recalibrates degraded capabilities and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code is available at https://github.com/RemRico/Recall.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReCALL：面向基于MLLM的合成图像检索的能力重校准
合成图像检索（CIR）旨在根据由参考图像与修改文本组成的混合查询检索目标图像。早期的双塔视觉-语言模型（VLMs）难以应对该任务所需的跨模态组合推理。虽然将生成式多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）适配用于检索是一个有前景的方向，但我们发现该策略忽略了根本性问题：将生成式MLLM压缩为单嵌入判别式检索器会引发范式冲突，导致能力退化——即在检索适配后，模型原有的细粒度推理能力下降。为解决此问题，我们提出ReCALL，一个模型无关的框架，遵循诊断-生成-优化流程：首先，通过自引导信息性实例挖掘诊断检索器的认知盲区；接着，通过提示基础MLLM生成纠正指令与三元组，并以基于视觉问答（VQA）的一致性过滤进行质量控制；最后，通过在这些三元组上采用分组对比方案持续训练，优化检索器，使其内化细粒度视觉-语义差异，并重新对齐检索器的判别嵌入空间与MLLM内在的组合推理能力。在CIRR和FashionIQ上的大量实验表明，ReCALL持续重校准退化能力并取得最先进性能。代码见https://github.com/RemRico/Recall。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of fine-grained reasoning in MLLMs when adapted for retrieval tasks in Composed Image Retrieval, the authors propose ReCALL, a model-agnostic framework that diagnoses, generates, and refines corrective training data to recalibrate the retriever’s capability. The method first identifies cognitive blind spots via self-guided instance mining, then constructs corrective instruction-triplet pairs using a foundation MLLM and filters them for consistency via VQA. Finally, it refines the retriever through grouped contrastive training on these triplets, aligning its embedding space with the MLLM’s compositional reasoning. Experiments on CIRR and FashionIQ show ReCALL consistently restores degraded performance and achieves state-of-the-art results.
为解决MLLM在适配组合图像检索时因压缩导致的细粒度推理能力退化问题，作者提出ReCALL框架，通过自引导实例挖掘诊断检索器认知盲区，利用基础MLLM生成并经VQA过滤的修正三元组，再通过分组对比学习优化检索器。在CIRR与FashionIQ数据集上的实验表明，ReCALL能有效修复能力退化并达到当前最优性能。
Meteorology-Driven GPT4AP: A Multi-Task Forecasting LLM for Atmospheric Air Pollution in Data-Scarce Settings
Authors: Prasanjit Dey, Soumyabrata Dev, Bianca Schoen-Phelan
First: 2026-03-31T16:37:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:37:06+00:00
Comments: This manuscript is under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate forecasting of air pollution is important for environmental monitoring and policy support, yet data-driven models often suffer from limited generalization in regions with sparse observations. This paper presents Meteorology-Driven GPT for Air Pollution (GPT4AP), a parameter-efficient multi-task forecasting framework based on a pre-trained GPT-2 backbone and Gaussian rank-stabilized low-rank adaptation (rsLoRA). The model freezes the self-attention and feed-forward layers and adapts lightweight positional and output modules, substantially reducing the number of trainable parameters. GPT4AP is evaluated on six real-world air quality monitoring datasets under few-shot, zero-shot, and long-term forecasting settings. In the few-shot regime using 10% of the training data, GPT4AP achieves an average MSE/MAE of 0.686/0.442, outperforming DLinear (0.728/0.530) and ETSformer (0.734/0.505). In zero-shot cross-station transfer, the proposed model attains an average MSE/MAE of 0.529/0.403, demonstrating improved generalization compared with existing baselines. In long-term forecasting with full training data, GPT4AP remains competitive, achieving an average MAE of 0.429, while specialized time-series models show slightly lower errors. These results indicate that GPT4AP provides a data-efficient forecasting approach that performs robustly under limited supervision and domain shift, while maintaining competitive accuracy in data-rich settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：气象驱动的GPT4AP：面向数据稀缺区域的多任务大气污染预测大语言模型
准确预测空气污染对环境监测和政策支持至关重要，但数据驱动模型常在观测稀疏区域泛化能力受限。本文提出气象驱动的大气污染GPT（GPT4AP），基于预训练GPT-2骨干网络和高斯秩稳定低秩适配（rsLoRA）的参数高效多任务预测框架。该模型冻结自注意力与前馈层，仅适配轻量级位置与输出模块，大幅减少可训练参数。GPT4AP在六个真实空气质量监测数据集上评估，涵盖少样本、零样本及长期预测场景。在仅使用10%训练数据的少样本设置下，GPT4AP平均MSE/MAE为0.686/0.442，优于DLinear（0.728/0.530）和ETSformer（0.734/0.505）。在零样本跨站点迁移中，模型平均MSE/MAE为0.529/0.403，泛化能力优于现有基线。在使用全部训练数据的长期预测中，GPT4AP仍具竞争力，平均MAE为0.429，而专用时序模型误差略低。结果表明，GPT4AP提供了一种数据高效、在有限监督与领域偏移下表现稳健的预测方法，同时在数据丰富场景下保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurate air pollution forecasting in data-scarce regions, this study introduces GPT4AP, a parameter-efficient multi-task forecasting model built on a pre-trained GPT-2 architecture with rsLoRA adaptation. It freezes core layers and trains only lightweight positional and output modules, reducing trainable parameters while preserving performance. Evaluated across six real-world datasets under few-shot, zero-shot, and long-term settings, GPT4AP achieves superior accuracy: 0.686 MSE/0.442 MAE in few-shot (outperforming DLinear and ETSformer), 0.529 MSE/0.403 MAE in zero-shot cross-station transfer, and 0.429 MAE in long-term forecasting — demonstrating strong generalization and competitiveness even with limited data or domain shifts.
为解决数据稀疏地区空气污染预测难题，本文提出GPT4AP模型，基于预训练GPT-2架构并采用rsLoRA轻量化适配方法，冻结自注意力与前馈层，仅训练轻量位置与输出模块以大幅减少可训练参数。在六个真实空气质量数据集上，该模型在少样本（10%训练数据）下平均MSE/MAE达0.686/0.442，优于DLinear与ETSformer；零样本跨站点迁移中达0.529/0.403，泛化能力突出；长期预测中MAE为0.429，与专用时序模型相当，表明其在数据受限或领域偏移下仍保持高鲁棒性与竞争力。
Do covariates explain why these groups differ? The choice of reference group can reverse conclusions in the Oaxaca-Blinder decomposition
Authors: Manuel Quintero, Advik Shreekumar, William T. Stephenson, Tamara Broderick
First: 2026-03-31T16:34:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:34:48+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientists often want to explain why an outcome is different in two groups. For instance, differences in patient mortality rates across two hospitals could be due to differences in the patients themselves (covariates) or differences in medical care (outcomes given covariates). The Oaxaca--Blinder decomposition (OBD) is a standard tool to tease apart these factors. It is well known that the OBD requires choosing one of the groups as a reference, and the numerical answer can vary with the reference. To the best of our knowledge, there has not been a systematic investigation into whether the choice of OBD reference can yield different substantive conclusions and how common this issue is. In the present paper, we give existence proofs in real and simulated data that the OBD references can yield substantively different conclusions and that these differences are not entirely driven by model misspecification or small data. We prove that substantively different conclusions occur in up to half of the parameter space, but find these discrepancies rare in the real-data analyses we study. We explain this empirical rarity by examining how realistic data-generating processes can be biased towards parameters that do not change conclusions under the OBD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协变量能否解释这些群体差异的原因？奥克斯卡-布林德分解中的参考组选择可能逆转结论
科学家常希望解释为何两组间结果存在差异。例如，两家医院患者死亡率的差异可能源于患者自身特征（协变量）或医疗护理差异（在协变量给定下的结果）。奥克斯卡-布林德分解（OBD）是分离这些因素的标准工具。众所周知，OBD需要选择一个参考组，且数值结果会随参考组变化。据我们所知，尚无系统研究探讨OBD参考组选择是否会导致实质性结论不同，以及该问题多普遍。本文通过真实与模拟数据给出存在性证明，表明OBD参考组选择可导致实质性结论不同，且这些差异并非完全由模型设定错误或样本量小所致。我们证明，实质性结论差异在参数空间中可达一半，但在所研究的真实数据中罕见。我们通过分析现实数据生成过程如何偏向于在OBD下结论不变的参数，解释了该现象的实证罕见性。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how the choice of reference group in the Oaxaca-Blinder decomposition (OBD) can lead to substantively different conclusions about group differences, even when the underlying data and model are consistent. The authors demonstrate through theoretical proofs and simulations that such reversals in interpretation can occur in up to half of the parameter space, yet are empirically rare in real-world data. They attribute this rarity to the fact that realistic data-generating processes tend to favor parameter configurations where the OBD reference choice does not alter substantive conclusions, even though the method’s output is sensitive to reference selection.
为理解不同群体间结果差异的原因，本文研究了在奥萨卡-布林德分解（OBD）中选择不同参照组可能导致实质性结论差异的问题。通过理论证明和真实及模拟数据，作者表明OBD结果会因参照组不同而变化，且这种差异并非仅由模型设定不当或样本量小引起。虽然在参数空间中最多一半情况下会出现实质性结论差异，但在真实数据中这种差异罕见，作者归因于现实数据生成过程倾向于偏向那些OBD结论稳定的参数区域。
TransFIRA: Transfer Learning for Face Image Recognizability Assessment
Authors: Allen Tu, Kartik Narayan, Joshua Gleason, Jennifer Xu, Matthew Meyn, Tom Goldstein, Vishal M. Patel
Venue: Proceedings of the IEEE International Conference on Automatic Face and Gesture Recognition (FG), 2026
First: 2025-10-07T18:16:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:33:31+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://transfira.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Face recognition in unconstrained environments such as surveillance, video, and web imagery must contend with extreme variation in pose, blur, illumination, and occlusion, where conventional visual quality metrics fail to predict whether inputs are truly recognizable to the deployed encoder. Existing FIQA methods typically rely on visual heuristics, curated annotations, or computationally intensive generative pipelines, leaving their predictions detached from the encoder's decision geometry. We introduce TransFIRA (Transfer Learning for Face Image Recognizability Assessment), a lightweight and annotation-free framework that grounds recognizability directly in embedding space. TransFIRA delivers three advances: (i) a definition of recognizability via class-center similarity (CCS) and class-center angular separation (CCAS), yielding the first natural, decision-boundary-aligned criterion for filtering and weighting; (ii) a recognizability-informed aggregation strategy that achieves state-of-the-art verification accuracy on BRIAR and IJB-C while nearly doubling correlation with true recognizability, all without external labels, heuristics, or backbone-specific training; and (iii) new extensions beyond faces, including encoder-grounded explainability that reveals how degradations and subject-specific factors affect recognizability, and the first method for body recognizability assessment. Experiments confirm state-of-the-art results on faces, strong performance on body recognition, and robustness under cross-dataset shifts and out-of-distribution evaluation. Together, these contributions establish TransFIRA as a unified, geometry-driven framework for recognizability assessment that is encoder-specific, accurate, interpretable, and extensible across modalities, significantly advancing FIQA in accuracy, explainability, and scope.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TransFIRA：面向人脸图像可识别性评估的迁移学习
在监控、视频和网络图像等非约束环境下的人脸识别必须应对姿态、模糊、光照和遮挡的极端变化，而传统视觉质量度量无法预测输入是否真正可被部署的编码器识别。现有FIQA方法通常依赖视觉启发式、人工标注或计算密集型生成流程，导致其预测与编码器的决策几何脱节。我们提出TransFIRA（面向人脸图像可识别性评估的迁移学习），一个轻量级、无需标注的框架，直接在嵌入空间中定义可识别性。TransFIRA实现三大突破：(i) 通过类中心相似性（CCS）和类中心角分离（CCAS）定义可识别性，首次提供自然且与决策边界对齐的过滤与加权准则；(ii) 一种可识别性引导的聚合策略，在BRIAR和IJB-C上达到最先进验证准确率，同时几乎将与真实可识别性的相关性翻倍，且无需外部标签、启发式或特定骨干网络训练；(iii) 新的扩展应用，包括编码器驱动的可解释性，揭示退化与主体特异性因素如何影响可识别性，以及首个身体可识别性评估方法。实验验证了其在人脸上的最先进性能、在身体识别上的强表现，以及在跨数据集迁移和分布外评估中的鲁棒性。这些贡献共同确立TransFIRA为一个统一、几何驱动的可识别性评估框架，具有编码器特异性、高精度、可解释性及多模态扩展能力，显著提升了FIQA在准确性、可解释性和适用范围方面的水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of conventional metrics to predict face recognizability in real-world conditions, TransFIRA introduces a lightweight, annotation-free framework that directly assesses recognizability within the embedding space of face encoders. It defines recognizability using class-center similarity and angular separation, aligns predictions with decision boundaries, and aggregates scores using a recognizability-informed strategy. Experiments show it achieves state-of-the-art verification accuracy on BRIAR and IJB-C while nearly doubling correlation with true recognizability, without external labels or backbone-specific training, and extends to body recognition with encoder-grounded explainability.
为解决传统视觉质量指标在真实场景中无法准确预测人脸可识别性的问题，TransFIRA提出一种轻量级、无需标注的框架，直接在嵌入空间中通过类中心相似度与角度分离度衡量可识别性。该方法基于编码器决策边界定义自然的筛选与加权准则，并采用可识别性引导的聚合策略，在BRIAR和IJB-C数据集上达到最先进验证精度，同时将与真实可识别性的相关性近倍提升。此外，该方法扩展至身体识别并提供编码器驱动的可解释性，验证了其在跨数据集和模态下的鲁棒性。
Trimodal Deep Learning for Glioma Survival Prediction: A Feasibility Study Integrating Histopathology, Gene Expression, and MRI
Authors: Iain Swift, JingHua Ye
First: 2026-03-31T16:32:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:32:22+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 1 figure, submitted to the IEEE CBMS 2026 conference, still waiting for notification
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal deep learning has improved prognostic accuracy for brain tumours by integrating histopathology and genomic data, yet the contribution of volumetric MRI within unified survival frameworks remains unexplored. This pilot study extends a bimodal framework by incorporating Fluid Attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) MRI from BraTS2021 as a third modality. Using the TCGA-GBMLGG cohort (664 patients), we evaluate three unimodal models, nine bimodal configurations, and three trimodal configurations across early, late, and joint fusion strategies. In this small cohort setting, trimodal early fusion achieves an exploratory Composite Score (CS = 0.854), with a controlled $Δ$CS of +0.011 over the bimodal baseline on identical patients, though this difference is not statistically significant (p = 0.250, permutation test). MRI achieves reasonable unimodal discrimination (CS = 0.755) but does not substantially improve bimodal pairs, while providing measurable uplift in the three-way combination. All MRI containing experiments are constrained to 19 test patients, yielding wide bootstrap confidence intervals (e.g. [0.400,1.000]) that preclude definitive conclusions. These findings provide preliminary evidence that a third imaging modality may add prognostic value even with limited sample sizes, and that additional modalities require sufficient multimodal context to contribute effectively.

Learning Structural-Functional Brain Representations through Multi-Scale Adaptive Graph Attention for Cognitive Insight
Authors: Badhan Mazumder, Sir-Lord Wiafe, Aline Kotoski, Vince D. Calhoun, Dong Hye Ye
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:32:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:32:15+00:00
Comments: Preprint version of the paper accepted to the IEEE International Conference on Acoustics, Speech, and Signal Processing (ICASSP 2026). This is the author's accepted manuscript. The final published version will appear in IEEE Xplore
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how brain structure and function interact is key to explaining intelligence yet modeling them jointly is challenging as the structural and functional connectome capture complementary aspects of organization. We introduced Multi-scale Adaptive Graph Network (MAGNet), a Transformer-style graph neural network framework that adaptively learns structure-function interactions. MAGNet leverages source-based morphometry from structural MRI to extract inter-regional morphological features and fuses them with functional network connectivity from resting-state fMRI. A hybrid graph integrates direct and indirect pathways, while local-global attention refines connectivity importance and a joint loss simultaneously enforces cross-modal coherence and optimizes the prediction objective end-to-end. On the ABCD dataset, MAGNet outperformed relevant baselines, demonstrating effective multimodal integration for advancing our understanding of cognitive function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多尺度自适应图注意力学习结构-功能脑表征以获得认知洞察
理解大脑结构与功能如何相互作用是解释智能的关键，但同时建模二者具有挑战性，因为结构和功能连接组分别捕捉组织的互补方面。我们提出了多尺度自适应图网络（MAGNet），这是一种Transformer风格的图神经网络框架，可自适应学习结构-功能交互。MAGNet利用结构MRI的源基形态测量提取区域间形态特征，并将其与静息态fMRI的功能网络连接性融合。混合图整合了直接和间接通路，局部-全局注意力机制优化连接重要性，联合损失函数同时强制跨模态一致性并端到端优化预测目标。在ABCD数据集上，MAGNet优于相关基线方法，展示了有效多模态整合以推进认知功能理解的能力。
Summary / 总结
To better understand how brain structure and function jointly contribute to cognition, the authors developed MAGNet, a Transformer-inspired graph neural network that adaptively models structure-function interactions. It integrates morphological features from structural MRI with functional connectivity from resting-state fMRI, combining direct and indirect brain pathways into a hybrid graph. Using local-global attention and a joint loss function, MAGNet simultaneously refines connectivity importance and enforces cross-modal alignment while optimizing prediction. Evaluated on the ABCD dataset, MAGNet consistently outperformed existing baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in multimodal integration for cognitive insight.
为深入理解脑结构与功能如何协同影响认知能力，作者提出MAGNet，一种受Transformer启发的图神经网络，可自适应学习结构与功能连接组间的交互关系。该方法从结构MRI中提取区域形态特征，并与静息态fMRI的功能连接图融合，构建包含直接与间接通路的混合图结构。通过局部-全局注意力机制调整连接权重，并采用联合损失函数同步优化跨模态一致性与预测目标。在ABCD数据集上的实验表明，MAGNet优于现有基线模型，验证了其在多模态整合中对认知建模的有效性。
Scaling Video Pretraining for Surgical Foundation Models
Authors: Sicheng Lu, Zikai Xiao, Jianhui Wei, Danyu Sun, Qi Lu, Keli Hu, Yang Feng, Jian Wu, Zongxin Yang, Zuozhu Liu
First: 2026-03-31T16:31:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:31:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surgical video understanding is essential for computer-assisted interventions, yet existing surgical foundation models remain constrained by limited data scale, procedural diversity, and inconsistent evaluation, often lacking a reproducible training pipeline. We propose SurgRec, a scalable and reproducible pretraining recipe for surgical video understanding, instantiated with two variants: SurgRec-MAE and SurgRec-JEPA. We curate a large multi-source corpus of 10,535 videos and 214.5M frames spanning endoscopy, laparoscopy, cataract, and robotic surgery. Building on this corpus, we develop a unified pretraining pipeline with balanced sampling and standardize a reproducible benchmark across 16 downstream datasets and four clinical domains with consistent data splits. Across extensive comparisons against SSL baselines and vision-language models, SurgRec consistently achieves superior performance across downstream datasets. In contrast, VLMs prove unreliable for fine-grained temporal recognition, exhibiting both performance gaps and sensitivity to prompt phrasing. Our work provides a reproducible, scalable foundation for the community to build more general surgical video models. All code, models, and data will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向外科手术基础模型的视频预训练扩展
外科手术视频理解对计算机辅助手术至关重要，但现有外科手术基础模型受限于数据规模小、手术程序多样性不足及评估不一致，常缺乏可复现的训练流程。我们提出SurgRec，一种可扩展且可复现的外科手术视频理解预训练方案，包含两个变体：SurgRec-MAE和SurgRec-JEPA。我们构建了一个包含10,535个视频和2.145亿帧的多源大型语料库，涵盖内窥镜、腹腔镜、白内障和机器人手术。基于该语料库，我们开发了统一的预训练流程并采用平衡采样，同时在16个下游数据集和四个临床领域中标准化了可复现的基准测试（数据划分一致）。在与SSL基线和视觉-语言模型的广泛对比中，SurgRec在所有下游数据集上均表现更优。相比之下，视觉-语言模型在细粒度时间识别任务中表现不可靠，存在性能差距且对提示词表述敏感。我们的工作为社区提供了一个可复现、可扩展的基础，以构建更通用的外科手术视频模型。所有代码、模型和数据将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing surgical foundation models in data scale, procedural diversity, and evaluation consistency, this work introduces SurgRec, a scalable and reproducible pretraining framework for surgical video understanding, implemented via two variants: SurgRec-MAE and SurgRec-JEPA. The method leverages a large multi-source corpus of 10,535 videos and 214.5M frames across four surgical domains, and employs a unified pretraining pipeline with balanced sampling and standardized benchmarking across 16 downstream datasets. Experimental results show SurgRec outperforms SSL baselines and vision-language models across all tasks, while VLMs demonstrate poor reliability in fine-grained temporal recognition, showing performance gaps and sensitivity to prompt phrasing.
为解决现有手术基础模型在数据规模、术式多样性及评估一致性方面的不足，本文提出SurgRec框架，包含SurgRec-MAE与SurgRec-JEPA两个变体，用于手术视频理解的可扩展与可复现预训练。该方法构建了涵盖内窥镜、腹腔镜、白内障及机器人手术四大领域的10,535个视频、2.145亿帧的多源语料库，并采用平衡采样与标准化评估流程，在16个下游数据集上进行统一验证。实验表明，SurgRec在所有任务中均优于现有自监督基线与视觉-语言模型，而视觉-语言模型在细粒度时序识别中表现不稳定，且对提示词表述敏感。
SkillRouter: Skill Routing for LLM Agents at Scale
Authors: YanZhao Zheng, ZhenTao Zhang, Chao Ma, YuanQiang Yu, JiHuai Zhu, Yong Wu, Tianze Xu, Baohua Dong, Hangcheng Zhu, Ruohui Huang, Gang Yu
First: 2026-03-23T18:23:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:28:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reusable skills let LLM agents package task-specific procedures, tool affordances, and execution guidance into modular building blocks. As skill ecosystems grow to tens of thousands of entries, exposing every skill at inference time becomes infeasible. This creates a skill-routing problem: given a user task, the system must identify relevant skills before downstream planning or execution. Existing agent stacks often rely on progressive disclosure, exposing only skill names and descriptions while hiding the full implementation body. We examine this design choice on a SkillsBench-derived benchmark with approximately 80K candidate skills, targeting the practically important setting of large skill registries with heavy overlap. Across representative sparse, dense, and reranking baselines on this setting, hiding the skill body causes a 31--44 percentage point drop in routing accuracy, showing that full skill text is a critical routing signal in this setting rather than a minor metadata refinement. Motivated by this finding, we present SkillRouter, a compact 1.2B full-text retrieve-and-rerank pipeline. SkillRouter achieves 74.0% Hit@1 on our benchmark -- the strongest average top-1 routing performance among the baselines we evaluate -- while using 13$\times$ fewer parameters and running 5.8$\times$ faster than the strongest base pipeline. The ranking gains further generalize to a supplementary benchmark independently constructed from three skill sources. In a complementary end-to-end study across four coding agents, routing gains transfer to improved task success, with larger gains for more capable agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillRouter：面向大规模LLM代理的技能路由
可复用的技能使LLM代理能够将任务特定的程序、工具功能和执行指导封装为模块化构建块。随着技能生态系统扩展至数万个条目，推理时暴露所有技能变得不可行。这导致了一个技能路由问题：给定用户任务，系统必须在下游规划或执行前识别相关技能。现有代理堆栈通常依赖渐进式披露，仅暴露技能名称和描述，而隐藏完整实现体。我们在基于SkillsBench的约8万候选技能基准上考察了这一设计选择，目标是实际重要的大规模技能注册表场景，且存在大量重叠。在该场景下，针对代表性稀疏、密集和重排序基线，隐藏技能体导致路由准确率下降31至44个百分点，表明完整技能文本是该场景下关键的路由信号，而非次要的元数据优化。受此发现启发，我们提出了SkillRouter，一个紧凑的12亿参数全文检索与重排序管道。SkillRouter在我们的基准上达到74.0%的Hit@1，是所评估基线中最强的Top-1路由性能，同时参数量仅为最强基线的1/13，运行速度快5.8倍。排序增益进一步泛化至从三个技能源独立构建的补充基准。在四个编码代理的互补端到端研究中，路由增益转化为任务成功率提升，能力更强的代理获得更大增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently route user tasks to relevant skills in large-scale LLM agent systems, the paper investigates how hiding full skill implementations impacts routing accuracy. Using a benchmark of 80K skills with high overlap, it finds that concealing skill bodies reduces routing accuracy by 31–44 percentage points compared to using full text, demonstrating the critical role of full skill content. To address this, the authors introduce SkillRouter, a 1.2B-parameter retrieve-and-rerank model that achieves 74.0% Hit@1 — the highest among evaluated baselines — while being 13 times smaller and 5.8 times faster than the best baseline. The gains generalize to other skill sources and translate into improved task success across four coding agents, especially for more capable models.
随着LLM代理技能生态扩展至数万条，推理时暴露完整技能实现变得不现实，因此亟需高效的技能路由机制。研究发现，仅暴露技能元数据而隐藏实现体会导致路由准确率下降31–44个百分点。为此，论文提出SkillRouter，一个1.2B参数的检索-重排系统，利用完整技能文本进行路由，在8万条技能的基准测试中达到74.0%的Hit@1，优于所有基线模型，参数量减少13倍，速度提升5.8倍。该优势在其他技能源上也具有一致性，并在四个编码代理的端到端测试中转化为更高的任务成功率，尤其对能力更强的模型效果更显著。
SurgTEMP: Temporal-Aware Surgical Video Question Answering with Text-guided Visual Memory for Laparoscopic Cholecystectomy
Authors: Shi Li, Vinkle Srivastav, Nicolas Chanel, Saurav Sharma, Nabani Banik, Lorenzo Arboit, Kun Yuan, Pietro Mascagni, Nicolas Padoy
First: 2026-03-31T16:27:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:27:46+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 14 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surgical procedures are inherently complex and risky, requiring extensive expertise and constant focus to well navigate evolving intraoperative scenes. Computer-assisted systems such as surgical visual question answering (VQA) offer promises for education and intraoperative support. Current surgical VQA research largely focuses on static frame analysis, overlooking rich temporal semantics. Surgical video question answering is further challenged by low visual contrast, its highly knowledge-driven nature, diverse analytical needs spanning scattered temporal windows, and the hierarchy from basic perception to high-level intraoperative assessment. To address these challenges, we propose SurgTEMP, a multimodal LLM framework featuring (i) a query-guided token selection module that builds hierarchical visual memory (spatial and temporal memory banks) and (ii) a Surgical Competency Progression (SCP) training scheme. Together, these components enable effective modeling of variable-length surgical videos while preserving procedure-relevant cues and temporal coherence, and better support diverse downstream assessment tasks. To support model development, we introduce CholeVidQA-32K, a surgical video question answering dataset comprising 32K open-ended QA pairs and 3,855 video segments (approximately 128 h total) from laparoscopic cholecystectomy. The dataset is organized into a three-level hierarchy -- Perception, Assessment, and Reasoning -- spanning 11 tasks from instrument/action/anatomy perception to Critical View of Safety (CVS), intraoperative difficulty, skill proficiency, and adverse event assessment. In comprehensive evaluations against state-of-the-art open-source multimodal and video LLMs (fine-tuned and zero-shot), SurgTEMP achieves substantial performance improvements, advancing the state of video-based surgical VQA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SurgTEMP：基于文本引导视觉记忆的时序感知腹腔镜胆囊切除术手术视频问答
外科手术固有复杂且高风险，需高度专业技能与持续专注以应对术中动态场景。计算机辅助系统如手术视觉问答（VQA）在教育与术中支持方面具有潜力。当前手术VQA研究多聚焦静态帧分析，忽视丰富的时间语义。手术视频问答还面临视觉对比度低、高度依赖知识、分析需求多样且跨越分散时间窗口、以及从基础感知到高级术中评估的层次结构等挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出SurgTEMP，一种多模态大语言模型框架，包含（i）基于查询的令牌选择模块，构建分层视觉记忆（空间与时间记忆库），以及（ii）外科能力进展（SCP）训练方案。这些组件共同实现对可变长度手术视频的有效建模，保留手术相关线索与时间连贯性，并更好地支持多样化的下游评估任务。为支持模型开发，我们引入CholeVidQA-32K数据集，包含32,000组开放式问答对及3,855个视频片段（总计约128小时），源自腹腔镜胆囊切除术。该数据集按三级层次组织——感知、评估与推理——涵盖11项任务，从器械/动作/解剖结构感知至安全关键视野（CVS）、术中难度、技能熟练度及不良事件评估。在与当前最先进的开源多模态及视频大语言模型（微调与零样本）的全面评估中，SurgTEMP显著提升性能，推动基于视频的手术VQA发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust computer-assisted support in complex laparoscopic cholecystectomy procedures, this work introduces SurgTEMP, a multimodal LLM framework designed to handle temporal dynamics in surgical video question answering. It employs a query-guided token selection module to construct hierarchical visual memory banks capturing both spatial and temporal cues, paired with a Surgical Competency Progression training scheme to enhance model understanding across procedural stages. Evaluated on the newly proposed CholeVidQA-32K dataset — containing 32K QA pairs and 128 hours of surgical video organized into Perception, Assessment, and Reasoning tiers — SurgTEMP outperforms existing multimodal and video LLMs in both fine-tuned and zero-shot settings, demonstrating superior capability in modeling variable-length videos while preserving critical temporal coherence and supporting diverse intraoperative assessment tasks.
为提升腹腔镜胆囊切除术中的术中支持与教学效果，本文提出SurgTEMP框架，通过查询引导的令牌选择机制构建空间与时间层级的视觉记忆库，并结合手术能力进展训练方案，以对齐模型学习与手术技能发展。在包含32K问答对、11项任务的CholeVidQA-32K新数据集上，SurgTEMP在微调与零样本设置下均显著优于现有多模态及视频大模型，有效建模可变长度手术视频，保留关键时序一致性，并支持多样化的术中评估需求。
NeuroBRIDGE: Behavior-Conditioned Koopman Dynamics with Riemannian Alignment for Early Substance Use Initiation Prediction from Longitudinal Functional Connectome
Authors: Badhan Mazumder, Sir-Lord Wiafe, Vince D. Calhoun, Dong Hye Ye
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:24:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:24:59+00:00
Comments: Preprint version of the paper accepted to the IEEE International Conference on Acoustics, Speech, and Signal Processing (ICASSP 2026). This is the author's accepted manuscript. The final published version will appear in IEEE Xplore
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Early identification of adolescents at risk for substance use initiation (SUI) is vital yet difficult, as most predictors treat connectivity as static or cross-sectional and miss how brain networks change over time and with behavior. We proposed NeuroBRIDGE (Behavior conditioned RIemannian Koopman Dynamics on lonGitudinal connEctomes), a novel graph neural network-based framework that aligns longitudinal functional connectome in a Riemannian tangent space and couples dual-time attention with behavioral-conditioned Koopman dynamics to capture temporal change. Evaluated on ABCD, NeuroBRIDGE improved future SUI prediction over relevant baselines while offering interpretable insights into neural pathways, refining our understanding of neurodevelopmental risk and informing targeted prevention.

标题：NeuroBRIDGE：基于黎曼对齐的行为条件Koopman动力学，用于从纵向功能连接组预测青少年早期物质使用启动
早期识别有物质使用启动风险的青少年至关重要，但困难重重，因为大多数预测方法将连接性视为静态或横断面数据，忽略了大脑网络随时间及行为变化的动态性。我们提出了NeuroBRIDGE（行为条件黎曼Koopman动力学在纵向连接组上的应用），一种新颖的图神经网络框架，通过在黎曼切线空间对齐纵向功能连接组，并结合双时间注意力与行为条件Koopman动力学，捕捉时间变化。在ABCD数据集上的评估表明，NeuroBRIDGE在预测未来物质使用启动方面优于相关基线方法，同时提供可解释的神经通路洞察，深化了我们对神经发育风险的理解，并为精准预防提供依据。
