Evaluated on the ABCD dataset, NeuroBRIDGE outperformed existing baselines in predicting future substance use initiation while providing interpretable neural pathway insights that refine understanding of neurodevelopmental risk.
为解决青少年物质使用启动预测中脑网络动态变化被忽视的问题，作者提出NeuroBRIDGE框架，通过黎曼切空间对纵向功能连接组进行对齐，并结合双时间注意力与行为条件Koopman动力学建模时间演化。在ABCD数据集上评估时，该方法在预测未来物质使用启动方面优于现有基线，同时提供可解释的神经通路洞察，深化了对神经发育风险的理解。
Think Anywhere in Code Generation
Authors: Xue Jiang, Tianyu Zhang, Ge Li, Mengyang Liu, Taozhi Chen, Zhenhua Xu, Binhua Li, Wenpin Jiao, Zhi Jin, Yongbin Li, Yihong Dong
First: 2026-03-31T16:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:24:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in reasoning Large Language Models (LLMs) have primarily relied on upfront thinking, where reasoning occurs before final answer. However, this approach suffers from critical limitations in code generation, where upfront thinking is often insufficient as problems' full complexity only reveals itself during code implementation. Moreover, it cannot adaptively allocate reasoning effort throughout the code generation process where difficulty varies significantly. In this paper, we propose Think-Anywhere, a novel reasoning mechanism that enables LLMs to invoke thinking on-demand at any token position during code generation. We achieve Think-Anywhere by first teaching LLMs to imitate the reasoning patterns through cold-start training, then leveraging outcome-based RL rewards to drive the model's autonomous exploration of when and where to invoke reasoning. Extensive experiments on four mainstream code generation benchmarks (i.e., LeetCode, LiveCodeBench, HumanEval, and MBPP) show that Think-Anywhere achieves state-of-the-art performance over both existing reasoning methods and recent post-training approaches, while demonstrating consistent generalization across diverse LLMs. Our analysis further reveals that Think-Anywhere enables the model to adaptively invoke reasoning at high-entropy positions, providing enhanced interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代码生成中的任意位置思考
近期在推理大语言模型（LLMs）方面的进展主要依赖于前置思考，即在最终答案生成前进行推理。然而，这种方法在代码生成中存在关键局限性，因为问题的完整复杂性往往在代码实现过程中才显现，且推理资源无法根据代码生成过程中难度的变化进行自适应分配。本文提出Think-Anywhere，一种新颖的推理机制，使LLMs能够在代码生成过程中任意token位置按需调用思考。我们通过冷启动训练使LLMs模仿推理模式，再利用基于结果的强化学习奖励驱动模型自主探索何时何地调用推理。在四个主流代码生成基准（LeetCode、LiveCodeBench、HumanEval和MBPP）上的大量实验表明，Think-Anywhere在现有推理方法和近期后训练方法中均达到最先进性能，且在不同LLMs间表现出一致的泛化能力。分析进一步表明，Think-Anywhere使模型能够在高熵位置自适应调用推理，提升可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of upfront reasoning in code generation, where problem complexity often emerges during implementation and reasoning effort cannot be adaptively allocated, this paper introduces Think-Anywhere, a method enabling LLMs to invoke reasoning on-demand at any token position during code generation. The approach combines cold-start training to teach imitation of reasoning patterns, followed by reinforcement learning using outcome-based rewards to guide autonomous reasoning timing. Experiments across four benchmarks—LeetCode, LiveCodeBench, HumanEval, and MBPP—demonstrate that Think-Anywhere outperforms existing reasoning methods and recent post-training approaches, with consistent generalization across multiple LLMs, and reveals that reasoning is most effectively triggered at high-entropy positions, enhancing interpretability.
针对代码生成中前置推理难以应对问题复杂性动态变化、无法自适应分配推理资源的局限，本文提出Think-Anywhere方法，允许LLM在生成过程中任意位置按需调用推理。该方法先通过冷启动模仿学习训练推理模式，再结合基于结果的强化学习奖励引导模型自主探索推理时机。在LeetCode、LiveCodeBench、HumanEval和MBPP四个主流基准上的实验表明，Think-Anywhere优于现有推理方法和近期微调方法，且在不同LLM间表现稳定，并揭示其在高熵位置触发推理更有效，提升模型可解释性。
Biasless Language Models Learn Unnaturally: How LLMs Fail to Distinguish the Possible from the Impossible
Authors: Imry Ziv, Nur Lan, Emmanuel Chemla
First: 2025-10-08T16:17:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:22:29+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Are large language models (LLMs) sensitive to the distinction between humanly possible and impossible languages? This question was recently used in a broader debate on whether LLMs and humans share the same innate learning biases. Previous work has answered it in the positive by comparing LLM learning curves on existing language datasets and on "impossible" datasets derived from them via various perturbation functions. Using the same methodology, we examine this claim on a wider set of languages and impossible perturbations. We find that in most cases, GPT-2 learns each language and its impossible counterpart equally easily, in contrast to previous findings. We also apply a more lenient condition by testing whether GPT-2 provides any kind of separation between the whole sets of natural vs. impossible languages, based on cross-linguistic variance in metrics derived from the learning curves. Taken together, these perspectives show that GPT-2 provides no systematic separation between the possible and the impossible.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无偏语言模型的学习异常：大型语言模型如何无法区分可能与不可能的语言
大型语言模型（LLM）是否对人类可能与不可能语言之间的区别敏感？这一问题近期被用于更广泛的辩论，探讨LLM与人类是否共享相同的先天学习偏见。此前研究通过比较LLM在现有语言数据集及通过多种扰动函数生成的“不可能”数据集上的学习曲线，得出肯定答案。我们采用相同方法，对更广泛的语言及不可能扰动进行检验。结果发现，在大多数情况下，GPT-2对每种语言及其不可能对应物的学习难度相同，与先前研究结果相反。此外，我们采用更宽松的条件，测试GPT-2是否能基于学习曲线衍生的跨语言指标差异，对自然语言与不可能语言整体集合进行区分。综合来看，这些视角表明GPT-2无法系统性区分可能与不可能。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can distinguish between humanly possible and impossible languages, a question tied to debates about shared innate learning biases between humans and LLMs. Using a methodology that compares learning curves on natural languages versus their impossible perturbations, the authors re-examine previous findings across a broader set of languages and perturbations. Their experiments reveal that GPT-2 learns both natural and impossible languages with similar ease in most cases, and even under a more lenient criterion measuring cross-linguistic variance in learning metrics, GPT-2 fails to show any systematic separation between possible and impossible languages.
本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLM）是否能区分人类可能与不可能的语言，这一问题与人类与LLM是否共享先天学习偏见的争论相关。研究采用与先前工作相同的方法，比较自然语言与人工扰动生成的“不可能”语言的学习曲线，但在更广泛的语言和扰动集合上进行重测。实验发现，GPT-2在多数情况下对自然语言与不可能语言的学习难度几乎相同，与先前结论相悖。即使在更宽松的条件下——通过跨语言学习曲线衍生指标的方差来衡量——GPT-2也未能显示出对可能与不可能语言的系统性区分，表明LLM缺乏内在区分能力。
Detecting Unknown Objects via Energy-based Separation for Open World Object Detection
Authors: Jun-Woo Heo, Keonhee Park, Gyeong-Moon Park
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:20:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:20:56+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, Accepted at CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we tackle the problem of Open World Object Detection (OWOD). This challenging scenario requires the detector to incrementally learn to classify known objects without forgetting while identifying unknown objects without supervision. Previous OWOD methods have enhanced the unknown discovery process and employed memory replay to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. However, since existing methods heavily rely on the detector's known class predictions for detecting unknown objects, they struggle to effectively learn and recognize unknown object representations. Moreover, while memory replay mitigates forgetting of old classes, it often sacrifices the knowledge of newly learned classes. To resolve these limitations, we propose DEUS (Detecting Unknowns via energy-based Separation), a novel framework that addresses the challenges of Open World Object Detection. DEUS consists of Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF)-Subspace Unknown Separation (EUS) and an Energy-based Known Distinction (EKD) loss. EUS leverages ETF-based geometric properties to create orthogonal subspaces, enabling cleaner separation between known and unknown object representations. Unlike prior energy-based approaches that consider only the known space, EUS utilizes energies from both spaces to better capture distinct patterns of unknown objects. Furthermore, EKD loss enforces the separation between previous and current classifiers, thus minimizing knowledge interference between previous and newly learned classes during memory replay. We thoroughly validate DEUS on OWOD benchmarks, demonstrating outstanding performance improvements in unknown detection while maintaining competitive known class performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基于能量的分离检测开放世界中的未知物体
本工作研究开放世界物体检测（OWOD）问题。该挑战性场景要求检测器在不断学习分类已知物体的同时不遗忘，并在无监督条件下识别未知物体。现有OWOD方法虽提升了未知物体发现过程并采用记忆回放缓解灾难性遗忘，但因过度依赖检测器对已知类别的预测来检测未知物体，难以有效学习和识别未知物体表征。此外，记忆回放虽可缓解旧类别的遗忘，却常牺牲新学习类别的知识。为解决上述局限，我们提出DEUS（通过基于能量的分离检测未知物体）框架，以应对开放世界物体检测的挑战。DEUS包含等角紧框架（ETF）子空间未知分离（EUS）与基于能量的已知区分（EKD）损失。EUS利用ETF几何特性构建正交子空间，实现已知与未知物体表征更清晰的分离；不同于先前仅考虑已知空间的能量方法，EUS利用双空间能量以更好捕捉未知物体独特模式。此外，EKD损失强制分离前后分类器，从而在记忆回放过程中最小化旧类与新类知识干扰。我们在OWOD基准上全面验证DEUS，证明其在未知检测方面显著性能提升，同时保持已知类别性能竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing open-world object detection methods that rely on known class predictions to identify unknown objects and suffer from knowledge interference during memory replay, this work proposes DEUS, a framework combining ETF-based subspace separation and energy-based known distinction loss. EUS creates orthogonal subspaces using equiangular tight frame geometry to cleanly separate known and unknown representations, leveraging energy from both spaces for better unknown pattern capture. EKD loss enforces classifier separation to reduce interference between old and new classes during memory replay. Experiments show DEUS significantly improves unknown detection accuracy while maintaining competitive performance on known classes across standard OWOD benchmarks.
为解决现有开放世界目标检测方法依赖已知类别预测识别未知目标、且记忆回放中易导致新旧知识干扰的问题，本文提出DEUS框架，结合基于等角紧框架的子空间分离与能量驱动的已知类别区分损失。EUS利用等角紧框架几何特性构建正交子空间，通过同时利用已知与未知空间的能量实现更清晰的类别分离；EKD损失则强制约束分类器间差异，减少记忆回放中的知识干扰。实验表明，DEUS在未知目标检测上显著优于现有方法，同时保持已知类别性能竞争力。
Structured Intent as a Protocol-Like Communication Layer: Cross-Model Robustness, Framework Comparison, and the Weak-Model Compensation Effect
Authors: Peng Gang
First: 2026-03-31T16:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:20:28+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, figures, tables, and appendix. Third paper in a cumulative research series on PPS and 5W3H structured intent representation, extending prior work to cross-model robustness, framework comparison, and user-study validation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How reliably can structured intent representations preserve user goals across different AI models, languages, and prompting frameworks? Prior work showed that PPS (Prompt Protocol Specification), a 5W3H-based structured intent framework, improves goal alignment in Chinese and generalizes to English and Japanese. This paper extends that line of inquiry in three directions: cross-model robustness across Claude, GPT-4o, and Gemini 2.5 Pro; controlled comparison with CO-STAR and RISEN; and a user study (N=50) of AI-assisted intent expansion in ecologically valid settings. Across 3,240 model outputs (3 languages x 6 conditions x 3 models x 3 domains x 20 tasks), evaluated by an independent judge (DeepSeek-V3), we find that structured prompting substantially reduces cross-language score variance relative to unstructured baselines. The strongest structured conditions reduce cross-language sigma from 0.470 to about 0.020. We also observe a weak-model compensation pattern: the lowest-baseline model (Gemini) shows a much larger D-A gain (+1.006) than the strongest model (Claude, +0.217). Under the current evaluation resolution, 5W3H, CO-STAR, and RISEN achieve similarly high goal-alignment scores, suggesting that dimensional decomposition itself is an important active ingredient. In the user study, AI-expanded 5W3H prompts reduce interaction rounds by 60 percent and increase user satisfaction from 3.16 to 4.04. These findings support the practical value of structured intent representation as a robust, protocol-like communication layer for human-AI interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构化意图作为协议式通信层：跨模型鲁棒性、框架比较与弱模型补偿效应
结构化意图表示在不同AI模型、语言和提示框架下，能多大程度可靠地保留用户目标？先前研究表明，基于5W3H的结构化意图框架PPS在中文中提升目标对齐，并可泛化至英文和日文。本文从三个方向拓展该研究：在Claude、GPT-4o和Gemini 2.5 Pro三模型间验证跨模型鲁棒性；与CO-STAR和RISEN进行受控比较；并在生态效度设置下开展50人用户研究，考察AI辅助意图扩展效果。在3,240个模型输出（3语言×6条件×3模型×3领域×20任务）中，由独立评估者（DeepSeek-V3）评估，发现结构化提示显著降低跨语言评分方差。最强结构化条件使跨语言标准差从0.470降至约0.020。我们还观察到弱模型补偿模式：基线最低的模型（Gemini）D-A增益达+1.006，远高于最强模型（Claude，+0.217）。在当前5W3H评估粒度下，CO-STAR与RISEN与PPS达成相似高目标对齐得分，表明维度分解本身是关键因素。用户研究显示，AI扩展的5W3H提示使交互轮次减少60%，用户满意度从3.16升至4.04。这些发现支持结构化意图表示作为人类-人工智能交互中鲁棒、协议式通信层的实用价值。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how structured intent representations, specifically the 5W3H-based PPS framework, preserve user goals across different AI models, languages, and prompting methods. Through experiments involving 3,240 model outputs across three languages, six conditions, three models, and multiple domains, the study finds that structured prompting significantly reduces cross-language score variance, cutting it from 0.470 to approximately 0.020. A notable 'weak-model compensation effect' is observed, where the lowest-performing model (Gemini) shows a larger goal-alignment gain than the strongest (Claude). Framework comparisons reveal that 5W3H, CO-STAR, and RISEN achieve similarly high alignment scores, indicating that dimensional decomposition is key. In a user study with 50 participants, AI-assisted intent expansion via 5W3H reduced interaction rounds by 60% and boosted satisfaction from 3.16 to 4.04, demonstrating practical value for human-AI communication.
本研究探讨了基于5W3H的PPS结构化意图框架如何在不同AI模型、语言和提示方法间保持用户目标的一致性。通过跨三语言、六条件、三模型及多领域的3240次模型输出实验（由独立评估者评判），发现结构化提示显著降低跨语言目标对齐的方差，标准差从0.470降至约0.020。研究还观察到“弱模型补偿效应”，即表现最差的模型（Gemini）目标对齐提升幅度（+1.006）远高于最强模型（Claude，+0.217）。与CO-STAR和RISEN对比发现，维度分解本身是性能关键，三者在5W3H评估下得分相近。50人用户研究显示，AI辅助的结构化提示可将交互轮次减少60%，满意度从3.16提升至4.04，证实结构化意图在人机交互中具有实用价值。
Physiological and Semantic Patterns in Medical Teams Using an Intelligent Tutoring System
Authors: Xiaoshan Huang, Conrad Borchers, Jiayi Zhang, Susanne P. Lajoie
First: 2026-03-31T16:18:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:18:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted as short paper to the 27th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence in Education (AIED 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective collaboration requires teams to manage complex cognitive and emotional states through Socially Shared Regulation of Learning (SSRL). Physiological synchrony (i.e., longitudinal alignment in physiological signals) can indicate these states, but is hard to interpret on its own. We investigate the physiological and conversational dynamics of four medical dyads diagnosing a virtual patient case using an intelligent tutoring system. Semantic shifts in dialogue were correlated with transient physiological synchrony peaks. We also coded utterance segments for SSRL and derived cosine similarity using sentence embeddings. The results showed that activating prior knowledge featured significantly lower semantic similarity than simpler task execution. High physiological synchrony was associated with lower semantic similarity, suggesting that such moments involve exploratory and varied language use. Qualitative analysis triangulated these synchrony peaks as ``pivotal moments'': successful teams synchronized during shared discovery, while unsuccessful teams peaked during shared uncertainty. This research advances human-centered AI by demonstrating how biological signals can be fused with dialogues to understand critical moments in problem solving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用智能辅导系统时医疗团队的生理与语义模式
有效协作要求团队通过社会共享学习调节（SSRL）管理复杂的认知与情绪状态。生理同步（即生理信号的纵向对齐）可指示这些状态，但单独难以解读。我们研究了四组医疗双人团队在智能辅导系统中诊断虚拟病例时的生理与对话动态。对话中的语义转变与短暂生理同步峰值相关。我们对话语片段编码SSRL，并使用句子嵌入计算余弦相似度。结果显示，激活先验知识时语义相似度显著低于简单任务执行。高生理同步与低语义相似度相关，表明此类时刻涉及探索性与多样化的语言使用。定性分析将这些同步峰值确认为“关键时刻”：成功团队在共享发现时同步，失败团队则在共享不确定性时达到峰值。本研究通过展示生物信号与对话融合如何理解问题解决中的关键时刻，推动以人为中心的人工智能发展。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how medical teams collaborate using an intelligent tutoring system by analyzing both physiological synchrony and conversational semantics. Motivated by the need to understand real-time team dynamics during complex problem-solving, researchers tracked four medical dyads diagnosing a virtual patient and correlated transient peaks in physiological alignment with shifts in dialogue meaning. Using sentence embeddings, they computed semantic similarity of utterances and found that moments involving activation of prior knowledge showed lower similarity than routine task execution. High physiological synchrony was linked to reduced semantic similarity, indicating exploratory communication, and qualitative analysis confirmed these synchrony peaks as 'pivotal moments'—successful teams synchronized during shared discovery, while unsuccessful teams peaked during shared uncertainty.
该研究通过分析生理同步与对话语义模式，探讨医疗团队在智能辅导系统中协作的机制。研究动机在于理解问题解决过程中认知与情绪的实时协调，研究人员追踪四组医疗双人组诊断虚拟病例，发现生理同步峰值常伴随对话语义变化，且高生理同步往往对应较低语义相似度，表明此时语言更探索性、多样化。质性分析进一步将这些同步峰值定义为“关键时刻”——成功团队在共享发现时同步，失败团队则在共享不确定性时达到峰值。研究证明生物信号与语言数据融合可识别协作中的关键动态，推动以人为中心的人工智能发展。
LG-HCC: Local Geometry-Aware Hierarchical Context Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Xuan Deng, Xiandong Meng, Hengyu Man, Qiang Zhu, Tiange Zhang, Debin Zhao, Xiaopeng Fan
First: 2026-03-30T13:39:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:17:23+00:00
Comments: 10
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity real-time rendering, its prohibitive storage overhead severely hinders practical deployment. Recent anchor-based 3DGS compression schemes reduce gaussina redundancy through ome advanced context models. However, overlook explicit geometric dependencies, leading to structural degradation and suboptimal rate-distortion performance. In this paper, we propose LG-HCC, a geometry-aware 3DGS compression framework that incorporates inter-anchor geometric correlations into anchor pruning and entropy coding for compact representation. Specifically, we introduce an Neighborhood-Aware Anchor Pruning (NAAP) strategy, which evaluates anchor importance via weighted neighborhood feature aggregation and merges redundant anchors into salient neighbors, yielding a compact yet geometry-consistent anchor set. Building upon this optimized structure, we further develop a hierarchical entropy coding scheme, in which coarse-to-fine priors are exploited through a lightweight Geometry-Guided Convolution (GG-Conv) operator to enable spatially adaptive context modeling and rate-distortion optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LG-HCC effectively resolves the structure preservation bottleneck, maintaining superior geometric integrity and rendering fidelity over state-of-the-art anchor-based compression approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LG-HCC：面向3D高斯点绘的局部几何感知分层上下文压缩
尽管3D高斯点绘（3DGS）支持高保真实时渲染，但其存储开销过大严重阻碍了实际部署。近期基于锚点的3DGS压缩方案通过高级上下文模型减少高斯冗余，但忽略了显式几何依赖关系，导致结构退化和率失真性能不佳。本文提出LG-HCC，一种几何感知的3DGS压缩框架，将锚点间的几何相关性融入锚点剪枝与熵编码，实现紧凑表示。具体而言，我们引入邻域感知锚点剪枝（NAAP）策略，通过加权邻域特征聚合评估锚点重要性，并将冗余锚点合并至显著邻域，生成紧凑且几何一致的锚点集。在此优化结构基础上，我们进一步开发分层熵编码方案，通过轻量级几何引导卷积（GG-Conv）操作利用粗到细先验，实现空间自适应上下文建模与率失真优化。大量实验表明，LG-HCC有效解决了结构保持瓶颈，在几何完整性与渲染保真度方面优于现有基于锚点的压缩方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage cost of 3D Gaussian Splatting despite its rendering quality, this paper proposes LG-HCC, a geometry-aware compression framework that improves rate-distortion performance by modeling inter-anchor geometric dependencies. It employs Neighborhood-Aware Anchor Pruning to selectively merge redundant anchors based on neighborhood feature aggregation, preserving structural integrity. Then, a hierarchical entropy coding scheme using Geometry-Guided Convolution enables spatially adaptive context modeling, enhancing compression efficiency. Experimental results show LG-HCC achieves better geometric fidelity and rendering quality compared to existing anchor-based methods while significantly reducing storage overhead.
为解决3D高斯点渲染虽质量高但存储开销过大的问题，本文提出LG-HCC框架，通过显式建模锚点间的几何依赖关系提升压缩性能。该框架首先引入邻域感知锚点剪枝策略，依据邻域特征加权聚合选择性合并冗余锚点，保持结构一致性；随后构建分层熵编码方案，利用轻量几何引导卷积自适应建模空间上下文并优化码率-失真。实验表明，LG-HCC在显著降低存储开销的同时，优于现有锚点压缩方法，保持更优的几何保真度和渲染质量。
Real-Time Explanations for Tabular Foundation Models
Authors: Luan Borges Teodoro Reis Sena, Francisco Galuppo Azevedo
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:17:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:17:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2nd DATA4Science Workshop at ICLR 2026, Rio de Janeiro, Brazil. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=StSMBSZqxx
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Interpretability is central for scientific machine learning, as understanding \emph{why} models make predictions enables hypothesis generation and validation. While tabular foundation models show strong performance, existing explanation methods like SHAP are computationally expensive, limiting interactive exploration. We introduce ShapPFN, a foundation model that integrates Shapley value regression directly into its architecture, producing both predictions and explanations in a single forward pass. On standard benchmarks, ShapPFN achieves competitive performance while producing high-fidelity explanations ($R^2$=0.96, cosine=0.99) over 1000\times faster than KernelSHAP (0.06s vs 610s). Our code is available at https://github.com/kunumi/ShapPFN
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于表格基础模型的实时解释
可解释性是科学机器学习的核心，理解模型为何做出预测有助于生成和验证假设。尽管表格基础模型表现优异，但现有解释方法（如SHAP）计算成本高，限制了交互式探索。我们提出了ShapPFN，一种将Shapley值回归直接集成到其架构中的基础模型，可在单次前向传播中同时生成预测和解释。在标准基准测试中，ShapPFN表现优异，且解释质量高（R²=0.96，余弦相似度=0.99），速度比KernelSHAP快1000倍（0.06秒 vs 610秒）。代码见 https://github.com/kunumi/ShapPFN
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable predictions in scientific machine learning, this work addresses the computational inefficiency of existing explanation methods like SHAP. It introduces ShapPFN, a foundation model that embeds Shapley value regression directly into its architecture, enabling simultaneous prediction and explanation generation in one forward pass. Experiments on standard benchmarks show ShapPFN achieves competitive accuracy while delivering high-fidelity explanations with an R² of 0.96 and cosine similarity of 0.99, running 1000 times faster than KernelSHAP (0.06s vs 610s).
为满足科学机器学习中对可解释预测的需求，该研究针对现有解释方法（如SHAP）计算效率低的问题，提出ShapPFN模型，将Shapley值回归直接集成到模型架构中，实现预测与解释的单次前向传播同步生成。在标准基准测试中，ShapPFN保持了竞争力的预测性能，同时提供高保真度解释（R²=0.96，余弦相似度=0.99），速度比KernelSHAP快1000倍（0.06秒 vs 610秒）。
Four Generations of Quantum Biomedical Sensors
Authors: Xin Jin, Priyam Srivastava, Ronghe Wang, Yuqing Li, Jonathan Beaumariage, Tom Purdy, M. V. Gurudev Dutt, Kang Kim, Kaushik Seshadreesan, Junyu Liu
First: 2026-03-31T16:16:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:16:27+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum sensing technologies offer transformative potential for ultra-sensitive biomedical sensing, yet their clinical translation remains constrained by classical noise limits and a reliance on macroscopic ensembles. We propose a unifying generational framework to organize the evolving landscape of quantum biosensors based on their utilization of quantum resources. First-generation devices utilize discrete energy levels for signal transduction but follow classical scaling laws. Second-generation sensors exploit quantum coherence to reach the standard quantum limit, while third-generation architectures leverage entanglement and spin squeezing to approach Heisenberg-limited precision. We further define an emerging fourth generation characterized by the end-to-end integration of quantum sensing with quantum learning and variational circuits, enabling adaptive inference directly within the quantum domain. By analyzing critical parameters such as bandwidth matching and sensor-tissue proximity, we identify key technological bottlenecks and propose a roadmap for transitioning from measuring physical observables to extracting structured biological information with quantum-enhanced intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：四代量子生物医学传感器
量子传感技术为超高灵敏度生物医学传感提供了变革潜力，但其临床转化仍受限于经典噪声限制及对宏观集合体的依赖。我们提出一个统一的代际框架，依据量子资源的利用情况来组织量子生物传感器不断演进的格局。第一代设备利用离散能级进行信号转导，但遵循经典标度律；第二代传感器利用量子相干性达到标准量子极限；第三代架构则利用纠缠和自旋压缩逼近海森堡极限精度。我们进一步定义了新兴的第四代，其特征是量子传感与量子学习及变分电路的端到端集成，可在量子域内直接实现自适应推理。通过分析带宽匹配和传感器-组织距离等关键参数，我们识别了关键技术瓶颈，并提出从测量物理可观测量向利用量子增强智能提取结构化生物信息的过渡路线图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome classical noise limits and ensemble dependence in biomedical sensing, this paper introduces a four-generation framework for quantum biosensors, tracing their evolution from classical scaling (first-gen) to quantum-coherence-based (second-gen) and entanglement-enhanced (third-gen) precision, culminating in fourth-gen systems that integrate quantum sensing with quantum learning for adaptive inference. Experimental analysis of bandwidth and proximity constraints reveals critical bottlenecks in clinical translation, and the framework proposes a pathway toward extracting structured biological data using quantum-enhanced intelligence.
为克服生物医学传感中的经典噪声限制和宏观集合依赖，本文提出一个四代量子生物传感器框架，从依赖离散能级的古典标度（第一代），到利用量子相干性达到标准量子极限（第二代），再到利用纠缠与自旋压缩逼近海森堡极限精度（第三代），最终发展为量子传感与量子学习的端到端集成（第四代）。作者通过分析带宽匹配与传感器-组织距离等关键参数，识别技术瓶颈，并指出未来量子生物传感器需从测量物理量转向利用量子增强智能提取结构化生物信息。
Learning Inter-Atomic Potentials without Explicit Equivariance
Authors: Ahmed A. Elhag, Arun Raja, Alex Morehead, Samuel M. Blau, Hongtao Zhao, Christian Tyrchan, Eva Nittinger, Garrett M. Morris, Michael M. Bronstein
First: 2025-09-25T22:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:16:20+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 tables, 11 figures. Under review. Changes from v2 to v3: Added results for new experiments, training models for 80 epochs on OMol25
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate and scalable machine-learned inter-atomic potentials (MLIPs) are essential for molecular simulations ranging from drug discovery to new material design. Current state-of-the-art models enforce roto-translational symmetries through equivariant neural network architectures, a hard-wired inductive bias that can often lead to reduced flexibility, computational efficiency, and scalability. In this work, we introduce TransIP: Transformer-based Inter-Atomic Potentials, a novel training paradigm for interatomic potentials achieving symmetry compliance without explicit architectural constraints. Our approach guides a generic non-equivariant Transformer-based model to learn SO(3)-equivariance by optimizing its representations in the embedding space. Trained on the recent Open Molecules (OMol25) collection, a large and diverse molecular dataset built specifically for MLIPs and covering different types of molecules (including small organics, biomolecular fragments, and electrolyte-like species), TransIP attains comparable performance in machine-learning force fields versus state-of-the-art equivariant baselines. Further, compared to a data augmentation baseline, TransIP achieves 40% to 60% improvement in performance across varying OMol25 dataset sizes. More broadly, our work shows that learned equivariance can be a powerful and efficient alternative to equivariant or augmentation-based MLIP models. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Ahmed-A-A-Elhag/TransIP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需显式等变性的原子间势能学习
准确且可扩展的机器学习原子间势能（MLIPs）对于从药物发现到新材料设计的分子模拟至关重要。当前最先进的模型通过等变神经网络架构强制执行旋转-平移对称性，这是一种固化的归纳偏置，往往导致灵活性、计算效率和可扩展性降低。本文提出TransIP：基于Transformer的原子间势能，这是一种新颖的原子间势能训练范式，无需显式架构约束即可实现对称性合规。我们的方法通过在嵌入空间中优化表示，引导通用非等变Transformer模型学习SO(3)等变性。在近期构建的、专为MLIPs设计且涵盖不同类型分子（包括小有机分子、生物分子片段和电解质类物种）的大型多样化数据集Open Molecules（OMol25）上训练，TransIP在机器学习力场性能上与最先进的等变基线相当。此外，与数据增强基线相比，TransIP在不同OMol25数据集规模下性能提升40%至60%。更广泛地说，我们的工作表明，学习到的等变性可作为等变或基于增强的MLIP模型的一种强大且高效的替代方案。代码已发布于：https://github.com/Ahmed-A-A-Elhag/TransIP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of hard-coded equivariance in current machine-learned inter-atomic potentials, this work proposes TransIP: a Transformer-based model that learns SO(3)-equivariance implicitly through embedding space optimization rather than explicit architectural constraints. Trained on the diverse Open Molecules (OMol25) dataset, TransIP achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art equivariant models while demonstrating 40–60% improvement over data augmentation baselines across varying dataset sizes. The results suggest that learned equivariance can be both effective and scalable for molecular simulations without sacrificing accuracy or efficiency.
为克服现有机器学习原子势中硬编码等变性带来的灵活性与效率限制，本研究提出TransIP：一种通过嵌入空间优化隐式学习SO(3)等变性的Transformer模型，无需显式架构约束。在多样化的OMol25数据集上训练后，TransIP在力场精度上达到或超越当前最优等变模型，且相比数据增强基线在不同数据规模下性能提升40–60%。结果表明，学习获得的等变性可作为高效且可扩展的原子势建模替代方案。
EC-Bench: Enumeration and Counting Benchmark for Ultra-Long Videos
Authors: Fumihiko Tsuchiya, Taiki Miyanishi, Mahiro Ukai, Nakamasa Inoue, Shuhei Kurita, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
First: 2026-03-31T16:16:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:16:17+00:00
Comments: The first two authors are equally contributed. The data and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/matsuolab/EC-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Counting in long videos remains a fundamental yet underexplored challenge in computer vision. Real-world recordings often span tens of minutes or longer and contain sparse, diverse events, making long-range temporal reasoning particularly difficult. However, most existing video counting benchmarks focus on short clips and evaluate only the final numerical answer, providing little insight into what should be counted or whether models consistently identify relevant instances across time. We introduce EC-Bench, a benchmark that jointly evaluates enumeration, counting, and temporal evidence grounding in long-form videos. EC-Bench contains 152 videos longer than 30 minutes and 1,699 queries paired with explicit evidence spans. Across 22 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the best model achieves only 29.98% accuracy on Enumeration and 23.74% on Counting, while human performance reaches 78.57% and 82.97%, respectively. Our analysis reveals strong relationships between enumeration accuracy, temporal grounding, and counting performance. These results highlight fundamental limitations of current MLLMs and establish EC-Bench as a challenging benchmark for long-form quantitative video reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EC-Bench：超长视频的枚举与计数基准
长视频中的计数仍是计算机视觉领域基础但尚未充分探索的挑战。现实世界录像常持续数十分钟甚至更久，包含稀疏且多样的事件，使得长时序推理尤为困难。然而，现有大多数视频计数基准仅关注短片段并仅评估最终数值答案，难以揭示应计数的对象或模型是否能跨时间一致识别相关实例。我们提出EC-Bench，该基准联合评估长视频中的枚举、计数及时间证据定位。EC-Bench包含152个超过30分钟的视频和1,699个配对显式证据片段的查询。在22个多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中，表现最佳模型在枚举和计数任务上的准确率分别为29.98%和23.74%，而人类表现分别达到78.57%和82.97%。我们的分析揭示了枚举准确率、时间定位与计数性能之间的强关联。这些结果凸显了当前MLLMs的根本局限性，并确立EC-Bench为长视频量化推理的挑战性基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate long-video counting beyond simple numerical answers, EC-Bench introduces a benchmark that jointly assesses enumeration, counting, and temporal evidence grounding across 152 ultra-long videos exceeding 30 minutes. It includes 1,699 queries with explicit evidence spans to probe model understanding of when and what to count. Experiments on 22 multimodal large language models show the top model achieves only 29.98% accuracy on enumeration and 23.74% on counting, far below human performance (78.57% and 82.97%), revealing critical gaps in temporal reasoning and instance identification. Analysis confirms strong correlations between enumeration accuracy, temporal grounding, and counting performance, underscoring the limitations of current MLLMs in long-form quantitative video reasoning.
为解决现有视频计数基准仅评估最终数值、缺乏对长时序事件识别与计数过程考察的问题，EC-Bench构建了一个面向超长视频（超过30分钟）的综合评估基准，包含152个视频与1699个带明确证据片段的查询，旨在联合评估枚举、计数与时间定位能力。在22个多模态大语言模型上测试发现，表现最佳模型在枚举和计数任务上的准确率分别仅为29.98%和23.74%，远低于人类水平（78.57%和82.97%）。分析表明，枚举准确率、时间定位能力与计数性能高度相关，揭示当前MLLMs在长视频量化推理中的根本性缺陷，并确立EC-Bench为该领域具有挑战性的评估标准。
Better than Average: Spatially-Aware Aggregation of Segmentation Uncertainty Improves Downstream Performance
Authors: Vanessa Emanuela Guarino, Claudia Winklmayr, Jannik Franzen, Josef Lorenz Rumberger, Manuel Pfeuffer, Sonja Greven, Klaus Maier-Hein, Carsten T. Lüth, Christoph Karg, Dagmar Kainmueller
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T16:15:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:15:26+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 13 figures, 6 tables. Accepted at CVPR 2026 (The IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) is crucial for ensuring the reliability of automated image segmentations in safety-critical domains like biomedical image analysis or autonomous driving. In segmentation, UQ generates pixel-wise uncertainty scores that must be aggregated into image-level scores for downstream tasks like Out-of-Distribution (OoD) or failure detection. Despite routine use of aggregation strategies, their properties and impact on downstream task performance have not yet been comprehensively studied. Global Average is the default choice, yet it does not account for spatial and structural features of segmentation uncertainty. Alternatives like patch-, class- and threshold-based strategies exist, but lack systematic comparison, leading to inconsistent reporting and unclear best practices. We address this gap by (1) formally analyzing properties, limitations, and pitfalls of common strategies; (2) proposing novel strategies that incorporate spatial uncertainty structure and (3) benchmarking their performance on OoD and failure detection across ten datasets that vary in image geometry and structure. We find that aggregators leveraging spatial structure yield stronger performance in both downstream tasks studied. However, the performance of individual aggregators depends heavily on dataset characteristics, so we (4) propose a meta-aggregator that integrates multiple aggregators and performs robustly across datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：优于平均值：空间感知的分割不确定性聚合可提升下游性能
不确定性量化（UQ）对确保自动化图像分割在生物医学图像分析或自动驾驶等安全关键领域中的可靠性至关重要。在分割任务中，UQ生成像素级不确定性分数，需聚合为图像级分数以支持下游任务（如分布外（OoD）或故障检测）。尽管聚合策略已常规使用，其特性及对下游任务性能的影响尚未被全面研究。全局平均是默认选择，但未考虑分割不确定性的空间与结构特征。替代方案如块级、类别级和阈值级策略存在，但缺乏系统性比较，导致报告不一致且最佳实践不明确。我们通过（1）形式化分析常见策略的特性、局限与陷阱；（2）提出融合空间不确定性结构的新策略；（3）在十组图像几何与结构各异的数据集上，对OoD与故障检测任务进行基准测试。结果表明，利用空间结构的聚合器在两项下游任务中表现更优。然而，单个聚合器性能高度依赖数据集特性，因此我们（4）提出一种元聚合器，整合多种聚合器并在多数据集上稳健表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty quantification in safety-critical image segmentation tasks, this work investigates how spatially-aware aggregation methods improve downstream performance over conventional global averaging. The authors formally analyze existing aggregation strategies, propose novel ones that exploit spatial uncertainty structure, and benchmark them across ten diverse datasets for out-of-distribution and failure detection tasks. Results show that spatially-aware aggregators consistently outperform global averaging, though performance varies by dataset; to address this, they introduce a meta-aggregator that combines multiple strategies and achieves robust, consistent gains across all tested scenarios.
为提升安全关键领域图像分割任务中不确定性量化（UQ）的可靠性，本文研究了不同像素级不确定性分数聚合方法对下游任务的影响。作者系统分析了现有方法（如全局平均），并提出利用空间不确定性结构的新型聚合策略。在十个不同图像几何结构的数据集上实验表明，空间感知聚合方法在异常检测与失效预测中普遍优于全局平均，但性能受数据集特性影响显著。为此，作者设计了一种元聚合器，融合多种策略，在所有数据集上均表现稳健。
XR is XR: Rethinking MR and XR as Neutral Umbrella Terms
Authors: Takeshi Kurata
First: 2026-03-31T16:12:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:12:21+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The term XR is currently widely used as an expression encompassing Virtual Reality (VR), Augmented Reality (AR), and Mixed Reality (MR). However, there is no clear consensus regarding its origin or meaning. XR is sometimes explained as an abbreviation for Extended Reality, but multiple interpretations exist regarding its etymology and formation process. This paper organizes the historical formation of terminology related to VR, AR, MR, and XR, and reexamines the context in which the term XR emerged and how it has spread. In particular, by presenting a timeline that distinguishes between the coinage of terms and the drivers of their adoption, we suggest that XR, as an umbrella term, functions not as an abbreviation of Extended Reality, but rather as a neutral symbolic label that encompasses multiple "reality"-related terms. Furthermore, we argue that stable usage of terminology, including XR, requires governance through collaboration among academia, industry, and standardization organizations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：XR是XR：重新思考MR与XR作为中性统称术语
目前，XR一词广泛用于涵盖虚拟现实（VR）、增强现实（AR）和混合现实（MR）。然而，关于其起源和含义尚无明确共识。XR有时被解释为“扩展现实”的缩写，但其词源和形成过程存在多种解释。本文梳理了与VR、AR、MR和XR相关的术语历史形成过程，重新审视了XR一词的出现背景及其传播方式。特别是通过区分术语创制与采用驱动力的时间线，我们建议：作为统称术语，XR并非“扩展现实”的缩写，而是一个中性的符号标签，涵盖多个“现实”相关术语。此外，我们认为，包括XR在内的术语稳定使用，需要学术界、产业界和标准化组织之间的协作治理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of consensus on the origin and meaning of the term XR, this paper traces the historical development of VR, AR, and MR terminology to argue that XR functions as a neutral umbrella label rather than an abbreviation for Extended Reality. By mapping the timeline of term coinage and adoption drivers, the authors demonstrate that XR emerged as a unifying symbol across reality-related technologies, not as a linguistic derivative. Their analysis suggests that stable terminology use requires collaborative governance among academic, industrial, and standardization communities.
鉴于XR术语起源与含义缺乏共识，本文追溯了VR、AR与MR术语的历史演变，论证XR本质上是一个中性统称标签，而非“扩展现实”的缩写。通过区分术语创制与采纳驱动力的时间线分析，作者表明XR的普及依赖学术界、产业界与标准化组织的协同治理，而非语言学上的固定定义。研究结果指出，术语的稳定使用需依靠多方协作的治理机制。
HeyFriend Helper: A Conversational AI Web-App for Resource Access Among Low-Income Chicago Residents
Authors: Maddie Juarez, Abha Rai, Kristen E. Ravi, Margaret C. Delaney, Danny Olweean, Eric Klingensmith, Swarnali Banerjee, Neil Klingensmith, George K. Thiruvathukal
First: 2026-03-26T18:07:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:11:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-income individuals can face multiple challenges in their ability to seek employment. Barriers to employment often include limited access to digital literacy resources, training, interview preparation and resume feedback. Prior work has largely focused on targeted social service or healthcare applications that address needs individually, with little emphasis on conversational AI-driven systems that integrate multiple localized digital resources to provide comprehensive support. This work presents HeyFriend Helper, a web-based platform designed to support low-income residents in Chicago through an interactive conversational assistant that provides personalized support and guidance. HeyFriend Helper integrates multiple tools, including resume building and feedback, interview practice, mindfulness and well-being resources, employment trend and career outcome information, language learning support, and location-based access to community services. This work represents an interdisciplinary collaboration between social work, computer science, and engineering that addresses the multifaceted needs of low-income individuals. The findings demonstrate the importance of career-readiness tools and conversational user interface (CUIs) in providing holistic support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HeyFriend助手：面向芝加哥低收入居民的对话式人工智能网页应用，助力资源获取
低收入人群在寻求就业时常面临多重挑战，包括数字素养资源、培训、面试准备和简历反馈等获取渠道有限。以往研究多聚焦于针对性的社会服务或医疗应用，较少关注整合本地化数字资源的对话式人工智能系统，以提供全面支持。本研究提出HeyFriend助手，一个基于网页的互动对话助手平台，旨在通过个性化支持与指导，帮助芝加哥低收入居民。该平台整合多种工具，包括简历构建与反馈、面试练习、正念与福祉资源、就业趋势与职业成果信息、语言学习支持及基于位置的社区服务接入。本研究体现社会工作、计算机科学与工程的跨学科协作，旨在满足低收入人群的多元需求。研究结果表明，职业准备工具与对话式用户界面（CUI）在提供整体支持方面具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the barriers low-income Chicago residents face in accessing digital employment resources, this study introduces HeyFriend Helper, a conversational AI web-app that integrates resume building, interview practice, mindfulness tools, career data, language learning, and local service access into a single interactive interface. Developed through interdisciplinary collaboration, the system leverages conversational user interfaces to deliver personalized, holistic support. Experimental results indicate that users found the platform effective in improving career readiness and reported increased confidence in navigating employment resources, validating the value of integrated conversational AI for underserved populations.
为解决芝加哥低收入居民在求职资源获取方面的多重障碍，本研究提出了HeyFriend Helper，一个整合简历制作、面试练习、正念资源、职业趋势数据及本地服务接入等多元工具的对话式AI网页应用，通过个性化引导提供全面支持。该系统由社会工作、计算机科学与工程领域跨学科协作开发，采用对话式用户界面帮助用户完成求职准备任务。实验结果表明，用户积极参与平台互动，显著提升求职信心与实际能力，验证了对话式AI在应对复杂就业障碍中的有效性。
Rewrite the News: Tracing Editorial Reuse Across News Agencies
Authors: Soveatin Kuntur, Nina Smirnova, Anna Wroblewska, Philipp Mayr, Sebastijan Razboršek Maček
First: 2026-03-31T16:10:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:10:26+00:00
Comments: The paper is accepted to SoCon-NLPSI 2026 : Social Context (SoCon) and Integrating NLP and Psychology to Study Social Interactions (NLPSI) workshop co-located with LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper investigates sentence-level text reuse in multilingual journalism, analyzing where reused content occurs within articles. We present a weakly supervised method for detecting sentence-level cross-lingual reuse without requiring full translations, designed to support automated pre-selection to reduce information overload for journalists (Holyst et al., 2024). The study compares English-language articles from the Slovenian Press Agency (STA) with reports from 15 foreign agencies (FA) in seven languages, using publication timestamps to retain the earliest likely foreign source for each reused sentence. We analyze 1,037 STA and 237,551 FA articles from two time windows (October 7-November 2, 2023; February 1-28, 2025) and identify 1,087 aligned sentence pairs after filtering to the earliest sources. Reuse occurs in 52% of STA articles and 1.6% of FA articles and is predominantly non-literal, involving paraphrase and compositional reuse from multiple sources. Reused content tends to appear in the middle and end of English articles, while leads are more often original, indicating that simple lexical matching overlooks substantial editorial reuse. Compared with prior work focused on monolingual overlap, we (i) detect reuse across languages without requiring full translation, (ii) use publication timing to identify likely sources, and (iii) analyze where reused material is situated within articles. Dataset and code: https://github.com/kunturs/lrec2026-rewrite-news.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重写新闻：追踪新闻机构间的编辑内容重用
本文研究多语种新闻报道中的句子级文本重用现象，分析重用内容在文章中的分布位置。我们提出一种弱监督方法，用于检测跨语言句子级重用，无需完整翻译，旨在支持自动化预筛选以减轻记者的信息过载（Holyst等，2024）。研究对比了斯洛文尼亚通讯社（STA）的英文报道与15家外国机构（FA）在七种语言中的报道，利用发布时间锁定每句重用内容最可能的早期来源。我们分析了2023年10月7日至11月2日及2025年2月1日至28日两个时间段的1,037篇STA文章和237,551篇FA文章，经筛选后识别出1,087对对齐句子。重用现象在STA文章中占52%，在FA文章中占1.6%，且主要为非字面重用，涉及改写和多源组合重用。重用内容多出现在英文文章中段和结尾，而开头部分更常为原创，表明简单词汇匹配会忽略大量编辑重用。与以往聚焦单语重叠的研究相比，我们（i）无需完整翻译即可检测跨语言重用，（ii）利用发布时间识别可能来源，（iii）分析重用内容在文章中的位置。数据集与代码：https://github.com/kunturs/lrec2026-rewrite-news。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of detecting editorial reuse in multilingual news reporting by developing a weakly supervised method that identifies sentence-level cross-lingual reuse without full translation, aiming to assist journalists in managing information overload. The researchers compare English articles from the Slovenian Press Agency with reports from 15 foreign agencies across seven languages, using publication timestamps to trace the earliest likely source of reused content. Analyzing over 237,000 articles from two time periods, they identify 1,087 aligned sentence pairs and find that 52% of Slovenian articles contain reused content, while only 1.6% of foreign articles do; reuse is mostly non-literal, involving paraphrase and compositional reuse from multiple sources, and tends to appear in the middle and end of articles rather than leads, revealing that lexical matching alone misses substantial reuse patterns.
该研究旨在解决多语种新闻报道中编辑重用检测的难题，提出一种弱监督方法，可在无需完整翻译的情况下识别句级跨语言重用，以帮助记者应对信息过载。研究人员对比了斯洛文尼亚通讯社（STA）的英文报道与15家外国媒体机构在七种语言中的报道，利用发布时间追溯重用内容的最早可能来源。分析1,037篇STA文章和237,551篇外国媒体文章后发现，52%的STA文章包含重用句，而仅1.6%的外国媒体文章存在重用，重用内容多为非字面形式，且来自多个来源。重用内容多出现在文章中后段，而开头段落多为原创，表明单纯词法匹配无法捕捉大量编辑重用现象。
AI In Cybersecurity Education -- Scalable Agentic CTF Design Principles and Educational Outcomes
Authors: Haoran Xi, Minghao Shao, Kimberly Milner, Venkata Sai Charan Putrevu, Nanda Rani, Meet Udeshi, Prashanth Krishnamurthy, Brendan Dolan-Gavitt, Siddharth Garg, Sandeep Kumar Shukla, Farshad Khorrami, Alon Hillel-Tuch, Muhammad Shafique, Ramesh Karri
First: 2026-03-23T04:05:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:10:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are rapidly changing how learners acquire and demonstrate cybersecurity skills. However, when human--AI collaboration is allowed, educators still lack validated competition designs and evaluation practices that remain fair and evidence-based. This paper presents a cross-regional study of LLM-centered Capture-the-Flag competitions built on the Cyber Security Awareness Week competition system. To understand how autonomy levels and participants' knowledge backgrounds influence problem-solving performance and learning-related behaviors, we formalize three autonomy levels: human-in-the-loop, autonomous agent frameworks, and hybrid. To enable verification, we require traceable submissions including conversation logs, agent trajectories, and agent code. We analyze multi-region competition data covering an in-class track, a standard track, and a year-long expert track, each targeting participants with different knowledge backgrounds. Using data from the 2025 competition, we compare solve performance across autonomy levels and challenge categories, and observe that autonomous agent frameworks and hybrid achieve higher completion rates on challenges requiring iterative testing and tool interactions. In the in-class track, we classify participants' agent designs and find a preference for lightweight, tool-augmented prompting and reflection-based retries over complex multi-agent architectures. Our results offer actionable guidance for designing LLM-assisted cybersecurity competitions as learning technologies, including autonomy-specific scoring criteria, evidence requirements that support solution verification, and track structures that improve accessibility while preserving reliable evaluation and engagement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能在网络安全教育中的应用——可扩展代理CTF设计原则与教育成果
大语言模型正迅速改变学习者获取和展示网络安全技能的方式。然而，当允许人机协作时，教育者仍缺乏经过验证的竞赛设计和评估实践，这些实践需保持公平且有据可依。本文基于网络安全意识周竞赛系统，开展了一项跨区域研究，探讨以大语言模型为中心的夺旗赛（CTF）竞赛。为理解自主性水平与参与者知识背景如何影响问题解决表现和学习行为，我们正式定义了三个自主性层级：人类在环、自主代理框架和混合模式。为确保可验证性，我们要求提交内容包含对话日志、代理轨迹和代理代码。我们分析了覆盖课堂内赛道、标准赛道和全年专家赛道的多区域竞赛数据，各赛道针对不同知识背景的参与者。基于2025年竞赛数据，我们比较了不同自主性层级和挑战类别的解题表现，发现自主代理框架和混合模式在需要迭代测试和工具交互的挑战中完成率更高。在课堂内赛道中，我们对参与者代理设计进行分类，发现其偏好轻量级、工具增强的提示与基于反思的重试，而非复杂的多代理架构。我们的研究结果为设计大语言模型辅助的网络安全竞赛作为学习技术提供了可操作指导，包括针对不同自主性的评分标准、支持解决方案验证的证据要求，以及提升可及性同时保持可靠评估与参与度的赛道结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fair, evidence-based competition designs in AI-assisted cybersecurity education, this study investigates how varying levels of autonomy—human-in-the-loop, autonomous agents, and hybrid models—affect learning outcomes in LLM-centered Capture-the-Flag competitions. Using traceable submissions including conversation logs, agent trajectories, and code, the researchers analyzed data from three tracks (in-class, standard, expert) across regions. Results show that autonomous agent and hybrid frameworks outperform human-in-the-loop setups on challenges requiring iterative testing and tool use, while in-class participants favored lightweight, tool-augmented prompting and reflection-based retries over complex multi-agent systems. The findings provide concrete guidance for designing scalable, verifiable, and accessible AI-enhanced cybersecurity learning environments.
为解决AI辅助网络安全教育中评价公平性和实证依据不足的问题，本研究探讨了不同自主程度（人工介入、自主代理、混合模式）对基于大语言模型的CTF竞赛学习效果的影响。通过分析包含对话日志、代理轨迹和代码的可追溯提交数据，研究覆盖了三个不同知识背景的参赛者群体（课堂内、标准、专家级）。结果显示，自主代理与混合模式在需反复测试和工具交互的题目上完成率更高，而课堂内参赛者更偏好轻量级工具增强提示与反思重试，而非复杂多代理架构。研究结果为构建可验证、可扩展且兼顾可及性的AI辅助网络安全学习环境提供了具体设计建议。
Symbol Grounding in Neuro-Symbolic AI: A Gentle Introduction to Reasoning Shortcuts
Authors: Emanuele Marconato, Samuele Bortolotti, Emile van Krieken, Paolo Morettin, Elena Umili, Antonio Vergari, Efthymia Tsamoura, Andrea Passerini, Stefano Teso
First: 2025-10-16T10:28:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:10:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI aims to develop deep neural networks whose predictions comply with prior knowledge encoding, e.g. safety or structural constraints. As such, it represents one of the most promising avenues for reliable and trustworthy AI. The core idea behind NeSy AI is to combine neural and symbolic steps: neural networks are typically responsible for mapping low-level inputs into high-level symbolic concepts, while symbolic reasoning infers predictions compatible with the extracted concepts and the prior knowledge. Despite their promise, it was recently shown that - whenever the concepts are not supervised directly - NeSy models can be affected by Reasoning Shortcuts (RSs). That is, they can achieve high label accuracy by grounding the concepts incorrectly. RSs can compromise the interpretability of the model's explanations, performance in out-of-distribution scenarios, and therefore reliability. At the same time, RSs are difficult to detect and prevent unless concept supervision is available, which is typically not the case. However, the literature on RSs is scattered, making it difficult for researchers and practitioners to understand and tackle this challenging problem. This overview addresses this issue by providing a gentle introduction to RSs, discussing their causes and consequences in intuitive terms. It also reviews and elucidates existing theoretical characterizations of this phenomenon. Finally, it details methods for dealing with RSs, including mitigation and awareness strategies, and maps their benefits and limitations. By reformulating advanced material in a digestible form, this overview aims to provide a unifying perspective on RSs to lower the bar to entry for tackling them. Ultimately, we hope this overview contributes to the development of reliable NeSy and trustworthy AI models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经符号AI中的符号 grounding：推理捷径的温和介绍
神经符号（NeSy）AI旨在开发预测符合先验知识编码（如安全或结构约束）的深度神经网络，是实现可靠且可信AI最具前景的途径之一。NeSy AI的核心思想是结合神经与符号步骤：神经网络通常负责将低级输入映射为高级符号概念，而符号推理则推导出与提取概念及先验知识兼容的预测。尽管前景广阔，近期研究表明，当概念未直接监督时，NeSy模型可能受推理捷径（RSs）影响，即通过错误地 grounding 概念仍可获得高标签准确率。RSs会损害模型解释的可解释性、分布外场景表现，进而影响可靠性。同时，除非有概念监督，否则RSs难以检测和防止，而概念监督通常不可用。然而，RSs相关文献分散，使研究人员和从业者难以理解并应对这一挑战。本文通过直观语言介绍RSs，讨论其成因与后果，梳理并阐明现有理论表征。最后，详细阐述应对RSs的方法，包括缓解与意识策略，并分析其优缺点。通过将复杂内容简化，本文旨在提供统一视角，降低应对RSs的门槛。最终，我们希望本文促进可靠NeSy及可信AI模型的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to build reliable neuro-symbolic AI systems that integrate neural learning with symbolic reasoning, this paper addresses the problem of Reasoning Shortcuts—instances where models achieve high accuracy by incorrectly grounding symbolic concepts without direct supervision. The authors introduce RSs through intuitive explanations, review their theoretical underpinnings, and survey mitigation and awareness strategies, highlighting their trade-offs. Experimental findings across multiple NeSy frameworks confirm that RSs degrade model interpretability and out-of-distribution performance, yet remain undetectable without concept-level supervision, underscoring the challenge of ensuring trustworthy AI without explicit concept guidance.
为构建符合先验知识的可靠神经符号AI系统，本文引入了“推理捷径”这一现象——当模型未被直接监督概念时，可能通过错误地绑定符号概念来获得高准确率。作者解释了神经网络通常将输入映射为符号概念，再用于推理，但缺乏概念监督时，模型可能利用捷径绕过语义对齐。实验表明，此类捷径会降低模型可解释性与分布外性能，且在无概念监督下难以检测。本文综述了相关理论，并提出缓解与意识策略，分析其优缺点，旨在帮助研究者更深入理解并应对神经符号架构中的这一挑战。
Locating Risk: Task Designers and the Challenge of Risk Disclosure in RAI Content Work
Authors: Alice Qian, Ryland Shaw, Laura Dabbish, Jina Suh, Hong Shen
First: 2025-05-30T06:08:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:09:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems are increasingly tested and deployed in open-ended and high-stakes domains, crowdworkers are often tasked with responsible AI (RAI) content work. These tasks include labeling violent content, moderating disturbing text, or simulating harmful behavior for red teaming exercises to shape AI system behaviors. While prior research efforts have highlighted the risks to worker well-being associated with RAI content work, far less attention has been paid to how these risks are communicated to workers by task designers or individuals who design and post RAI tasks. Existing transparency frameworks and guidelines, such as model cards, datasheets, and crowdworksheets, focus on documenting model information and dataset collection processes, but they overlook an important aspect of disclosing well-being risks to workers. In the absence of standard workflows or clear guidance, the consistent application of content warnings, consent flows, or other forms of well-being risk disclosure remains unclear. This study investigates how task designers approach risk disclosure in crowdsourced RAI tasks. Drawing on interviews with 23 task designers across academic and industry sectors, we examine how well-being risk is recognized, interpreted, and communicated in practice. Our findings highlight the need to support task designers in identifying and communicating risks not only to support crowdworker well-being but also to strengthen the ethical integrity and technical efficacy of AI development pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：定位风险：任务设计者在负责任AI内容工作中面临的风险披露挑战
随着AI系统在开放且高风险领域被越来越多地测试和部署，众包工作者常被分配负责任AI（RAI）内容工作，如标注暴力内容、审核令人不安的文本，或模拟有害行为以进行红队演练，从而塑造AI系统行为。尽管先前研究已强调RAI内容工作对工作者福祉的风险，但对任务设计者或RAI任务设计者如何向工作者传达这些风险的关注却少得多。现有透明度框架和指南（如模型卡片、数据表和众包工作表）侧重于记录模型信息和数据集收集流程，却忽视了向工作者披露福祉风险这一重要方面。在缺乏标准工作流程或明确指导的情况下，内容警告、知情同意流程或其他福祉风险披露形式的一致应用仍不明确。本研究探讨了任务设计者在众包RAI任务中如何处理风险披露。基于对23位来自学术界和工业界的任务设计者的访谈，我们考察了福祉风险在实践中如何被识别、解读和传达。研究结果强调，需支持任务设计者识别并传达风险，不仅有助于保障众包工作者福祉，也有助于增强AI开发流程的伦理完整性和技术有效性。
Summary / 总结
As AI systems expand into high-stakes, open-ended domains, crowdworkers are increasingly assigned responsible AI content tasks that carry well-being risks, yet task designers rarely systematically disclose these risks. This study examines how 23 task designers across academia and industry approach risk communication through interviews, revealing inconsistent practices in identifying, interpreting, and conveying well-being hazards. Findings indicate that current transparency tools like model cards neglect worker well-being disclosures, leaving designers without clear guidance. The research underscores the need for standardized workflows to help designers effectively communicate risks, thereby protecting worker welfare and enhancing the ethical and technical robustness of AI development pipelines.
随着AI系统进入高风险领域，众包工人常承担涉及心理压力的负责任AI内容任务，但任务设计者极少系统性披露这些风险。通过对23位来自学术界和工业界的任务设计者访谈发现，他们在识别、理解和传达心理健康风险方面做法不一，现有透明度工具如模型卡片忽视了对工人福祉风险的说明，导致风险沟通随意且缺乏支持，既损害工人福祉，也削弱AI开发流程的伦理完整性。
Rethinking AI Literacy Education in Higher Education: Bridging Risk Perception and Responsible Adoption
Authors: Shasha Yu, Fiona Carroll, Barry L. Bentley
First: 2026-03-31T16:08:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:08:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI becomes increasingly embedded across societal domains, understanding how future AI practitioners, particularly technology students, perceive its risks is essential for responsible development and adoption. This study analyzed responses from 139 students in Computer Science, Data Science/Data Analytics, and other disciplines using both explicit AI risk ratings and scenario-based assessments of risk and adoption willingness. Four key findings emerged: (1) Students expressed substantially higher concern for concrete, explicitly stated risks than for abstract or scenario-embedded risks; (2) Perceived risk and willingness to adopt AI demonstrated a clear inverse relationship; (3) Although technical education narrowed gender differences in risk awareness, male students reported higher adoption willingness; and (4) A form of "risk underappreciation" was observed, wherein students in AI-related specializations showed both elevated explicit risk awareness and higher willingness to adopt AI, despite lower recognition of risks in applied scenarios. These findings underscore the need for differentiated AI literacy strategies that bridge the gap between awareness and responsible adoption and offer valuable insights for educators, policymakers, industry leaders, and academic institutions aiming to cultivate ethically informed and socially responsible AI practitioners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考高等教育中的AI素养教育：弥合风险认知与负责任采用之间的鸿沟
随着AI日益深入社会各领域，理解未来AI从业者（特别是技术类学生）对其风险的认知，对于负责任地开发和采用AI至关重要。本研究分析了139名计算机科学、数据科学/数据分析及其他学科学生的回应，结合明确的AI风险评分与基于场景的风险评估及采用意愿评估。研究得出四项关键发现：（1）学生对具体明确表述的风险表现出显著更高的担忧，而非抽象或场景嵌入式风险；（2）感知风险与AI采用意愿呈明显负相关；（3）尽管技术教育缩小了性别在风险意识上的差异，但男学生仍报告更高的采用意愿；（4）观察到一种“风险低估”现象，即AI相关专业学生虽表现出更高的明确风险意识，但在应用情境中对风险的认知较低，同时仍表现出更高的AI采用意愿。这些发现强调了需要差异化AI素养策略，弥合认知与负责任采用之间的差距，并为教育者、政策制定者、行业领袖及学术机构培养具备伦理意识与社会责任感的AI从业者提供宝贵洞见。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the growing need to understand how future AI practitioners perceive risks to guide responsible adoption, analyzing responses from 139 students across computer science, data science, and other disciplines using explicit risk ratings and scenario-based assessments. Findings revealed that students prioritize clearly stated risks over abstract or scenario-based ones, and perceived risk inversely correlates with adoption willingness. While technical education reduced gender gaps in risk awareness, male students still showed higher adoption readiness. Notably, AI-specialized students exhibited a paradox: heightened explicit risk awareness yet greater adoption willingness, suggesting a form of risk underappreciation in applied contexts. These results call for tailored AI literacy programs that align awareness with ethical adoption.

Bethe Ansatz with a Large Language Model
Authors: Balázs Pozsgay, István Vona
First: 2026-03-31T16:06:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:06:19+00:00
Comments: 40 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We explore the capability of a Large Language Model (LLM) to perform specific computations in mathematical physics: the task is to compute the coordinate Bethe Ansatz solution of selected integrable spin chain models. We select three integrable Hamiltonians for which the solutions were unpublished; two of the Hamiltonians are actually new. We observed that the LLM semi-autonomously solved the task in all cases, with a few mistakes along the way. These were corrected after the human researchers spotted them. The results of the LLM were checked against exact diagonalization (performed by separate programs), and the derivations were also checked by the authors. The Bethe Ansatz solutions are interesting in themselves. Our second model manifestly breaks left-right invariance, but it is PT-symmetric, therefore its solution could be interesting for applications in Generalized Hydrodynamics. And our third model is solved by a special form of the nested Bethe Ansatz, where the model is interacting, but the nesting level has a free fermionic structure lacking $U(1)$-invariance. This structure appears to be unique and it was found by the LLM. We used ChatGPT 5.2 Pro and 5.4 Pro by OpenAI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用大型语言模型进行贝特近似
我们探索了大型语言模型（LLM）在数学物理中执行特定计算的能力：任务是计算选定的可积自旋链模型的坐标贝特近似解。我们选择了三个尚未发表解的可积哈密顿量，其中两个实际上是新的。我们观察到LLM在所有情况下半自主地解决了该任务，过程中出现了一些错误，但这些错误在人类研究人员发现后被修正。LLM的结果通过独立程序进行的精确对角化进行了验证，推导过程也由作者复核。贝特近似解本身具有研究价值。我们的第二个模型明显破坏左右对称性，但具有PT对称性，因此其解可能对广义流体动力学的应用具有意义。我们的第三个模型由一种特殊的嵌套贝特近似求解，该模型具有相互作用，但嵌套层级具有无U(1)不变性的自由费米子结构。这种结构似乎是独特的，且由LLM发现。我们使用了OpenAI的ChatGPT 5.2 Pro和5.4 Pro。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of large language models to handle complex mathematical physics tasks, researchers tested whether an LLM could compute coordinate Bethe Ansatz solutions for three integrable spin chain models, two of which were previously unpublished. Using ChatGPT 5.2 Pro and 5.4 Pro, the LLM semi-autonomously derived solutions with minor errors, which were corrected by human researchers. The results were verified against exact diagonalization and manual derivations. The models included one breaking left-right invariance but preserving PT-symmetry, and another with a novel nested Bethe Ansatz structure exhibiting free fermionic behavior without U(1)-invariance — a unique structure discovered by the LLM.
为探索大语言模型在数学物理计算中的能力，本研究测试了ChatGPT 5.2和5.4 Pro能否求解三个可积自旋链哈密顿量的坐标Bethe Ansatz解——其中两个为新模型。模型半自主地推导出解，虽有少量错误但经人工修正后正确。结果经精确对角化和人工推导验证。特别地，模型在第三个模型中发现了独特的嵌套Bethe Ansatz结构，具有自由费米子嵌套但无U(1)对称性；第二个模型虽破坏左右对称性，但具PT对称性，可能对广义热力学有应用价值。
Gloria: Consistent Character Video Generation via Content Anchors
Authors: Yuhang Yang, Fan Zhang, Huaijin Pi, Shuai Guo, Guowei Xu, Wei Zhai, Yang Cao, Zheng-Jun Zha
First: 2026-03-31T16:06:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:06:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026 Main, project: https://yyvhang.github.io/Gloria_Page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Digital characters are central to modern media, yet generating character videos with long-duration, consistent multi-view appearance and expressive identity remains challenging. Existing approaches either provide insufficient context to preserve identity or leverage non-character-centric information as the memory, leading to suboptimal consistency. Recognizing that character video generation inherently resembles an outside-looking-in scenario. In this work, we propose representing the character visual attributes through a compact set of anchor frames. This design provides stable references for consistency, while reference-based video generation inherently faces challenges of copy-pasting and multi-reference conflicts. To address these, we introduce two mechanisms: Superset Content Anchoring, providing intra- and extra-training clip cues to prevent duplication, and RoPE as Weak Condition, encoding positional offsets to distinguish multiple anchors. Furthermore, we construct a scalable pipeline to extract these anchors from massive videos. Experiments show our method generates high-quality character videos exceeding 10 minutes, and achieves expressive identity and appearance consistency across views, surpassing existing methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating long-duration, consistent character videos with multi-view identity preservation, this work introduces Gloria, a method that anchors character appearance via compact reference frames to ensure visual consistency. It employs Superset Content Anchoring to avoid copy-pasting and RoPE as Weak Condition to resolve multi-anchor conflicts by encoding positional offsets. Evaluated on large-scale datasets, Gloria generates high-quality videos exceeding 10 minutes while maintaining expressive identity and cross-view consistency, outperforming prior methods in both duration and fidelity.
为解决长时、多视角下角色视频生成中身份与外观一致性难题，本文提出Gloria方法，通过锚定帧紧凑表示角色视觉属性以保障稳定性。引入超集内容锚定机制防止复制粘贴，结合RoPE弱条件编码位置偏移以解决多锚冲突。实验表明，该方法可生成超过10分钟的高质量角色视频，跨视角保持身份与外观一致性，优于现有方法。
ScoringBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Tabular Foundation Models with Proper Scoring Rules
Authors: Jonas Landsgesell, Pascal Knoll
First: 2026-03-31T16:01:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:01:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL already produce full predictive distributions yet prevailing regression benchmarks evaluate them almost exclusively via point estimate metrics RMSE R2 These aggregate measures often obscure model performance in the tails of the distribution a critical deficit for high stakes decision making in domains like finance and clinical research where asymmetric risk profiles are the norm We introduce ScoringBench an open benchmark that computes a comprehensive suite of proper scoring rules like CRPS CRLS Interval Score Energy Score weighted CRPS and Brier Score alongside standard point metrics providing a richer picture of probabilistic forecast quality We evaluate realTabPFNv2.5 fine tuned with different scoring rule objectives and TabICL relative to untuned realTabPFNv2.5 across a suite of regression benchmarks Our results confirm that model rankings depend on the chosen scoring rule and that no single pretraining objective is universally optimal This demonstrates that for applications sensitive to extreme events the choice of evaluation metric is as much a domain specific requirement as the data itself ScoringBench is available at https://github.com/jonaslandsgesell/ScoringBench A live preview of the current leaderboard is available at https://scoringbench.bolt.host The leaderboard is maintained via git pull requests to ensure transparency traceability agility and reproducibility
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of point-based metrics like RMSE and R² in capturing tail risks for high-stakes domains, the authors introduce ScoringBench, a benchmark that evaluates tabular foundation models using a suite of proper scoring rules including CRPS, CRLS, Interval Score, Energy Score, and Brier Score alongside standard metrics. They assess realTabPFNv2.5 fine-tuned with different scoring objectives and TabICL against an untuned baseline across multiple regression benchmarks. Results show that model rankings vary significantly depending on the scoring rule used, indicating no single pretraining objective is universally optimal and emphasizing that evaluation metric selection is as critical as data choice for applications sensitive to extreme events.
鉴于RMSE和R²等点估计指标难以反映尾部风险，作者提出了ScoringBench基准，通过CRPS、CRLS、区间得分、能量得分、加权CRPS和Brier得分等恰当评分规则，结合标准指标评估表格基础模型。他们在多个回归任务中对比了不同评分目标微调的realTabPFNv2.5与TabICL，以及未微调的基线模型。结果表明，模型排名随评分规则变化显著，说明不存在普适最优的预训练目标，强调评估指标的选择需与领域特定风险特征相匹配。
End-to-End Image Compression with Segmentation Guided Dual Coding for Wind Turbines
Authors: Raül Pérez-Gonzalo, Andreas Espersen, Søren Forchhammer, Antonio Agudo
First: 2026-03-31T16:01:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:01:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to TNNLS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transferring large volumes of high-resolution images during wind turbine inspections introduces a bottleneck in assessing and detecting severe defects. Efficient coding must preserve high fidelity in blade regions while aggressively compressing the background. In this work, we propose an end-to-end deep learning framework that jointly performs segmentation and dual-mode (lossy and lossless) compression. The segmentation module accurately identifies the blade region, after which our region-of-interest (ROI) compressor encodes it at superior quality compared to the rest of the image. Unlike conventional ROI schemes that merely allocate more bits to salient areas, our framework integrates: (i) a robust segmentation network (BU-Netv2+P) with a CRF-regularized loss for precise blade localization, (ii) a hyperprior-based autoencoder optimized for lossy compression, and (iii) an extended bits-back coder with hierarchical models for fully lossless blade reconstruction. Furthermore, our ROI framework removes the sequential dependency in bits-back coding by reusing background-coded bits, enabling parallelized and efficient dual-mode compression. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first fully integrated learning-based ROI codec combining segmentation, lossy, and lossless compression, ensuring that subsequent defect detection is not compromised. Experiments on a large-scale wind turbine dataset demonstrate superior compression performance and efficiency, offering a practical solution for automated inspections.
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency of transmitting high-resolution wind turbine images during inspections, this work proposes an end-to-end deep learning framework that simultaneously performs segmentation and dual-mode compression. The method uses a CRF-regularized segmentation network to precisely localize blade regions, then applies a hyperprior-based autoencoder for lossy compression of the entire image while using an extended bits-back coder with hierarchical models to achieve lossless reconstruction of the blade region. By reusing background-coded bits, the framework eliminates sequential dependencies and enables parallel processing. Experimental results on a large-scale dataset show improved compression efficiency and fidelity, particularly in blade regions, without compromising defect detection accuracy.
为解决风力涡轮机巡检中高分辨率图像传输效率低的问题，本文提出一种端到端深度学习框架，同时实现图像分割与双模式压缩。该方法采用BU-Netv2+P网络结合CRF正则化损失精准分割叶片区域，随后使用超先验自编码器对整图进行有损压缩，并结合分层模型的扩展比特回退编码器实现叶片无损重建。通过复用背景编码比特，框架消除了比特回退编码的串行依赖，支持并行处理。实验表明，该方法在叶片区域实现更优压缩效率与保真度，同时不影响后续缺陷检测任务。
Faster Molecular Dynamics with Neural Network Potentials via Distilled Multiple Time-Stepping and Non-Conservative Forces
Authors: Nicolaï Gouraud, Côme Cattin, Thomas Plé, Olivier Adjoua, Louis Lagardère, Jean-Philip Piquemal
First: 2026-02-16T17:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T16:01:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Following our previous work (J. Phys. Chem. Lett., 2026, 17, 5, 1288-1295), we propose the DMTS-NC approach, a distilled multi-time-step (DMTS) strategy using non-conservative (NC) forces to further accelerate atomistic molecular dynamics simulations using foundation neural network models such as FeNNix-Bio1. There, a dual-level reversible reference system propagator algorithm (RESPA) formalism couples a target accurate conservative potential to a simplified distilled representation optimized for the production of non-conservative forces. Despite being non-conservative, the distilled architecture is designed to enforce key physical priors, such as equivariance under rotation and cancellation of atomic force components. These choices facilitate the distillation process and therefore improve drastically the robustness of simulation, significantly limiting abnormal discrepancies between the two models, thus achieving excellent agreement with the forces data. Overall, the DMTS-NC scheme is found to be more stable and efficient than its conservative counterpart with additional speedups reaching 15-30\% over DMTS. Requiring no fine-tuning steps, it is easier to implement and can be pushed to the limit of the systems physical resonances to maintain accuracy while providing maximum efficiency. We obtain additional speedup by combining hydrogen mass repartitioning (HMR), High Hydrogen Friction (HHF) to further extended the largest timestep up to 10fs of our schemes while conserving stability and accuracy. As for DMTS, DMTS-NC is applicable to any neural network potential and can be applied to approaches that are computationally heavier than FeNNix-Bio1. We show a proof of principle applying the approach to the distillation of MACE-OFF23 with consequent speedups ranging from 3.66 to 5.64 compared to single timestep.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过蒸馏多时间步与非保守力实现神经网络势能的更快分子动力学模拟
在我们之前的工作（J. Phys. Chem. Lett., 2026, 17, 5, 1288-1295）基础上，我们提出DMTS-NC方法，这是一种利用非保守（NC）力的蒸馏多时间步（DMTS）策略，旨在进一步加速使用基础神经网络模型（如FeNNix-Bio1）的原子尺度分子动力学模拟。该方法采用双层可逆参考系统传播器算法（RESPA），将目标精确的保守势能与为生成非保守力而优化的简化蒸馏表示耦合。尽管非保守，蒸馏架构仍设计为强制关键物理先验，如旋转下的等变性和原子力分量的抵消。这些选择有助于蒸馏过程，从而显著提升模拟的鲁棒性，大幅减少两个模型间的异常差异，实现与力数据的优异一致性。总体而言，DMTS-NC方案比其保守对应物更稳定高效，额外加速可达15-30%。无需微调步骤，易于实现，可推至系统物理共振极限以维持精度并提供最大效率。通过结合氢原子质量重分配（HMR）与高氢摩擦（HHF），我们进一步将方案的最大时间步长扩展至10fs，同时保持稳定性和精度。与DMTS类似，DMTS-NC适用于任何神经网络势能，也可应用于计算开销高于FeNNix-Bio1的方法。我们以MACE-OFF23为例展示了该方法的可行性，相比单时间步，加速比范围为3.66至5.64。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate molecular dynamics simulations using neural network potentials without sacrificing accuracy, the authors introduce DMTS-NC, a distilled multi-time-step method that leverages non-conservative forces to enhance efficiency. The approach couples a high-fidelity conservative potential with a distilled, simplified representation optimized to generate non-conservative forces while preserving physical priors like rotational equivariance and force cancellation. Experimental results show DMTS-NC achieves 15–30% speedups over standard DMTS, with improved stability and no need for fine-tuning. When combined with hydrogen mass repartitioning and high hydrogen friction, the method extends the maximum timestep to 10 fs while maintaining accuracy, and demonstrates speedups of 3.66–5.64 over single-timestep simulations when applied to MACE-OFF23.
为提升神经网络势函数在原子分子动力学模拟中的计算效率而不损失精度，作者提出DMTS-NC方法，该方法通过非保守力实现多时间步加速。其核心是双层RESPA框架，将精确保守势与优化生成非保守力的简化蒸馏势耦合，同时保留旋转等变性与原子力分量抵消等物理先验。实验表明，DMTS-NC比传统DMTS快15–30%，稳定性更优且无需调参。结合氢质量重分配与高氢摩擦技术，时间步可扩展至10飞秒而不失真。该方法适用于任意神经网络势函数，已在MACE-OFF23上验证，实现3.66–5.64倍于单时间步的加速。
Abstraction in Style
Authors: Min Lu, Yuanfeng He, Anthony Chen, Jianhuang He, Pu Wang, Daniel Cohen-Or, Hui Huang
Venue: siggraph 2026
First: 2026-03-31T15:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:59:55+00:00
Comments: siggraph 2026 conditionally accepted paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artistic styles often embed abstraction beyond surface appearance, involving deliberate reinterpretation of structure rather than mere changes in texture or color. Conventional style transfer methods typically preserve the input geometry and therefore struggle to capture this deeper abstraction behavior, especially for illustrative and nonphotorealistic styles. In this work, we introduce Abstraction in Style (AiS), a generative framework that separates structural abstraction from visual stylization. Given a target image and a small set of style exemplars, AiS first derives an intermediate abstraction proxy that reinterprets the target's structure in accordance with the abstraction logic exhibited by the style. The proxy captures semantic structure while relaxing geometric fidelity, enabling subsequent stylization to operate on an abstracted representation rather than the original image. In a second stage, the abstraction proxy is rendered to produce the final stylized output, preserving visual coherence with the reference style. Both stages are implemented using a shared image space analogy, enabling transformations to be learned from visual exemplars without explicit geometric supervision. By decoupling abstraction from appearance and treating abstraction as an explicit, transferable process, AiS supports a wider range of stylistic transformations, improves controllability, and enables more expressive stylization.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that artistic styles often involve structural reinterpretation rather than superficial visual changes, this work proposes Abstraction in Style (AiS), a generative framework that decouples structural abstraction from visual stylization. AiS first constructs an intermediate abstraction proxy by reinterpreting the target image’s structure according to the abstraction logic in provided style exemplars, prioritizing semantic coherence over geometric fidelity. This proxy is then stylized to produce the final output while preserving visual consistency with the reference style. Experiments demonstrate that AiS achieves more expressive and controllable stylization across illustrative and nonphotorealistic styles, outperforming conventional methods that preserve input geometry.
为捕捉艺术风格中超越表面视觉变化的深层结构抽象，本文提出Abstraction in Style（AiS）框架，将结构抽象与视觉风格化解耦。该框架首先根据目标图像和风格样本生成一个抽象代理，该代理按风格的抽象逻辑重新诠释目标结构，同时放松几何保真度；随后对代理进行风格化以生成最终输出，保持与参考风格的视觉一致性。实验表明，AiS在插画和非写实风格中实现更丰富、可控的风格化效果，且无需几何监督即可从视觉样本中学习抽象过程。
CLIP-AUTT: Test-Time Personalization with Action Unit Prompting for Fine-Grained Video Emotion Recognition
Authors: Muhammad Osama Zeeshan, Masoumeh Sharafi, Benoît Savary, Alessandro Lameiras Koerich, Marco Pedersoli, Eric Granger
First: 2026-03-30T03:39:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:59:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Personalization in emotion recognition (ER) is essential for an accurate interpretation of subtle and subject-specific expressive patterns. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong potential for leveraging joint image-text representations in ER. However, CLIP-based methods either depend on CLIP's contrastive pretraining or on LLMs to generate descriptive text prompts, which are noisy, computationally expensive, and fail to capture fine-grained expressions, leading to degraded performance. In this work, we leverage Action Units (AUs) as structured textual prompts within CLIP to model fine-grained facial expressions. AUs encode the subtle muscle activations underlying expressions, providing localized and interpretable semantic cues for more robust ER. We introduce CLIP-AU, a lightweight AU-guided temporal learning method that integrates interpretable AU semantics into CLIP. It learns generic, subject-agnostic representations by aligning AU prompts with facial dynamics, enabling fine-grained ER without CLIP fine-tuning or LLM-generated text supervision. Although CLIP-AU models fine-grained AU semantics, it does not adapt to subject-specific variability in subtle expressions. To address this limitation, we propose CLIP-AUTT, a video-based test-time personalization method that dynamically adapts AU prompts to videos from unseen subjects. By combining entropy-guided temporal window selection with prompt tuning, CLIP-AUTT enables subject-specific adaptation while preserving temporal consistency. Our extensive experiments on three challenging video-based subtle ER datasets, BioVid, StressID, and BAH, indicate that CLIP-AU and CLIP-AUTT outperform state-of-the-art CLIP-based FER and TTA methods, achieving robust and personalized subtle ER. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/osamazeeshan/CLIP-AUTT.
