Summary / 总结
To improve fine-grained video emotion recognition by adapting to subject-specific facial expressions, this work introduces CLIP-AUTT, a test-time personalization method that uses Action Units as structured prompts within CLIP to capture subtle facial dynamics. Unlike prior CLIP-based approaches that rely on noisy or computationally heavy text prompts, CLIP-AU first learns generic AU-guided representations by aligning AU semantics with facial motion, enabling accurate emotion recognition without fine-tuning. CLIP-AUTT then adapts these representations at test time by dynamically tuning AU prompts based on entropy-guided temporal windows, allowing personalized responses to unseen subjects while preserving temporal coherence. Experiments on BioVid, StressID, and BAH show CLIP-AUTT significantly outperforms existing CLIP-based and test-time adaptation methods in recognizing subtle emotions across diverse individuals.
为提升对细微面部表情的个性化识别，该研究提出CLIP-AUTT方法，利用动作单元（AU）作为结构化提示嵌入CLIP模型中以捕捉面部动态。CLIP-AU先学习通用的AU引导表示，无需微调或大语言模型提示；CLIP-AUTT进一步在测试时通过熵引导的时间窗口选择与提示调优动态适配未见过的个体。在BioVid、StressID和BAH三个视频情感识别数据集上的实验表明，CLIP-AUTT显著优于现有基于CLIP和测试时自适应的方法，实现更鲁棒且个性化的细微情感识别。
Training deep learning based dynamic MR image reconstruction using synthetic fractals
Authors: Anirudh Raman, Olivier Jaubert, Mark Wrobel, Tina Yao, Ruaraidh Campbell, Rebecca Baker, Ruta Virsinskaite, Daniel Knight, Michael Quail, Jennifer Steeden, Vivek Muthurangu
First: 2026-03-31T15:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:59:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: To investigate whether synthetically generated fractal data can be used to train deep learning (DL) models for dynamic MRI reconstruction, thereby avoiding the privacy, licensing, and availability limitations associated with cardiac MR training datasets. Methods: A training dataset was generated using quaternion Julia fractals to produce 2D+time images. Multi-coil MRI acquisition was simulated to generate paired fully sampled and radially undersampled k-space data. A 3D UNet deep artefact suppression model was trained using these fractal data (F-DL) and compared with an identical model trained on cardiac MRI data (CMR-DL). Both models were evaluated on prospectively acquired radial real-time cardiac MRI from 10 patients. Reconstructions were compared against compressed sensing(CS) and low-rank deep image prior (LR-DIP). All reconstrctuions were ranked for image quality, while ventricular volumes and ejection fraction were compared with reference breath-hold cine MRI. Results: There was no significant difference in qualitative ranking between F-DL and CMR-DL (p=0.9), while both outperformed CS and LR-DIP (p<0.001). Ventricular volumes and function derived from F-DL were similar to CMR-DL, showing no significant bias and accptable limits of agreement compared to reference cine imaging. However, LR-DIP had a signifcant bias (p=0.016) and wider lmits of agreement. Conclusion: DL models trained using synthetic fractal data can reconstruct real-time cardiac MRI with image quality and clinical measurements comparable to models trained on true cardiac MRI data. Fractal training data provide an open, scalable alternative to clinical datasets and may enable development of more generalisable DL reconstruction models for dynamic MRI.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of privacy, licensing, and scarcity of clinical cardiac MRI data, this study explores whether synthetic fractal data can train deep learning models for dynamic MRI reconstruction. The method generates 2D+time fractal images using quaternion Julia sets, simulates multi-coil MRI acquisitions to create undersampled k-space data, and trains a 3D UNet model on this synthetic data (F-DL), comparing it to a model trained on real cardiac MRI (CMR-DL). Both models are evaluated on real patient data, with reconstructions ranked for image quality and ventricular volumes/ejection fraction compared to reference cine MRI. Results show F-DL and CMR-DL yield statistically similar image quality and clinically comparable measurements, outperforming compressed sensing and LR-DIP, which showed significant bias and wider error margins.
为解决临床心脏MRI数据在隐私、授权和可用性方面的限制，本研究探讨了使用合成分形数据训练深度学习模型进行动态MRI重建的可行性。研究人员利用四元数Julia分形生成2D+时间图像，并模拟多线圈MRI采集，训练了3D UNet模型（F-DL），并与基于真实心脏MRI训练的模型（CMR-DL）进行比较。两者在真实患者数据上的测试中均优于压缩感知和低秩深度图像先验方法，在图像质量和临床指标上表现优异。F-DL所得的心室容积和射血分数与CMR-DL无显著差异，而LR-DIP则存在显著偏差和更宽的误差范围。
SkillReducer: Optimizing LLM Agent Skills for Token Efficiency
Authors: Yudong Gao, Zongjie Li, Yuanyuanyuan, Zimo Ji, Pingchuan Ma, Shuai Wang
First: 2026-03-31T15:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:57:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based coding agents rely on \emph{skills}, pre-packaged instruction sets that extend agent capabilities, yet every token of skill content injected into the context window incurs both monetary cost and attention dilution. To understand the severity of this problem, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of 55,315 publicly available skills and find systemic inefficiencies: 26.4\% lack routing descriptions entirely, over 60\% of body content is non-actionable, and reference files can inject tens of thousands of tokens per invocation. Motivated by these findings, we present \textsc{SkillReducer}, a two-stage optimization framework. Stage~1 optimizes the routing layer by compressing verbose descriptions and generating missing ones via adversarial delta debugging. Stage~2 restructures skill bodies through taxonomy-driven classification and progressive disclosure, separating actionable core rules from supplementary content loaded on demand, validated by faithfulness checks and a self-correcting feedback loop. Evaluated on 600 skills and the SkillsBench benchmark, \textsc{SkillReducer} achieves 48\% description compression and 39\% body compression while improving functional quality by 2.8\%, revealing a \emph{less-is-more} effect where removing non-essential content reduces distraction in the context window. These benefits transfer across five models from four families with a mean retention of 0.965, and generalize to an independent agent framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillReducer：优化大语言模型代理技能以提高令牌效率
基于大语言模型的编程代理依赖于\emph{技能}，即预先打包的指令集以扩展代理能力，但注入到上下文窗口中的每一条技能内容都会带来成本和注意力稀释。为了解决这一问题的严重性，我们对55,315个公开可用的技能进行了大规模实证研究，发现系统性低效：26.4\%完全缺乏路由描述，超过60\%的主体内容不可执行，参考文件每次调用可注入数万个令牌。受此启发，我们提出了\textsc{SkillReducer}，一个两阶段优化框架。第一阶段通过压缩冗长描述并利用对抗式增量调试生成缺失描述来优化路由层。第二阶段通过分类驱动的分类和渐进式披露重构技能主体，将可执行的核心规则与按需加载的补充内容分离，经由忠实性检查和自纠正反馈循环验证。在600个技能和SkillsBench基准上评估，\textsc{SkillReducer}实现了48\%的描述压缩和39\%的主体压缩，同时功能质量提升2.8\%，揭示了\emph{少即是多}的效果——移除非必要内容可减少上下文窗口中的干扰。这些收益在四个家族的五个模型中均得以迁移，平均保留率为0.965，并可推广至独立代理框架。

The first stage compresses routing descriptions using adversarial delta debugging, while the second stage restructures skill bodies via taxonomy-driven classification and progressive disclosure, separating core rules from supplementary content. Evaluated on 600 skills and SkillsBench, it achieves 48% description compression, 39% body compression, and a 2.8% improvement in functional quality, demonstrating that reducing non-essential content enhances agent performance by minimizing context window distraction. These gains generalize across five models from four families with a mean retention of 0.965 and transfer to an independent agent framework.
鉴于LLM编码代理技能中存在严重效率问题——26.4%技能缺乏路由描述，60%以上正文内容不可执行——SkillReducer提出两阶段优化框架。第一阶段通过对抗式差分调试压缩并生成缺失的路由描述；第二阶段通过分类和渐进式披露重构技能正文，分离可执行核心规则与按需加载的补充内容。在600个技能和SkillsBench基准上评估，该方法实现48%描述压缩和39%正文压缩，同时功能质量提升2.8%，表明减少非必要内容可降低上下文干扰，提升性能，且效果在五个模型和独立代理框架中普遍适用。
Diffusion-Based Feature Denoising with NNMF for Robust handwritten digit multi-class classification
Authors: Hiba Adil Al-kharsan, Róbert Rajkó
First: 2026-03-31T15:57:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:57:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents a robust multi-class classification framework for handwritten digits that combines diffusion-driven feature denoising with a hybrid feature representation. Inspired by our previous work on brain tumor classification, the proposed approach operates in a feature space to improve the robustness to noise and adversarial attacks. First, the input images are converted into tight, interpretable exemplification using Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (NNMF). In parallel, special deep features are extracted using a computational neural network (CNN). These integral features are combined into a united hybrid representation. To improve robustness, a step diffusion operation is used in the feature space by gradually adding Gaussian noise. A feature denoiser network is trained to reverse this operation and rebuild clean representations from tilted inputs. The courteous features are then applied for multi-class classification. The suggested method is evaluated in both baseline and adversarial settings using AutoAttack. The experimental outcome present that the diffusion-based hybrid model is both effective and robust, the CNN baseline models outperforming while maintain powerful classification performance. These results explain the activity of feature-level diffusion defense for reliable multi-class handwritten digit classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于扩散的特征去噪与NNMF相结合的鲁棒手写数字多分类方法
本文提出了一种鲁棒的手写数字多分类框架，结合扩散驱动的特征去噪与混合特征表示。受我们之前脑肿瘤分类工作的启发，该方法在特征空间中操作，以增强对噪声和对抗攻击的鲁棒性。首先，输入图像通过非负矩阵分解（NNMF）转换为紧凑且可解释的表示；同时，使用卷积神经网络（CNN）提取特殊深度特征。这些综合特征组合成统一的混合表示。为提升鲁棒性，特征空间中逐步添加高斯噪声，通过扩散操作实现；随后训练一个特征去噪网络，以逆转该操作，从受扰输入重建干净表示。最后，利用这些干净特征进行多分类。该方法在AutoAttack基准和对抗环境下进行评估，实验结果表明，基于扩散的混合模型既有效又鲁棒，而CNN基线模型在保持强大分类性能的同时表现更优。这些结果验证了特征级扩散防御在可靠手写数字多分类中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust handwritten digit classification under noise and adversarial attacks, this study combines diffusion-based feature denoising with Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (NNMF) to create a hybrid feature representation. The method first extracts interpretable NNMF components and deep CNN features in parallel, then applies a diffusion process to introduce controlled noise in feature space before training a denoiser network to recover clean features. Evaluated against adversarial attacks via AutoAttack, the diffusion-enhanced hybrid model demonstrates superior robustness while maintaining high classification accuracy, outperforming standard CNN baselines in both clean and adversarial settings.
为提升手写数字在噪声和对抗攻击下的分类鲁棒性，该研究提出一种基于扩散的特征去噪框架，结合非负矩阵分解（NNMF）提取可解释特征与CNN提取的深层特征，构建混合表征。在特征空间中引入高斯噪声扩散过程，并训练去噪网络恢复干净特征。在AutoAttack基准及对抗环境下评估表明，该方法在保持高分类性能的同时显著优于标准CNN基线，验证了特征级扩散防御在可靠多分类手写数字识别中的有效性。
Uncertainty Gating for Cost-Aware Explainable Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Georgii Mikriukov, Grégoire Montavon, Marina M. -C. Höhne
First: 2026-03-31T15:56:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:56:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-hoc explanation methods are widely used to interpret black-box predictions, but their generation is often computationally expensive and their reliability is not guaranteed. We propose epistemic uncertainty as a low-cost proxy for explanation reliability: high epistemic uncertainty identifies regions where the decision boundary is poorly defined and where explanations become unstable and unfaithful. This insight enables two complementary use cases: `improving worst-case explanations' (routing samples to cheap or expensive XAI methods based on expected explanation reliability), and `recalling high-quality explanations' (deferring explanation generation for uncertain samples under constrained budget). Across four tabular datasets, five diverse architectures, and four XAI methods, we observe a strong negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and explanation stability. Further analysis shows that epistemic uncertainty distinguishes not only stable from unstable explanations, but also faithful from unfaithful ones. Experiments on image classification confirm that our findings generalize beyond tabular data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向成本感知可解释人工智能的不确定性门控
后验解释方法广泛用于解释黑盒预测，但其生成通常计算成本高且可靠性无法保证。我们提出将认知不确定性作为解释可靠性的低成本代理：高认知不确定性标识决策边界定义不清、解释不稳定且不忠实的区域。这一见解支持两种互补应用场景：‘改进最坏情况解释’（根据预期解释可靠性将样本路由至低成本或高成本XAI方法），以及‘召回高质量解释’（在预算受限时对不确定样本延迟生成解释）。在四个表格数据集、五种不同架构和四种XAI方法上，我们观察到认知不确定性与解释稳定性呈强负相关。进一步分析表明，认知不确定性不仅能区分稳定与不稳定解释，还能区分忠实与不忠实解释。图像分类实验验证了我们的发现可泛化至表格数据之外。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational cost and reliability issues of post-hoc explainable AI methods, this work introduces epistemic uncertainty as a low-cost proxy to gauge explanation reliability, identifying regions where model decisions are ambiguous and explanations become unstable or unfaithful. The method enables two practical applications: routing samples to appropriate XAI methods based on expected reliability, or deferring expensive explanations for uncertain samples under budget constraints. Across multiple tabular datasets, architectures, and XAI techniques, experiments reveal a strong negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and explanation stability, and further demonstrate that epistemic uncertainty also correlates with explanation faithfulness, confirming its generalizability to image classification tasks.
为解决后验解释方法计算成本高且可靠性不足的问题，本文提出将认知不确定性作为低成本代理，用于评估解释的可靠性，识别模型决策边界模糊、解释不稳定或不忠实的区域。该方法支持两种应用场景：根据预期可靠性将样本路由至不同成本的解释方法，或在预算受限时对高不确定性样本延迟生成解释。在多个表格数据集、模型架构和解释方法上，实验发现认知不确定性与解释稳定性呈显著负相关，且能区分稳定与不稳定的解释，同时也能区分忠实与不忠实的解释，验证其在图像分类任务中的泛化能力。
Task Scarcity and Label Leakage in Relational Transfer Learning
Authors: Francisco Galuppo Azevedo, Clarissa Lima Loures, Denis Oliveira Correa
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-31T15:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:55:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 3rd DATA-FM Workshop at ICLR 2026, Rio de Janeiro, Brazil. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=nI2nsMMHXp
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training relational foundation models requires learning representations that transfer across tasks, yet available supervision is typically limited to a small number of prediction targets per database. This task scarcity causes learned representations to encode task-specific shortcuts that degrade transfer even within the same schema, a problem we call label leakage. We study this using K-Space, a modular architecture combining frozen pretrained tabular encoders with a lightweight message-passing core. To suppress leakage, we introduce a gradient projection method that removes label-predictive directions from representation updates. On RelBench, this improves within-dataset transfer by +0.145 AUROC on average, often recovering near single-task performance. Our results suggest that limited task diversity, not just limited data, constrains relational foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关系迁移学习中的任务稀缺与标签泄露
训练关系基础模型需要学习能在不同任务间迁移的表示，但通常每个数据库仅提供少量预测目标作为监督信号。这种任务稀缺导致学习到的表示编码了任务特定的捷径，即使在同一模式内也会损害迁移效果，我们称之为标签泄露。我们使用K-Space架构进行研究，该架构结合了冻结的预训练表格编码器与轻量级消息传递核心。为抑制泄露，我们引入一种梯度投影方法，从表示更新中移除与标签预测相关的方向。在RelBench数据集上，该方法平均提升跨数据集迁移性能0.145 AUROC，常能恢复接近单任务性能。我们的结果表明，任务多样性不足，而不仅是数据量有限，制约了关系基础模型的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that relational foundation models suffer from task scarcity—where supervision is limited to few prediction targets per database—the paper introduces label leakage, a phenomenon where learned representations encode task-specific shortcuts that degrade transfer even within the same schema. To address this, the authors employ K-Space, a modular architecture combining frozen tabular encoders with a lightweight message-passing core, and introduce a gradient projection method that removes label-predictive directions during training. Experiments on RelBench show this approach improves within-dataset transfer by an average of +0.145 AUROC, often restoring near single-task performance, indicating that limited task diversity, not just data scarcity, is a key bottleneck.
由于关系型基础模型训练时每个数据库通常仅提供少量预测目标，导致任务稀缺，该研究提出‘标签泄露’问题——即学习到的表示会编码任务特定捷径，即使在相同模式下也会损害迁移效果。作者设计了K-Space架构，结合冻结的表格编码器与轻量级消息传递核心，并引入梯度投影方法以在训练中剔除标签预测方向。在RelBench上的实验表明，该方法平均提升内部数据集迁移性能0.145 AUROC，常恢复至单任务水平，说明任务多样性不足而非数据量不足才是制约关系型基础模型的关键瓶颈。
SISA: A Scale-In Systolic Array for GEMM Acceleration
Authors: Luigi Altamura, Alessio Cicero, Mateo Vázquez Maceiras, Mohammad Ali Maleki, Pedro Trancoso
First: 2026-03-31T15:55:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:55:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The currently dominant AI/ML workloads, such as Large Language Models (LLMs), rely on the efficient execution of General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) operations. Thus, most systems are equipped with dedicated matrix hardware accelerators based on square Systolic Arrays (SAs) of Processing Elements (PEs). While this organization was effective for traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), LLMs introduce input-dependent and highly skewed matrices, leading to underutilized SA resources. To address this challenge, we propose SISA (Scale-In Systolic Array), a novel SA architecture that partitions the traditional square array into horizontal rectangular slabs. With minimal overhead, SISA exposes parallelism through independently scheduled slabs for efficient execution of small or skewed matrix shapes, while retaining full-array operation for large GEMMs. SISA achieves up to 8.52x speedup and 93% energy-delay-product (EDP) reduction for representative LLMs compared to a state-of-the-art monolithic SA with the same number of PEs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SISA：一种用于GEMM加速的可缩放脉动阵列
当前主流的AI/ML工作负载（如大语言模型LLMs）依赖高效执行通用矩阵-矩阵乘法（GEMM）运算。因此，大多数系统配备基于处理单元（PE）的方形脉动阵列（SA）专用矩阵硬件加速器。尽管该结构对传统深度神经网络（DNN）有效，但LLMs引入了输入依赖且高度非均匀的矩阵，导致SA资源利用率低下。为解决此问题，我们提出SISA（可缩放脉动阵列），一种新颖的SA架构，将传统方形阵列划分为水平矩形层。SISA在极小开销下，通过独立调度的层暴露并行性，高效执行小或非均匀矩阵形状，同时保留全阵列操作以支持大型GEMM。与同等PE数量的最先进单体SA相比，SISA在代表性LLMs上实现最高8.52倍加速和93%的能量-延迟积（EDP）降低。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to efficiently handle the irregular and input-dependent matrix operations in Large Language Models, the paper introduces SISA, a novel systolic array architecture that partitions the traditional square array into horizontal rectangular slabs to better exploit parallelism for skewed or small matrices. By enabling independent scheduling of these slabs with minimal overhead, SISA maintains full-array performance for large GEMMs while significantly improving efficiency for irregular workloads. Experimental results show SISA achieves up to 8.52x speedup and 93% energy-delay-product reduction compared to a state-of-the-art monolithic systolic array with the same number of processing elements.
为应对大语言模型中输入依赖且矩阵形状不规则的计算需求，本文提出SISA架构，将传统方形阵列划分为水平矩形层，通过独立调度各层以高效处理小或不规则矩阵，同时保留完整阵列模式处理大规模GEMM运算，开销极小。实验表明，与同等PE数量的现有单体阵列相比，SISA可实现最高8.52倍加速和93%的能量-延迟积降低。
LatentFM: A Latent Flow Matching Approach for Generative Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Huynh Trinh Ngoc, Hoang Anh Nguyen Kim, Toan Nguyen Hai, Long Tran Quoc
First: 2025-12-04T14:06:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:53:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative models have achieved remarkable progress with the emergence of flow matching (FM). It has demonstrated strong generative capabilities and attracted significant attention as a simulation-free flow-based framework capable of learning exact data densities. Motivated by these advances, we propose LatentFM, a flow-based model operating in the latent space for medical image segmentation. To model the data distribution, we first design two variational autoencoders (VAEs) to encode both medical images and their corresponding masks into a lower-dimensional latent space. We then estimate a conditional velocity field that guides the flow based on the input image. By sampling multiple latent representations, our method synthesizes diverse segmentation outputs whose pixel-wise variance reliably captures the underlying data distribution, enabling both highly accurate and uncertainty-aware predictions. Furthermore, we generate confidence maps that quantify the model certainty, providing clinicians with richer information for deeper analysis. We conduct experiments on two datasets, ISIC-2018 and CVC-Clinic, and compare our method with several prior baselines, including both deterministic and generative approach models. Through comprehensive evaluations, both qualitative and quantitative results show that our approach achieves superior segmentation accuracy while remaining highly efficient in the latent space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LatentFM：一种用于生成式医学图像分割的潜在流匹配方法
随着流匹配（FM）的出现，生成模型取得了显著进展。它展现出强大的生成能力，作为一种无需模拟的基于流的框架，能够学习精确的数据密度，因而备受关注。受此启发，我们提出LatentFM，一种在潜在空间中运行的基于流的模型，用于医学图像分割。为建模数据分布，我们首先设计两个变分自编码器（VAEs），将医学图像及其对应掩码编码至低维潜在空间。随后，我们估计一个条件速度场，根据输入图像引导流。通过采样多个潜在表示，我们的方法生成多样化的分割输出，其像素级方差可靠地捕捉底层数据分布，从而实现高精度且具备不确定性感知的预测。此外，我们生成置信度图，量化模型确定性，为临床医生提供更丰富的分析信息。我们在ISIC-2018和CVC-Clinic两个数据集上进行实验，并与多种基线方法（包括确定性和生成式模型）进行比较。综合评估表明，我们的方法在分割精度上表现更优，同时在潜在空间中保持高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the strong generative capabilities of flow matching, the authors propose LatentFM, a flow-based model operating in a latent space for medical image segmentation. The method first encodes medical images and their masks into a lower-dimensional latent space using two variational autoencoders, then estimates a conditional velocity field to guide the flow based on the input image. By sampling multiple latent representations, it generates diverse segmentation outputs whose pixel-wise variance reflects data distribution uncertainty, enabling accurate and uncertainty-aware predictions. Experiments on ISIC-2018 and CVC-Clinic datasets show LatentFM outperforms multiple baselines in segmentation accuracy while maintaining computational efficiency.
受流匹配强大生成能力的启发，作者提出LatentFM方法，该方法在潜在空间中基于流模型进行医学图像分割。首先通过两个变分自编码器将医学图像及其掩码编码至低维潜在空间，再根据输入图像估计条件速度场以引导流。通过采样多个潜在表示，生成多样化的分割结果，其像素级方差能可靠反映数据分布不确定性，实现高精度且具备不确定性感知的预测。在ISIC-2018和CVC-Clinic两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分割精度上优于多个基线模型，同时在潜在空间中保持高效。
C-TRAIL: A Commonsense World Framework for Trajectory Planning in Autonomous Driving
Authors: Zhihong Cui, Haoran Tang, Tianyi Li, Yushuai Li, Peiyuan Guan, Amir Taherkordi, Tor Skeie
First: 2026-03-31T15:53:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:53:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Trajectory planning for autonomous driving increasingly leverages large language models (LLMs) for commonsense reasoning, yet LLM outputs are inherently unreliable, posing risks in safety-critical applications. We propose C-TRAIL, a framework built on a Commonsense World that couples LLM-derived commonsense with a trust mechanism to guide trajectory planning. C-TRAIL operates through a closed-loop Recall, Plan, and Update cycle: the Recall module queries an LLM for semantic relations and quantifies their reliability via a dual-trust mechanism; the Plan module injects trust-weighted commonsense into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) through a Dirichlet trust policy; and the Update module adaptively refines trust scores and policy parameters from environmental feedback. Experiments on four simulated scenarios in Highway-env and two real-world levelXData datasets (highD, rounD) show that C-TRAIL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing ADE by 40.2%, FDE by 51.7%, and improving SR by 16.9 percentage points on average. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZhihongCui/CTRAIL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：C-TRAIL：面向自动驾驶轨迹规划的常识世界框架
自动驾驶轨迹规划日益依赖大型语言模型（LLM）进行常识推理，但LLM输出固有不可靠，这在安全关键应用中带来风险。我们提出C-TRAIL框架，基于常识世界，将LLM生成的常识与信任机制结合，引导轨迹规划。C-TRAIL通过闭环的召回、规划与更新循环运行：召回模块查询LLM获取语义关系，并通过双信任机制量化其可靠性；规划模块通过Dirichlet信任策略将信任加权的常识注入蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）；更新模块从环境反馈中自适应优化信任评分与策略参数。在Highway-env的四个模拟场景及两个真实levelXData数据集（highD、rounD）上的实验表明，C-TRAIL持续优于现有最先进基线，平均降低ADE 40.2%，FDE 51.7%，提升SR 16.9个百分点。源代码见https://github.com/ZhihongCui/CTRAIL。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical autonomous driving trajectory planning, C-TRAIL introduces a Commonsense World framework that integrates LLM-derived commonsense with a trust mechanism. It operates in a closed-loop cycle: Recall queries LLMs for semantic relations and assigns reliability scores via a dual-trust mechanism; Plan incorporates trust-weighted commonsense into Monte Carlo Tree Search using a Dirichlet trust policy; and Update refines trust scores and policy parameters based on environmental feedback. Experiments across four simulated Highway-env scenarios and two real-world datasets (highD, rounD) demonstrate that C-TRAIL reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 40.2%, Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 51.7%, and improves Success Rate (SR) by 16.9 percentage points over state-of-the-art baselines.
为解决大语言模型（LLM）在自动驾驶轨迹规划中固有的不可靠性问题，C-TRAIL提出一个结合常识推理与信任机制的常识世界框架。该框架通过闭环循环工作：召回模块向LLM查询语义关系并利用双信任机制量化其可靠性；规划模块将加权信任的常识注入蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），采用Dirichlet信任策略；更新模块根据环境反馈自适应调整信任评分与策略参数。在四个模拟场景及两个真实数据集（highD、rounD）上的实验表明，C-TRAIL显著优于现有方法，平均降低平均位移误差（ADE）40.2%，最终位移误差（FDE）51.7%，成功率（SR）提升16.9个百分点。
$p$-adic Character Neural Network
Authors: Tomoki Mihara
First: 2026-03-31T15:50:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:50:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a new frame work of $p$-adic neural network. Unlike the original $p$-adic neural network by S.\ Albeverio, A.\ Khrennikov, and B.\ Tirrozi using a family of characteristic functions indexed by hyperparameters of precision as activation functions, we use a single injective $p$-adic character on the topological Abelian group $\mathbb{Z}_p$ of $p$-adic integers as an activation function. We prove the $p$-adic universal approximation theorem for this formulation of $p$-adic neural network, and reduce it to the feasibility problem of polynomial equations over the finite ring of integers modulo a power of $p$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：p进特征神经网络
我们提出了一种新的p进神经网络框架。与S. Albeverio、A. Khrennikov和B. Tirrozi提出的原始p进神经网络不同，后者使用由精度超参数索引的一组特征函数作为激活函数，我们则使用拓扑阿贝尔群p进整数环Z_p上的单个单射p进特征作为激活函数。我们证明了该p进神经网络形式的p进通用逼近定理，并将其归约为在p的幂次模有限整数环上的多项式方程的可行性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a more structured and theoretically grounded approach to p-adic neural networks, this work introduces a framework using a single injective p-adic character as the activation function, replacing the previous method that relied on a family of characteristic functions indexed by precision hyperparameters. The authors prove a p-adic universal approximation theorem for this new formulation, demonstrating that such networks can approximate any continuous function on the p-adic integers. This theoretical result is further reduced to a feasibility problem involving polynomial equations over finite rings of integers modulo powers of p, establishing a concrete computational pathway for implementation.
为克服传统p进神经网络依赖于精度参数化特征函数族的局限性，本文提出一种新架构，采用单个单射p进特征函数作为p整数环上的激活函数。作者证明了该框架的p进通用逼近定理，表明任意连续p进函数均可被此类网络任意逼近。该理论结果进一步转化为在p的幂次模有限环上求解多项式方程的问题，为实际计算提供了可行路径。
ATP-Bench: Towards Agentic Tool Planning for MLLM Interleaved Generation
Authors: Yinuo Liu, Zi Qian, Heng Zhou, Jiahao Zhang, Yajie Zhang, Zhihang Li, Mengyu Zhou, Erchao Zhao, Xiaoxi Jiang, Guanjun Jiang
First: 2026-03-31T15:47:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:47:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Interleaved text-and-image generation represents a significant frontier for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), offering a more intuitive way to convey complex information. Current paradigms rely on either image generation or retrieval augmentation, yet they typically treat the two as mutually exclusive paths, failing to unify factuality with creativity. We argue that the next milestone in this field is Agentic Tool Planning, where the model serves as a central controller that autonomously determines when, where, and which tools to invoke to produce interleaved responses for visual-critical queries. To systematically evaluate this paradigm, we introduce ATP-Bench, a novel benchmark comprising 7,702 QA pairs (including 1,592 VQA pairs) across eight categories and 25 visual-critical intents, featuring human-verified queries and ground truths. Furthermore, to evaluate agentic planning independent of end-to-end execution and changing tool backends, we propose a Multi-Agent MLLM-as-a-Judge (MAM) system. MAM evaluates tool-call precision, identifies missed opportunities for tool use, and assesses overall response quality without requiring ground-truth references. Our extensive experiments on 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal that models struggle with coherent interleaved planning and exhibit significant variations in tool-use behavior, highlighting substantial room for improvement and providing actionable guidance for advancing interleaved generation. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/ATP-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATP-Bench：迈向多模态大语言模型的代理工具规划与交错生成
交错文本与图像生成是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的重要前沿，提供了一种更直观的复杂信息传达方式。当前范式依赖图像生成或检索增强，但通常将二者视为互斥路径，未能统一事实性与创造性。我们认为，该领域的下一个里程碑是代理工具规划，即模型作为中央控制器，自主决定何时、何地调用何种工具以生成视觉关键查询的交错响应。为系统评估该范式，我们引入ATP-Bench，一个包含7,702个问答对（含1,592个视觉问答对）的新型基准，涵盖八类和25种视觉关键意图，包含人工验证的查询与真实答案。此外，为独立评估代理规划能力（不依赖端到端执行及工具后端变化），我们提出多代理MLLM裁判系统（MAM）。MAM评估工具调用精度、识别未使用工具的机会，并评估整体响应质量，无需真实参考答案。我们在10个当前最先进的MLLMs上进行的广泛实验表明，模型在连贯的交错规划方面表现不佳，工具使用行为差异显著，凸显了改进空间并提供了推进交错生成的可操作指导。数据集与代码见https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/ATP-Bench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify factuality and creativity in multimodal generation, this work proposes ATP-Bench, a benchmark evaluating agentic tool planning for MLLMs to produce interleaved text-and-image responses. The method introduces a novel dataset of 7,702 QA pairs across 25 visual-critical intents and employs a Multi-Agent MLLM-as-a-Judge (MAM) system to assess tool-call precision and planning quality without ground-truth dependencies. Experimental results on 10 state-of-the-art models show inconsistent tool usage and poor coherence in interleaved planning, indicating significant room for improvement in agentic reasoning.
为统一多模态生成中的事实性与创造性，该研究提出ATP-Bench基准，用于评估模型在交错图文生成中的自主工具规划能力。方法构建包含7702组问答对（含1592组视觉问答）的多意图数据集，并设计多智能体MLLM裁判系统（MAM），无需真实标注即可评估工具调用精度与规划质量。实验表明，10个主流MLLM模型在交错规划上普遍表现不佳，工具调用行为差异显著，凸显当前模型在自主规划能力上的改进空间。
Less Is More? Selective Visual Attention to High-Importance Regions for Multimodal Radiology Summarization
Authors: Mst. Fahmida Sultana Naznin, Adnan Ibney Faruq, Mushfiqur Rahman, Niloy Kumar Mondal, Md. Mehedi Hasan Shawon, Md Rakibul Hasan
First: 2026-03-31T15:47:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:47:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated radiology report summarization aims to distill verbose findings into concise clinical impressions, but existing multimodal models often struggle with visual noise and fail to meaningfully improve over strong text-only baselines in the FINDINGS $\to$ IMPRESSION transformation. We challenge two prevailing assumptions: (1) that more visual input is always better, and (2) that multimodal models add limited value when findings already contain rich image-derived detail. Through controlled ablations on MIMIC-CXR benchmark, we show that selectively focusing on pathology-relevant visual patches rather than full images yields substantially better performance. We introduce ViTAS, Visual-Text Attention Summarizer, a multi-stage pipeline that combines ensemble-guided MedSAM2 lung segmentation, bidirectional cross-attention for multi-view fusion, Shapley-guided adaptive patch clustering, and hierarchical visual tokenization feeding a ViT. ViTAS achieves SOTA results with 29.25% BLEU-4 and 69.83% ROUGE-L, improved factual alignment in qualitative analysis, and the highest expert-rated human evaluation scores. Our findings demonstrate that less but more relevant visual input is not only sufficient but superior for multimodal radiology summarization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少即是多？面向多模态放射学摘要的高重要性区域选择性视觉注意
自动放射学报告摘要旨在将冗长的发现浓缩为简洁的临床印象，但现有多模态模型常受视觉噪声干扰，在FINDINGS→IMPRESSION转换中难以显著超越强文本基线。我们挑战两个主流假设：(1) 更多视觉输入总是更好；(2) 当发现已包含丰富图像细节时，多模态模型价值有限。通过在MIMIC-CXR基准上的受控消融实验，我们证明聚焦病理相关视觉块而非完整图像可显著提升性能。我们提出ViTAS（视觉-文本注意力摘要器），一个多阶段流程，结合集成引导的MedSAM2肺分割、双向跨注意力多视角融合、Shapley引导的自适应块聚类及层次化视觉标记输入ViT。ViTAS取得SOTA结果：BLEU-4达29.25%，ROUGE-L达69.83%，定性分析中事实对齐度提升，且专家评分最高。我们的研究证明，更少但更相关的视觉输入不仅足够，且优于多模态放射学摘要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current multimodal models in radiology summarization due to visual noise and limited gains over text-only baselines, this work challenges the assumptions that more visual input is better and that multimodal models add little value when text already contains image-derived detail. It introduces ViTAS, a multi-stage pipeline that uses MedSAM2 for lung segmentation, cross-attention for multi-view fusion, Shapley-guided patch clustering, and hierarchical tokenization feeding a ViT, selectively focusing on pathology-relevant visual regions. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR show ViTAS achieves state-of-the-art performance with 29.25% BLEU-4 and 69.83% ROUGE-L, improved factual alignment, and highest expert human ratings, demonstrating that selective, high-importance visual input outperforms full-image processing.
针对当前多模态模型在放射学摘要生成中因视觉噪声过多而难以超越纯文本基线的问题，该研究质疑‘视觉输入越多越好’和‘文本已含图像信息故多模态无显著增益’两个主流假设。作者提出ViTAS系统，通过MedSAM2肺分割、双向跨模态注意力融合、Shapley引导的视觉块聚类与分层视觉标记化，聚焦病理相关视觉区域。在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，ViTAS取得29.25% BLEU-4、69.83% ROUGE-L的SOTA性能，事实对齐更优，专家人工评分最高，证明精准而非泛滥的视觉输入在放射学摘要生成中更有效。
InfiniteVL: Synergizing Linear and Sparse Attention for Highly-Efficient, Unlimited-Input Vision-Language Models
Authors: Hongyuan Tao, Bencheng Liao, Shaoyu Chen, Haoran Yin, Qian Zhang, Wenyu Liu, Xinggang Wang
First: 2025-12-09T17:18:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:42:12+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 8 figures, conference or other essential info
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly tasked with ultra-long multimodal understanding. While linear architectures offer constant computation and memory footprints, they often struggle with high-frequency visual perception compared to standard Transformers. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{InfiniteVL}. We first develop a hybrid base model called \textbf{InfiniteVL-Base} that interleaves a small fraction of Full Attention layers with Gated DeltaNet. Empowered by a tailored distillation and fine-tuning strategy, InfiniteVL-Base matches the fundamental multimodal performance of equivalent Transformers while achieving a \textbf{1.7$\times$} decoding speedup. However, the quadratic complexity of the retained Full Attention inevitably becomes an efficiency bottleneck when scaling to ultra long context. To break this barrier, we propose a novel Long-Sequence Architectural Fine-Tuning strategy that seamlessly transforms the dense attention into vision-specific sparse mechanisms. This yields two specialized variants: \textbf{InfiniteVL-Offline} for offline retrieval and \textbf{InfiniteVL-Online} for online streaming. By eliminating the computation explosion of global attention without sacrificing high-frequency visual recall, InfiniteVL-Offline achieves Transformer-level length generalization with a \textbf{5x} prefill acceleration at 256K context. Concurrently, InfiniteVL-Online delivers robust streaming perception with a constant memory footprint and a real-time throughput of \textbf{25} FPS. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/InfiniteVL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InfiniteVL：融合线性与稀疏注意力的高效无限制输入视觉-语言模型
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地承担超长多模态理解任务。虽然线性架构提供恒定的计算与内存开销，但在高频视觉感知方面常不如标准Transformer。为弥合这一差距，我们提出\textbf{InfiniteVL}。首先，我们构建了一种混合基础模型\textbf{InfiniteVL-Base}，其交替嵌入少量全注意力层与门控DeltaNet。借助定制的蒸馏与微调策略，InfiniteVL-Base在保持与等效Transformer基本多模态性能的同时，实现\textbf{1.7倍}的解码加速。然而，保留的全注意力层的二次复杂度在扩展至超长上下文时必然成为效率瓶颈。为突破此限制，我们提出一种新颖的长序列架构微调策略，可无缝将密集注意力转化为视觉专用稀疏机制。由此衍生出两个专用变体：\textbf{InfiniteVL-Offline}用于离线检索，\textbf{InfiniteVL-Online}用于在线流式处理。InfiniteVL-Offline在消除全局注意力计算爆炸的同时，不牺牲高频视觉召回能力，实现Transformer级长度泛化，并在256K上下文下获得\textbf{5倍}的预填充加速。同时，InfiniteVL-Online提供稳健的流式感知能力，保持恒定内存开销，实时吞吐量达\textbf{25} FPS。代码与模型见https://github.com/hustvl/InfiniteVL。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient, scalable vision-language models capable of handling ultra-long inputs, InfiniteVL introduces a hybrid architecture that combines linear attention with sparse mechanisms. Its base model, InfiniteVL-Base, interleaves minimal full attention layers with Gated DeltaNet, achieving Transformer-level performance with 1.7x faster decoding via distillation and fine-tuning. To address quadratic complexity at scale, the paper proposes Long-Sequence Architectural Fine-Tuning, converting dense attention into vision-aware sparse attention, yielding two variants: InfiniteVL-Offline for offline retrieval, which achieves 5x prefill speedup at 256K context while maintaining Transformer-level length generalization, and InfiniteVL-Online for streaming, delivering 25 FPS real-time performance with constant memory usage.
为应对超长输入场景下视觉语言模型的效率瓶颈，作者提出InfiniteVL架构，融合线性注意力与稀疏注意力机制。基础模型InfiniteVL-Base通过插入稀疏层与门控Delta网络，在保持与Transformer相当的多模态性能的同时实现1.7倍解码加速。为突破长序列下的二次复杂度瓶颈，作者提出长序列架构微调策略，将密集注意力转化为视觉专用稀疏机制，衍生出两个变体：InfiniteVL-Offline在256K上下文下实现5倍预填充加速，且具备Transformer级别的长度泛化能力；InfiniteVL-Online则支持实时流式处理，保持常量内存占用并达到25 FPS吞吐量。
NES: An Instruction-Free, Low-Latency Next Edit Suggestion Framework Powered by Learned Historical Editing Trajectories
Authors: Xinfang Chen, Siyang Xiao, Xianying Zhu, Junhong Xie, Ming Liang, Dajun Chen, Wei Jiang, Yong Li, Peng Di
First: 2025-08-04T14:37:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:41:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted by FSE'26 Industry Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code editing is a frequent yet cognitively demanding task in software development. Existing AI-powered tools often disrupt developer flow by requiring explicit natural language instructions and suffer from high latency, limiting real-world usability. We present NES (Next Edit Suggestion), an instruction-free, low-latency code editing framework that leverages learned historical editing trajectories to implicitly capture developers' goals and coding habits. NES features a dual-model architecture: one model predicts the next edit location and the other generates the precise code change, both without any user instruction. Trained on our open-sourced SFT and DAPO datasets, NES achieves state-of-the-art performance (75.6% location accuracy, 27.7% exact match rate) while delivering suggestions in under 250ms. Deployed at Ant Group, NES serves over 20,000 developers through a seamless Tab-key interaction, achieving effective acceptance rates of 51.55% for location predictions and 43.44% for edits, demonstrating its practical impact in real-world development workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NES：一种基于学习历史编辑轨迹的无指令、低延迟的下一次编辑建议框架

FLEURS-Kobani: Extending the FLEURS Dataset for Northern Kurdish
Authors: Daban Q. Jaff, Mohammad Mohammadamini
First: 2026-03-31T15:40:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:40:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
FLEURS offers n-way parallel speech for 100+ languages, but Northern Kurdish is not one of them, which limits benchmarking for automatic speech recognition and speech translation tasks in this language. We present FLEURS-Kobani, a Northern Kurdish (ISO 639-3 KMR) spoken extension of the FLEURS benchmark. The FLEURS-Kobani dataset consists of 5,162 validated utterances, totaling 18 hours and 24 minutes. The data were recorded by 31 native speakers. It extends benchmark coverage to an under-resourced Kurdish variety. As baselines, we fine-tuned Whisper v3-large for ASR and E2E S2TT. A two-stage fine-tuning strategy (Common Voice to FLEURS-Kobani) yields the best ASR performance (WER 28.11, CER 9.84 on test). For E2E S2TT (KMR to EN), Whisper achieves 8.68 BLEU on test; we additionally report pivot-derived targets and a cascaded S2TT setup. FLEURS-Kobani provides the first public Northern Kurdish benchmark for evaluation of ASR, S2TT and S2ST tasks. The dataset is publicly released for research use under a CC BY 4.0 license.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLEURS-Kobani：扩展用于北部库尔德语的FLEURS数据集
FLEURS为100多种语言提供n路平行语音数据，但未包含北部库尔德语，这限制了该语言自动语音识别和语音翻译任务的基准测试。我们提出了FLEURS-Kobani，这是FLEURS基准的北部库尔德语（ISO 639-3 KMR）口语扩展版本。该数据集包含5,162条经验证的语句，总计18小时24分钟，由31名母语者录制。它扩展了对资源匮乏的库尔德语变体的基准覆盖范围。作为基线，我们对Whisper v3-large进行了ASR和端到端S2TT的微调。采用两阶段微调策略（Common Voice到FLEURS-Kobani）获得最佳ASR性能（测试集WER 28.11，CER 9.84）。对于端到端S2TT（KMR到EN），Whisper在测试集上达到8.68 BLEU；我们还报告了基于中继的翻译目标和级联S2TT设置。FLEURS-Kobani为北部库尔德语提供了首个公开的ASR、S2TT和S2ST任务评估基准。该数据集在CC BY 4.0许可下公开用于研究。

为填补FLEURS多语言语音语料库中对北库尔德语支持的空白，作者构建了FLEURS-Kobani数据集，包含31位母语者录制的18小时5162条验证语音。通过两阶段微调策略，Whisper v3-large在ASR任务上达到测试集WER 28.11、CER 9.84；在北库尔德语到英语的端到端语音翻译任务中，BLEU分数达8.68。该数据集首次为北库尔德语的ASR、S2TT和S2ST任务提供公开评测基准，已按CC BY 4.0协议开源。
Do Language Models Encode Semantic Relations? Probing and Sparse Feature Analysis
Authors: Andor Diera, Ansgar Scherp
First: 2026-03-18T11:42:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:38:46+00:00
Comments: accepted at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding whether large language models (LLMs) capture structured meaning requires examining how they represent concept relationships. In this work, we study three models of increasing scale: Pythia-70M, GPT-2, and Llama 3.1 8B, focusing on four semantic relations: synonymy, antonymy, hypernymy, and hyponymy. We combine linear probing with mechanistic interpretability techniques, including sparse autoencoders (SAE) and activation patching, to identify where these relations are encoded and how specific features contribute to their representation. Our results reveal a directional asymmetry in hierarchical relations: hypernymy is encoded redundantly and resists suppression, while hyponymy relies on compact features that are more easily disrupted by ablation. More broadly, relation signals are diffuse but exhibit stable profiles: they peak in the mid-layers and are stronger in post-residual/MLP pathways than in attention. Difficulty is consistent across models (antonymy easiest, synonymy hardest). Probe-level causality is capacity-dependent: on Llama 3.1, SAE-guided patching reliably shifts these signals, whereas on smaller models the shifts are weak or unstable. Our results clarify where and how reliably semantic relations are represented inside LLMs, and provide a reproducible framework for relating sparse features to probe-level causal evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型是否编码语义关系？探查与稀疏特征分析
理解大型语言模型（LLM）是否捕捉结构化意义，需考察其如何表示概念关系。本研究分析三个规模递增的模型：Pythia-70M、GPT-2 和 Llama 3.1 8B，聚焦四种语义关系：同义、反义、上位词与下位词。我们结合线性探查与机制可解释性技术（包括稀疏自编码器SAE和激活修补），识别这些关系的编码位置及特定特征对其表示的贡献。结果揭示层级关系存在方向性不对称：上位词编码冗余且抗干扰，下位词依赖紧凑特征，易被消融破坏。更广泛而言，关系信号分布弥散但具稳定模式：峰值位于中层，且在后残差/MLP路径强于注意力路径。各模型难度一致（反义最易，同义最难）。探查级因果性依赖容量：在Llama 3.1上，SAE引导的修补可稳定转移这些信号，而在小模型中则弱或不稳定。结果阐明LLM内部语义关系的可靠表示位置与方式，并提供稀疏特征与探查级因果证据关联的可复现框架。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models encode semantic relations by probing Pythia-70M, GPT-2, and Llama 3.1 8B for synonymy, antonymy, hypernymy, and hyponymy using linear probing and mechanistic methods like sparse autoencoders and activation patching. Results show hypernymy is redundantly encoded and resistant to disruption, while hyponymy depends on fragile, compact features. Semantic relations are most prominent in mid-layers and post-residual/MLP pathways, with antonymy being easiest and synonymy hardest to capture. Probe-level causality varies by model capacity: SAE-guided patching effectively shifts relation signals in Llama 3.1 but yields weak or unstable effects in smaller models.
该研究通过线性探测与稀疏自编码器、激活修补等机制性方法，探究Pythia-70M、GPT-2和Llama 3.1 8B模型对同义、反义、上下位关系的编码情况。结果显示，上下位关系中上位词编码冗余且抗干扰，下位词依赖脆弱的紧凑特征易被破坏；关系信号集中在中层且在残差/MLP路径中更强而非注意力层；模型间难度一致，反义最易识别，同义最难；因果干预（SAE引导修补）仅在大模型如Llama 3.1中稳定有效，表明其依赖模型容量。
Passive iFIR filters for data-driven velocity control in robotics
Authors: Yi Zhang, Zixing Wang, Fulvio Forni
First: 2026-03-31T15:34:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:34:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a passive, data-driven velocity control method for nonlinear robotic manipulators that achieves better tracking performance than optimized PID with comparable design complexity. Using only three minutes of probing data, a VRFT-based design identifies passive iFIR controllers that (i) preserve closed-loop stability via passivity constraints and (ii) outperform a VRFT-tuned PID baseline on the Franka Research 3 robot in both joint-space and Cartesian-space velocity control, achieving up to a 74.5% reduction in tracking error for the Cartesian velocity tracking experiment with the most demanding reference model. When the robot end-effector dynamics change, the controller can be re-learned from new data, regaining nominal performance. This study bridges learning-based control and stability-guaranteed design: passive iFIR learns from data while retaining passivity-based stability guarantees, unlike many learning-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人数据驱动速度控制的被动iFIR滤波器
我们提出了一种针对非线性机器人操作臂的被动、数据驱动的速度控制方法，其跟踪性能优于设计复杂度相当的优化PID控制器。仅使用三分钟探针数据，基于VRFT的设计方法即可识别出被动iFIR控制器：(i) 通过被动性约束保持闭环稳定性；(ii) 在Franka Research 3机器人上，无论是关节空间还是笛卡尔空间速度控制，均优于VRFT调优的PID基线控制器，在最苛刻的参考模型下，笛卡尔速度跟踪实验的跟踪误差最多减少74.5%。当机器人末端执行器动力学发生变化时，控制器可通过新数据重新学习，恢复标称性能。本研究弥合了基于学习的控制与稳定性保证设计之间的鸿沟：被动iFIR从数据中学习的同时保留基于被动性的稳定性保证，这与许多基于学习的方法不同。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable, data-driven velocity control in nonlinear robotic systems, this work introduces passive iFIR filters that combine data-driven learning with passivity-based stability guarantees. Using only three minutes of probing data, a VRFT-based method designs iFIR controllers that maintain closed-loop stability and outperform a VRFT-tuned PID baseline on the Franka Research 3 robot. Experimental results show up to a 74.5% reduction in Cartesian velocity tracking error under demanding reference models, and the controller can be re-learned when end-effector dynamics change, restoring nominal performance.
该研究旨在解决非线性机器人系统中高精度速度控制问题，提出一种基于数据且保持稳定性的被动iFIR控制器设计方法。通过仅使用三分钟探针数据，利用VRFT技术设计出满足被动性约束的控制器，确保闭环稳定性，同时在Franka Research 3机器人上优于VRFT调优的PID控制器。实验表明，在高难度参考模型下，其在笛卡尔速度跟踪任务中误差降低高达74.5%，且当末端执行器动力学变化时，可通过新数据重新学习恢复原有性能。
Interactive Force-Impedance Control
Authors: Fan Shao, Satoshi Endo, Sandra Hirche, Fanny Ficuciello
First: 2025-10-20T09:35:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:27:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human collaboration with robots requires flexible role adaptation, enabling the robot to switch between an active leader and a passive follower. Effective role switching depends on accurately estimating human intentions, which is typically achieved through external force analysis, nominal robot dynamics, or data-driven approaches. However, these methods are primarily effective in contact-sparse environments. When robots under hybrid or unified force-impedance control physically interact with active humans or non-passive environments, the robotic system may lose passivity and thus compromise safety. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a unified Interactive Force-Impedance Control (IFIC) framework that adapts to interaction power flow, ensuring safe and effortless interaction in contact-rich environments. The proposed control architecture is formulated within a port-Hamiltonian framework, incorporating both interaction and task control ports, thereby guaranteeing autonomous system passivity. Experiments in both rigid and soft contact scenarios demonstrate that IFIC ensures stable collaboration under active human interaction, reduces contact impact forces and interaction force oscillations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：交互力阻抗控制
人机协作需要机器人灵活调整角色，实现从主动领导者到被动跟随者的切换。有效的角色切换依赖于准确估计人类意图，通常通过外力分析、名义机器人动力学或数据驱动方法实现。然而，这些方法主要适用于接触稀疏环境。当机器人在混合或统一力阻抗控制下与主动人类或非被动环境物理交互时，机器人系统可能失去被动性，从而影响安全性。为解决此问题，本文提出一种统一的交互力阻抗控制（IFIC）框架，该框架适应交互功率流，确保在接触密集环境中安全且无负担的交互。所提出的控制架构基于端口哈密顿框架，同时包含交互与任务控制端口，从而保证系统自主被动性。在刚性和柔性接触场景的实验表明，IFIC在主动人类交互下确保稳定协作，降低接触冲击力和交互力振荡。
Summary / 总结
To enable safe and adaptive human-robot collaboration, this paper addresses the challenge of role switching between leader and follower modes under physical interaction, particularly in contact-rich environments where traditional force-based methods fail to ensure passivity. The proposed Interactive Force-Impedance Control (IFIC) framework, built within a port-Hamiltonian formalism, dynamically adapts to interaction power flow by integrating both interaction and task control ports, thereby preserving system passivity. Experimental results in both rigid and soft contact scenarios show that IFIC enables stable human-robot collaboration, significantly reducing contact impact forces and interaction force oscillations compared to baseline methods.
为实现人在密集接触环境下与机器人安全、自适应协作，本文提出交互力阻抗控制（IFIC）统一框架，该框架依据交互功率流动态调整机器人行为。基于端口哈密顿结构构建，IFIC融合交互与任务控制端口，确保系统在混合或统一力阻抗控制下仍保持被动性。实验表明，在刚性和柔性接触场景中，IFIC能稳定协作于主动人类操作下，显著降低接触冲击力与交互力振荡，优于传统方法。
Exploring the Relationship between Brain Hemisphere States and Frequency Bands through Classical Machine Learning and Deep Learning Optimization Techniques with Neurofeedback
Authors: Robiul Islam, Dmitry I. Ignatov, Karl Kaberg, Roman Nabatchikov
First: 2025-09-17T15:26:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:25:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates the performance of classifiers across EEG frequency bands, evaluating efficient class prediction for the left and right hemispheres using various optimisers. Three neural network architectures a deep dense network, a shallow three-layer network, and a convolutional neural network (CNN) are implemented and compared using the TensorFlow and PyTorch frameworks. Adagrad and RMSprop optimisers consistently outperformed others across frequency bands, with Adagrad excelling in the beta band and RMSprop achieving superior performance in the gamma band. Classical machine learning methods (Linear SVM and Random Forest) achieved perfect classification with 50--100 times faster training times than deep learning models. However, in neurofeedback simulations with real-time performance requirements, the deep neural network demonstrated superior feedback-signal generation (a 44.7% regulation rate versus 0% for classical methods). SHAP analysis reveals the nuanced contributions of EEG frequency bands to model decisions. Overall, the study highlights the importance of selecting a model dependent on the task: classical methods for efficient offline classification and deep learning for adaptive, real-time neurofeedback applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过经典机器学习与深度学习优化技术结合神经反馈，探索脑半球状态与频段的关系
本研究评估了不同EEG频段下分类器的性能，使用多种优化器对左右半球进行高效分类预测。在TensorFlow和PyTorch框架下，实现了三种神经网络架构——深度密集网络、浅层三层网络和卷积神经网络（CNN）并进行比较。Adagrad和RMSprop优化器在各频段表现优于其他方法，其中Adagrad在β频段表现最佳，RMSprop在γ频段表现最优。经典机器学习方法（线性SVM和随机森林）在分类上达到完美精度，训练速度比深度学习模型快50至100倍。但在要求实时性能的神经反馈模拟中，深度神经网络表现出更优的反馈信号生成能力（调节率44.7%，而经典方法为0%）。SHAP分析揭示了EEG频段对模型决策的细微贡献。总体而言，本研究强调应根据任务选择模型：经典方法适用于高效离线分类，深度学习适用于自适应实时神经反馈应用。
Summary / 总结
This study aims to evaluate how well machine learning models classify brain hemisphere states across EEG frequency bands, comparing classical and deep learning approaches. Adagrad and RMSprop optimizers outperformed others, with Adagrad excelling in beta and RMSprop in gamma bands. Classical methods achieved perfect classification but were 50–100 times faster to train, while deep learning models, particularly the deep neural network, generated superior real-time neurofeedback signals (44.7% regulation rate vs. 0% for classical methods). 为高效分类脑半球状态，本研究比较了经典机器学习与深度学习模型在EEG频段上的表现。实验评估了三种神经网络架构（深度密集网络、浅层三层网络、CNN）及经典方法（线性SVM与随机森林），并使用Adagrad和RMSprop优化器。结果显示，Adagrad和RMSprop表现最优，Adagrad在β频段表现最佳，RMSprop在γ频段表现最佳。经典方法实现完美分类且训练速度比深度学习快50至100倍，但在实时神经反馈任务中，深度神经网络以44.7%的调节率显著优于经典方法（0%）。SHAP分析进一步揭示了各频段对模型决策的差异化贡献。
ShapE-GRPO: Shapley-Enhanced Reward Allocation for Multi-Candidate LLM Training
Authors: Rui Ai, Yu Pan, David Simchi-Levi, Chonghuan Wang
First: 2026-03-31T15:24:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:24:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In user-agent interaction scenarios such as recommendation, brainstorming, and code suggestion, Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate sets of candidate recommendations where the objective is to maximize the collective utility of the entire set rather than individual candidates independently. However, existing reinforcement learning post-training paradigms, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), typically assign the same set-level scalar reward to every candidate in the set. This leads to noisy training signals where poor candidates free-ride on the high reward produced by a single strong peer, resulting in suboptimal exploration. To address this, we propose Shapley-Enhanced GRPO (ShapE-GRPO). By leveraging the permutation-invariant nature of set-level utility, we derive a Shapley-enhanced formulation from cooperative game theory to decompose set-level rewards into granular, candidate-specific signals. We show that our formulation preserves the fundamental axioms of the Shapley value while remaining computationally efficient with polynomial-time complexity. Empirically, ShapE-GRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO across diverse datasets with accelerated convergence during training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ShapE-GRPO：基于Shapley值增强的奖励分配方法用于多候选大语言模型训练
在推荐、头脑风暴和代码建议等用户代理交互场景中，大语言模型（LLMs）通常生成一组候选推荐，目标是最大化整个集合的集体效用，而非独立优化每个候选。然而，现有强化学习后训练范式（如组相对策略优化GRPO）通常将相同的集合级标量奖励分配给集合中的每个候选。这导致训练信号噪声大，表现不佳的候选会搭便车，利用单个优秀候选产生的高奖励，从而导致探索不足。为解决此问题，我们提出Shapley增强GRPO（ShapE-GRPO）。通过利用集合级效用的排列不变性，我们从合作博弈论中推导出一种Shapley增强公式，将集合级奖励分解为细粒度、候选特定的信号。我们证明该公式在保持Shapley值基本公理的同时，计算效率高，具有多项式时间复杂度。实验证明，ShapE-GRPO在多个数据集上持续优于标准GRPO，并在训练过程中加速收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize collective utility in multi-candidate LLM generation tasks like recommendation and code suggestion, this work introduces ShapE-GRPO, a method that enhances reward allocation by applying Shapley values from cooperative game theory to decompose set-level rewards into individual candidate signals. Unlike standard GRPO, which assigns uniform rewards to all candidates, ShapE-GRPO leverages permutation-invariance to compute candidate-specific contributions while maintaining computational efficiency through polynomial-time complexity. Experimental results demonstrate that ShapE-GRPO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple datasets, achieving faster convergence and higher performance due to reduced reward noise and more accurate gradient signals.
为提升推荐、头脑风暴和代码建议等多候选输出场景中LLM的整体效用，本文提出ShapE-GRPO方法，利用合作博弈论中的Shapley值将集合级奖励分解为每个候选个体的精确信号，避免了传统GRPO中所有候选获得相同奖励导致的搭便车问题。该方法在保持Shapley值基本公理的同时，计算复杂度为多项式级，实验表明其在多个数据集上表现优于标准GRPO，并训练收敛速度更快。
LeLaR: The First In-Orbit Demonstration of an AI-Based Satellite Attitude Controller
Authors: Kirill Djebko, Tom Baumann, Erik Dilger, Frank Puppe, Sergio Montenegro
Venue: IEEE Access, vol. 14, 2026
First: 2025-12-22T17:00:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:21:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in IEEE Access (DOI: 10.1109/ACCESS.2026.3678816). This is the author's version which has not been fully edited and content may change prior to final publication. 20 pages, 15 figures, 18 tables. The maneuver telemetry datasets are available in the GitHub repository under https://github.com/kdjebko/lelar-in-orbit-data
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Attitude control is essential for many satellite missions. Classical controllers, however, are time-consuming to design and sensitive to model uncertainties and variations in operational boundary conditions. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a promising alternative by learning adaptive control strategies through autonomous interaction with a simulation environment. Overcoming the Sim2Real gap, which involves deploying an agent trained in simulation onto the real physical satellite, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we present the first successful in-orbit demonstration of an AI-based attitude controller for inertial pointing maneuvers. The controller was trained entirely in simulation and deployed to the InnoCube 3U nanosatellite, which was developed by the Julius-Maximilians-Universität Würzburg in cooperation with the Technische Universität Berlin, and launched in January 2025. We present the AI agent design, the methodology of the training procedure, the discrepancies between the simulation and the observed behavior of the real satellite, and a comparison of the AI-based attitude controller with the classical PD controller of InnoCube. Steady-state metrics confirm the robust performance of the AI-based controller during repeated in-orbit maneuvers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LeLaR：首个基于AI的卫星姿态控制器在轨演示

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical attitude controllers in satellite missions—such as sensitivity to model uncertainties and lengthy design times—this work introduces LeLaR, the first in-orbit demonstration of an AI-based attitude controller using Deep Reinforcement Learning. The controller was trained entirely in simulation and deployed on the InnoCube 3U nanosatellite, launched in January 2025. Experimental results show that the AI controller outperforms the traditional PD controller in steady-state metrics during repeated inertial pointing maneuvers, demonstrating robustness and adaptability despite simulation-to-reality discrepancies.
为克服传统卫星姿态控制器设计周期长、对模型不确定性敏感的问题，本研究首次在轨演示了基于深度强化学习的AI姿态控制器LeLaR。该控制器完全在仿真环境中训练后部署于2025年1月发射的InnoCube 3U纳卫星。实验结果表明，AI控制器在多次惯性指向机动中表现稳健，稳态指标优于卫星原有的经典PD控制器，尽管存在仿真与真实行为差异。
Beyond AI advice -- independent aggregation boosts human-AI accuracy
Authors: Julian Berger, Pantelis P. Analytis, Ville Satopää, Ralf H. J. M. Kurvers
First: 2026-03-31T15:20:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:20:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) is broadly deployed as an advisor to human decision-makers: AI recommends a decision and a human accepts or rejects the advice. This approach, however, has several limitations: People frequently ignore accurate advice and rely too much on inaccurate advice, and their decision-making skills may deteriorate over time. Here, we compare the AI-as-advisor approach to the hybrid confirmation tree (HCT), an alternative strategy that preserves the independence of human and AI judgments. The HCT elicits a human judgment and an AI judgment independently of each other. If they agree, that decision is accepted. If not, a second human breaks the tie. For the comparison, we used 10 datasets from various domains, including medical diagnostics and misinformation discernment, and a subset of four datasets in which AI also explained its decision. The HCT outperformed the AI-as-advisor approach in all datasets. The HCT also performed better in almost all cases in which AI offered an explanation of its judgment. Using signal detection theory to interpret these results, we find that the HCT outperforms the AI-as-advisor approach because people cannot discriminate well enough between correct and incorrect AI advice. Overall, the HCT is a robust, accurate, and transparent alternative to the AI-as-advisor approach, offering a simple mechanism to tap into the wisdom of hybrid crowds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越AI建议——独立聚合提升人机协同准确性
人工智能（AI）广泛被用作人类决策者的顾问：AI推荐一个决策，人类接受或拒绝该建议。然而，这种方法存在若干局限：人们常忽略准确的建议，过度依赖错误的建议，且决策能力可能随时间下降。本文将AI作为顾问的方法与混合确认树（HCT）——一种保留人类与AI判断独立性的替代策略——进行比较。HCT独立获取人类与AI的判断，若二者一致则采纳该决策；若不一致，则由第二位人类裁决。比较中使用了来自不同领域的10个数据集，包括医学诊断与虚假信息识别，其中4个数据集还包含AI对其决策的解释。HCT在所有数据集中均优于AI作为顾问的方法，且在AI提供决策解释的几乎所有情况下表现更优。通过信号检测理论解读结果，我们发现HCT优于AI作为顾问的方法，是因为人们难以有效区分正确与错误的AI建议。总体而言，HCT是AI作为顾问方法的稳健、准确且透明的替代方案，提供了一种简单机制以利用混合群体的智慧。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the limitations of relying on AI as an advisor, where humans often misinterpret or over-trust AI recommendations. It introduces the hybrid confirmation tree (HCT), a method that independently collects human and AI judgments, then uses a second human to resolve disagreements. Tested across 10 diverse datasets—including medical diagnosis and misinformation detection—the HCT consistently outperformed the AI-as-advisor model. Even when AI provided explanations, HCT maintained superior accuracy, attributed to humans’ poor ability to distinguish correct from incorrect AI advice, as confirmed by signal detection theory. The HCT thus offers a more reliable, transparent, and crowd-sourced decision-making framework.
该研究指出，人类在依赖AI作为顾问时，常误判或过度信任AI建议，导致决策能力下降。作者提出混合确认树（HCT）方法，独立收集人类与AI判断，再由第二位人类裁决分歧。在包括医疗诊断和虚假信息识别在内的10个多样化数据集上，HCT始终优于AI顾问模式。即使AI提供解释时，HCT仍保持更高准确率。信号检测理论分析表明，人类难以可靠区分AI建议的正误，因此独立聚合更有效。HCT因此成为传统AI顾问模式的透明、稳健且准确的替代方案。
Towards Empowering Consumers through Sentence-level Readability Scoring in German ESG Reports
Authors: Benjamin Josef Schüßler, Jakob Prange
First: 2026-03-31T15:19:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:19:02+00:00
Comments: accepted to NLP4Ecology workshop at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the ever-growing urgency of sustainability in the economy and society, and the massive stream of information that comes with it, consumers need reliable access to that information. To address this need, companies began publishing so called Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) reports, both voluntarily and forced by law. To serve the public, these reports must be addressed not only to financial experts but also to non-expert audiences. But are they written clearly enough? In this work, we extend an existing sentence-level dataset of German ESG reports with crowdsourced readability annotations. We find that, in general, native speakers perceive sentences in ESG reports as easy to read, but also that readability is subjective. We apply various readability scoring methods and evaluate them regarding their prediction error and correlation with human rankings. Our analysis shows that, while LLM prompting has potential for distinguishing clear from hard-to-read sentences, a small finetuned transformer predicts human readability with the lowest error. Averaging predictions of multiple models can slightly improve the performance at the cost of slower inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过句级可读性评分赋能消费者：德国ESG报告研究
随着可持续发展在经济和社会中的紧迫性日益增长，以及随之而来的海量信息流，消费者亟需可靠的信息获取渠道。为满足这一需求，企业开始自愿或受法律要求发布所谓环境、社会与治理（ESG）报告。为服务公众，这些报告不仅需面向金融专家，也需面向非专业读者。但它们是否写得足够清晰？本研究扩展了现有德语ESG报告的句级数据集，加入众包可读性标注。我们发现，母语者普遍认为ESG报告中的句子易于阅读，但可读性具有主观性。我们应用多种可读性评分方法，评估其预测误差与人类排名的相关性。分析表明，尽管大语言模型提示法在区分易读与难读句子方面有潜力，但微调的小型Transformer模型在预测人类可读性时误差最低。多模型预测平均可略微提升性能，但推理速度更慢。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accessible ESG information amid rising sustainability concerns, this study enhances a German ESG report dataset with crowdsourced readability annotations to evaluate how well sentences are understood by native speakers. It tests multiple readability scoring methods—including LLM prompting and a small fine-tuned transformer—and finds that while LLMs can distinguish readable from unreadable sentences, the fine-tuned transformer achieves the lowest prediction error when compared to human rankings. Averaging multiple models marginally improves performance but increases inference time.
为提升德语ESG报告的可读性，本研究扩展了句级数据集并引入众包可读性标注，评估多种评分方法。结果表明，母语者普遍认为ESG句子易读，但可读性具有主观性。在模型对比中，小型微调Transformer模型在预测人类可读性评分时误差最小，LLM提示法具备区分易读与难读句子的潜力。多模型平均虽略微提升性能，但增加了推理延迟。
Bridging the Basilisk Astrodynamics Framework with ROS 2 for Modular Spacecraft Simulation and Hardware Integration
Authors: Elias Krantz, Ngai Nam Chan, Gunnar Tibert, Huina Mao, Christer Fuglesang
Venue: 2025 International Conference on Space Robotics (iSpaRo), Sendai, Japan, 2025
First: 2025-12-10T17:13:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-31T15:11:29+00:00
Comments: Presented at the International Conference on Space Robotics (iSpaRo) 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integrating high-fidelity spacecraft simulators with modular robotics frameworks remains a challenge for autonomy development. This paper presents a lightweight, open-source communication bridge between the Basilisk astrodynamics simulator and the Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2), enabling real-time, bidirectional data exchange for spacecraft control. The bridge requires no changes to Basilisk's core and integrates seamlessly with ROS 2 nodes. We demonstrate its use in a leader-follower formation flying scenario using nonlinear model predictive control, deployed identically in both simulation and on the ATMOS planar microgravity testbed. This setup supports rapid development, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and seamless transition from simulation to hardware. The bridge offers a flexible and scalable platform for modular spacecraft autonomy and reproducible research workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将Basilisk轨道动力学框架与ROS 2集成用于模块化航天器仿真与硬件集成
将高保真航天器仿真器与模块化机器人框架集成，仍是自主性开发的挑战。本文提出了一种轻量级、开源的通信桥梁，连接Basilisk轨道动力学仿真器与机器人操作系统2（ROS 2），支持航天器控制的实时双向数据交换。该桥梁无需修改Basilisk核心代码，可无缝集成至ROS 2节点。我们通过非线性模型预测控制，在仿真与ATMOS平面微重力试验台中部署相同的领导者-跟随编队飞行场景，支持快速开发、硬件在环测试及仿真到硬件的无缝过渡。该桥梁提供了一个灵活可扩展的平台，用于模块化航天器自主性及可复现研究工作流。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of integrating high-fidelity spacecraft simulators with modular robotics frameworks for autonomy development, this paper introduces a lightweight, open-source communication bridge that enables real-time, bidirectional data exchange between the Basilisk astrodynamics simulator and ROS 2 without modifying Basilisk’s core. The bridge integrates seamlessly with ROS 2 nodes and is demonstrated in a leader-follower formation flying scenario using nonlinear model predictive control, deployed identically in both simulation and on the ATMOS planar microgravity testbed. Results confirm the bridge’s effectiveness in supporting rapid development, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and smooth transition from simulation to hardware, offering a flexible, scalable platform for modular spacecraft autonomy and reproducible research.
为解决高保真航天器仿真器与模块化机器人框架集成以支持自主性开发的难题，本文提出一种轻量级开源通信桥接器，实现Basilisk动力学仿真器与ROS 2之间的实时双向数据交换，且无需修改Basilisk核心代码。该桥接器无缝集成ROS 2节点，并在非线性模型预测控制的领航跟随编队飞行场景中得到验证，仿真与ATMOS平面微重力实验台硬件部署一致。实验结果表明，该桥接器有效支持快速开发、硬件在环测试及仿真到硬件的平滑过渡，为模块化航天器自主性与可复现研究提供灵活可扩展平台。
